import { initializeApp, getApps, cert } from 'firebase-admin/app';
import { getFirestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';
import { getStorage } from 'firebase-admin/storage';
import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';
import * as path from 'path';

// Load environment variables from multiple possible locations
dotenv.config({ path: '.env.local' });
dotenv.config({ path: '.env' });

// Validate required environment variables
const requiredEnvVars = {
  FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID: process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL: process.env.FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL,
  FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY: process.env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY
};

// Check if all required variables are present
const missingVars = Object.entries(requiredEnvVars)
  .filter(([key, value]) => !value)
  .map(([key]) => key);

if (missingVars.length > 0) {
  console.error('âŒ Missing required environment variables:');
  missingVars.forEach(varName => console.error(`   - ${varName}`));
  console.error('\nPlease check your .env or .env.local file.');
  process.exit(1);
}

// Initialize Firebase Admin with proper error handling
if (!getApps().length) {
  try {
    const serviceAccount = {
      projectId: process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID!,
      clientEmail: process.env.FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL!,
      privateKey: process.env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY!.replace(/\\n/g, '\n'),
    };

    // Validate the service account object
    console.log('ðŸ”‘ Initializing Firebase Admin...');
    console.log(`   Project ID: ${serviceAccount.projectId}`);
    console.log(`   Client Email: ${serviceAccount.clientEmail}`);
    console.log(`   Private Key: ${serviceAccount.privateKey ? 'Present' : 'Missing'}`);

    initializeApp({
      credential: cert(serviceAccount),
      storageBucket: `${process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}.appspot.com`,
    });

    console.log('âœ… Firebase Admin initialized successfully');
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('âŒ Failed to initialize Firebase Admin:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

const db = getFirestore();
const storage = getStorage();

async function resetDatabase() {
  try {
    console.log('\nðŸš€ Starting database reset...');
    console.log('âš ï¸  This will permanently delete ALL data!\n');

    let totalDeleted = 0;

    // 1. Delete all candidates
    console.log('ðŸ“‹ Deleting all candidates...');
    try {
      const candidatesSnapshot = await db.collection('candidates').get();
      if (candidatesSnapshot.size > 0) {
        const candidateBatch = db.batch();
        candidatesSnapshot.docs.forEach(doc => {
          candidateBatch.delete(doc.ref);
        });
        await candidateBatch.commit();
        console.log(`âœ… Deleted ${candidatesSnapshot.size} candidates`);
        totalDeleted += candidatesSnapshot.size;
      } else {
        console.log('â„¹ï¸  No candidates found to delete');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('âŒ Error deleting candidates:', error);
    }

    // 2. Delete all jobs
    console.log('ðŸ’¼ Deleting all jobs...');
    try {
      const jobsSnapshot = await db.collection('jobs').get();
      if (jobsSnapshot.size > 0) {
        const jobsBatch = db.batch();
        jobsSnapshot.docs.forEach(doc => {
          jobsBatch.delete(doc.ref);
        });
        await jobsBatch.commit();
        console.log(`âœ… Deleted ${jobsSnapshot.size} jobs`);
        totalDeleted += jobsSnapshot.size;
      } else {
        console.log('â„¹ï¸  No jobs found to delete');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('âŒ Error deleting jobs:', error);
    }

    // 3. Clear storage bucket (candidate files)
    console.log('ðŸ“ Clearing storage files...');
    try {
      const bucket = storage.bucket();
      const [files] = await bucket.getFiles({ prefix: 'candidate_files/' });
      
      if (files.length > 0) {
        console.log(`   Found ${files.length} files to delete...`);
        const deletePromises = files.map(file => 
          file.delete().catch(err => {
            console.warn(`   âš ï¸  Failed to delete ${file.name}:`, err.message);
            return null;
          })
        );
        const results = await Promise.allSettled(deletePromises);
        const successCount = results.filter(r => r.status === 'fulfilled').length;
        console.log(`âœ… Deleted ${successCount}/${files.length} files from storage`);
      } else {
        console.log('â„¹ï¸  No files found in storage');
      }
    } catch (storageError) {
      console.warn('âš ï¸  Storage cleanup failed (this is often normal if bucket is empty):', storageError);
    }

    console.log('\nðŸŽ‰ Database reset completed successfully!');
    console.log('ðŸ“Š Summary:');
    console.log(`   - Total documents deleted: ${totalDeleted}`);
    console.log(`   - Candidates: 0 (collection cleared)`);
    console.log(`   - Jobs: 0 (collection cleared)`);
    console.log(`   - Storage: cleaned\n`);

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('âŒ Error resetting database:', error);
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      console.error('Error details:', error.message);
      if (error.stack) {
        console.error('Stack trace:', error.stack);
      }
    }
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

// Run the reset
console.log('ðŸ”¥ Firebase Database Reset Tool');
console.log('===============================');

resetDatabase()
  .then(() => {
    console.log('âœ… Script completed successfully');
    process.exit(0);
  })
  .catch((error) => {
    console.error('âŒ Script failed:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  });
"use client";

import React, { useState, FormEvent, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const { user, signIn, loading, error } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    // If user is already logged in, redirect to the dashboard
    if (user) {
      router.push('/admin-dashboard-secret');
    }
  }, [user, router]);

  const handleLogin = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    await signIn(email, password);
    // Redirect is handled by the useEffect hook
  };

  // Don't render the form if the user is already logged in and redirecting
  if (user) {
    return <p>Redirecting...</p>; // Or a loading spinner
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md p-8 space-y-6 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow-md">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-center text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">Admin Login</h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-6">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Email address</label>
            <input
              id="email"
              name="email"
              type="email"
              autoComplete="email"
              required
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              placeholder="you@example.com"
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">Password</label>
            <input
              id="password"
              name="password"
              type="password"
              autoComplete="current-password"
              required
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              className="mt-1 block w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-indigo-500 focus:border-indigo-500 sm:text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
              placeholder="Password"
              disabled={loading}
            />
          </div>

          {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">Error: {error}</p>}

          <div>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full flex justify-center py-2 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-md shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {loading ? 'Signing in...' : 'Sign in'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

        setActiveTab,

        setFilterStatus,
        filterName,

        setFilterCity,
        filterCountry,
        setFilterCountry,
        filterNationality,
        setFilterNationality,
        filterJobTitle,
        setFilterJobTitle,
        filterCategory,
        setFilterCategory,
        filterAgeMin,
        setFilterAgeMin,
        filterAgeMax,
        setFilterAgeMax,
        filterSpecialty,
        setFilterSpecialty,
        calendarFilter,
        setCalendarFilter,
        isScoringModalOpen,
        setIsScoringModalOpen,
        isProfileModalOpen,
        setIsProfileModalOpen,
        isJobManagementOpen,
        setIsJobManagementOpen,
        isInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        selectedCandidate,
        setSelectedCandidate,
        statusUpdateLoading,
        setStatusUpdateLoading,
        isScoring,
        setIsScoring,
        isScheduling,
        setIsScheduling,
        scoringError,
        setScoringError,
        toast,
        setToast,
        confirmationModal,
        setConfirmationModal,
        allInterviewEvents,
        filteredInterviewEvents,
        stats,
        filteredCandidates,
        showToast,
        openScoringModal,
        closeScoringModal,
        openProfileModal,
        closeProfileModal,
        openJobManagement,
        closeJobManagement,
        clearAllFilters,
    } = useDashboard();

    const statusChartData = useMemo(() => getStatusChartData(candidates), [candidates]);
    const scoreDistributionData = useMemo(() => getScoreDistributionData(candidates), [candidates]);

    const {
        handleScoreSubmit,
        handleStatusChange,
        handleScheduleInterview,
        handleCancelInterview,
        handleEditCandidate,
    } = createApiHandlers(
        showToast,
        closeScoringModal,
        closeProfileModal,
        setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        setConfirmationModal,
        setStatusUpdateLoading,
        setIsScoring,
        setScoringError,
        setIsScheduling,
        selectedCandidate,
        candidates
    );

    if (authLoading || (user && loadingCandidates)) {
        return (
            <div className="flex justify-center items-center min-h-screen">
                <p>Loading...</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return null;
    }

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-900 p-4 md:p-8">
            <DashboardHeader
                userEmail={user.email || ''}

            />

            <StatsCards stats={stats} candidates={candidates} />

            <DashboardCharts
                statusChartData={statusChartData}
                scoreDistributionData={scoreDistributionData}
            />

            <CandidateFilters
                candidates={candidates}
                filterStatus={filterStatus}
                filterName={filterName}
                filterCity={filterCity}
                filterCountry={filterCountry}
                filterNationality={filterNationality}
                filterJobTitle={filterJobTitle}
                filterCategory={filterCategory}
                filterSpecialty={filterSpecialty}
                filterAgeMin={filterAgeMin}
                filterAgeMax={filterAgeMax}
                onFilterStatusChange={setFilterStatus}
                onFilterNameChange={setFilterName}
                onFilterCityChange={setFilterCity}
                onFilterCountryChange={setFilterCountry}
                onFilterNationalityChange={setFilterNationality}
                onFilterJobTitleChange={setFilterJobTitle}
                onFilterCategoryChange={setFilterCategory}
                onFilterSpecialtyChange={setFilterSpecialty}
                onFilterAgeMinChange={setFilterAgeMin}
                onFilterAgeMaxChange={setFilterAgeMax}
                onClearAllFilters={clearAllFilters}
            />

            <CandidateManagement
                activeTab={activeTab}
                setActiveTab={setActiveTab}
                filteredCandidates={filteredCandidates}
                statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                openProfileModal={openProfileModal}
                openScoringModal={openScoringModal}
                handleStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                filters={{ 
                    status: filterStatus, 
                    name: filterName, 
                    city: filterCity, 
                    country: filterCountry, 
                    nationality: filterNationality,
                    jobTitle: filterJobTitle, 
                    category: filterCategory, 
                    specialty: filterSpecialty,
                    ageMin: filterAgeMin, 
                    ageMax: filterAgeMax 
                }}
            />

                allInterviewEvents={allInterviewEvents}

                scoringError={scoringError}

                closeProfileModal={closeProfileModal}

                confirmationModal={confirmationModal}
                setConfirmationModal={setConfirmationModal}
                toast={toast}
                setToast={setToast}
            />
        </div>
    );
}

export default function AdminDashboard() {

// src/app/api/cancel-interview/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { cancelInterview } from '@/services/interview';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { candidateId, reason = 'Cancelled by admin' } = await request.json();

    await cancelInterview(candidateId, reason);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: 'Interview cancelled successfully' 
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error cancelling interview:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { 
        error: 'Failed to cancel interview', 
        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error' 
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';

interface CandidateData {
  id: string;
  submittedAt: string | null;
  interviewDate: string | null;
  [key: string]: any;
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { email } = await request.json();

    if (!email) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Email is required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const searchEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    console.log('ðŸ” Checking database for email:', searchEmail);

    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    
    // ðŸ”¥ Optimized: Only query for this specific email (faster and cheaper)
    const candidatesRef = db.collection('candidates');
    const snapshot = await candidatesRef.where('email', '==', searchEmail).get();

    const exists = !snapshot.empty;
    const applications: CandidateData[] = [];

    if (exists) {
      console.log('âœ… Found existing candidate in database');
      snapshot.forEach(doc => {
        const data = doc.data();
        applications.push({
          id: doc.id,
          ...data,
          submittedAt: data.submittedAt?.toDate?.()?.toISOString() || null,
          interviewDate: data.interviewDate?.toDate?.()?.toISOString() || null,
        });
      });
    } else {
      console.log('âŒ No existing candidate found - will need email verification');
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      exists,
      applications,
      count: applications.length
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Check candidate error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Internal server error while checking candidate' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { email, jobId, jobCategory } = await request.json();

    if (!email || !jobId || !jobCategory) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Email, jobId, and jobCategory are required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    const candidatesRef = db.collection('candidates');
    
    // Check for applications to the same specific job in the last 6 months
    const sixMonthsAgo = new Date();
    sixMonthsAgo.setMonth(sixMonthsAgo.getMonth() - 6);
    
    const duplicateQuery = candidatesRef
      .where('email', '==', email.toLowerCase().trim())
      .where('jobTitle', '==', jobId)
      .where('submittedAt', '>=', sixMonthsAgo);
    
    const duplicateSnapshot = await duplicateQuery.get();
    
    if (!duplicateSnapshot.empty) {
      const lastApplication = duplicateSnapshot.docs[0].data();
      const lastSubmissionDate = lastApplication.submittedAt?.toDate();
      
      return NextResponse.json({
        isDuplicate: true,
        message: `You have already applied for this position on ${lastSubmissionDate?.toLocaleDateString()}. You can apply again after 6 months.`,
        lastApplicationDate: lastSubmissionDate?.toISOString()
      });
    }
    
    // Check for category limit (max 3 applications per category per month)
    const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date();
    thirtyDaysAgo.setDate(thirtyDaysAgo.getDate() - 30);
    
    const categoryQuery = candidatesRef
      .where('email', '==', email.toLowerCase().trim())
      .where('jobCategory', '==', jobCategory)
      .where('submittedAt', '>=', thirtyDaysAgo);
    
    const categorySnapshot = await categoryQuery.get();
    
    if (categorySnapshot.size >= 3) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        isDuplicate: true,
        message: `You have reached the maximum number of applications (3) for the "${jobCategory}" category this month.`,
        lastApplicationDate: null
      });
    }
    
    return NextResponse.json({
      isDuplicate: false,
      message: 'No duplicate applications found',
      lastApplicationDate: null
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Check duplicate error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Internal server error while checking duplicates' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { exportCandidates } from '@/services/candidate';
import { generateCSV } from '@/lib/csvUtils';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const exportFormat = searchParams.get('format') || 'json';
    const filterStatus = searchParams.get('status') || 'all';
    const filterName = searchParams.get('name') || '';
    const filterCity = searchParams.get('city') || '';
    const filterCountry = searchParams.get('country') || '';
    const filterNationality = searchParams.get('nationality') || '';
    const filterJobTitle = searchParams.get('jobTitle') || '';
    const filterCategory = searchParams.get('category') || '';
    const filterSpecialty = searchParams.get('specialty') || '';
    const filterAgeMin = searchParams.get('ageMin') || '';
    const filterAgeMax = searchParams.get('ageMax') || '';

    console.log('ðŸ” Export request with filters:', {
      format: exportFormat,
      status: filterStatus,
      name: filterName,
      city: filterCity,
      country: filterCountry,
      nationality: filterNationality,
      jobTitle: filterJobTitle,
      category: filterCategory,
      specialty: filterSpecialty,
      ageMin: filterAgeMin,
      ageMax: filterAgeMax
    });

    const candidates = await exportCandidates(
      filterStatus,
      filterName,
      filterNationality,
      filterCity,
      filterCountry,
      filterJobTitle,
      filterCategory,
      filterSpecialty,
      filterAgeMin,
      filterAgeMax
    );

    console.log(`âœ… Exporting ${candidates.length} candidates in ${exportFormat} format`);

    const timestamp = format(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm-ss');
    const baseFilename = `candidates_${timestamp}`;

    switch (exportFormat) {
      case 'csv':
        const csvContent = generateCSV(candidates);
        return new NextResponse(csvContent, {
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'text/csv; charset=utf-8',
            'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${baseFilename}.csv"`,
            'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
          }
        });

      case 'xlsx':
        return NextResponse.json({ 
          data: candidates,
          format: 'xlsx',
          filename: `${baseFilename}.xlsx`,
          count: candidates.length,
          exportedAt: new Date().toISOString()
        });

      case 'json':
      default:
        const jsonData = {
          candidates,
          metadata: {
            exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            totalCount: candidates.length,
            filters: {
              status: filterStatus,
              name: filterName,
              city: filterCity,
              country: filterCountry,
              nationality: filterNationality,
              jobTitle: filterJobTitle,
              category: filterCategory,
              specialty: filterSpecialty,
              ageRange: filterAgeMin || filterAgeMax ? `${filterAgeMin || '0'}-${filterAgeMax || 'âˆž'}` : null
            },
            format: 'json'
          }
        };

        return new NextResponse(JSON.stringify(jsonData, null, 2), {
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
            'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="${baseFilename}.json"`,
            'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
          }
        });
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('âŒ Export candidates error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { 
        error: 'Failed to export candidates',
        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error',
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
      }, 
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';

interface CandidateApplication {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  firstName?: string;
  lastName?: string;
  email: string;
  gender?: string;
  phoneNumber?: string;
  countryCode?: string;
  dateOfBirth?: string;
  city?: string;
  country?: string;
  nationality?: string;
  doubleNationality?: string;
  jobTitle?: string;
  jobTitleText: string;
  jobCategory: string;
  specialty: string;
  websites?: string[];
  status: string;
  score: number | null;
  scoreAnalysis?: string;
  cvUrl: string;
  passportUrl: string;
  photoUrl: string;
  submittedAt: Date | null;
  interviewDate: Date | null;
  meetLink: string;
  calendarEventId?: string;
  duration?: number;
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { email } = await request.json();

    if (!email) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Email is required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const searchEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
    console.log('ðŸ” Fetching applications for email:', searchEmail);

    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    const candidatesRef = db.collection('candidates');
    
    // Get all applications for this email, ordered by submission date (newest first)
    const snapshot = await candidatesRef
      .where('email', '==', searchEmail)
      .orderBy('submittedAt', 'desc')
      .get();

    const applications: CandidateApplication[] = [];

    if (!snapshot.empty) {
      snapshot.forEach(doc => {
        const data = doc.data();
        
        // Convert Firestore data to JavaScript objects
        const application: CandidateApplication = {
          id: doc.id,
          fullName: data.fullName || '',
          firstName: data.firstName || '',
          lastName: data.lastName || '',
          email: data.email || '',
          gender: data.gender || '',
          phoneNumber: data.phoneNumber || '',
          countryCode: data.countryCode || '',
          dateOfBirth: data.dateOfBirth || '',
          city: data.city || '',
          country: data.country || '',
          nationality: data.nationality || '',
          doubleNationality: data.doubleNationality || '',
          jobTitle: data.jobTitle || '',
          jobTitleText: data.jobTitleText || '',
          jobCategory: data.jobCategory || '',
          specialty: data.specialty || '',
          websites: data.websites || [],
          cvUrl: data.cvUrl || '',
          passportUrl: data.passportUrl || '',
          photoUrl: data.photoUrl || '',
          status: data.status || 'submitted',
          score: data.score || null,
          scoreAnalysis: data.scoreAnalysis || '',
          submittedAt: data.submittedAt ? data.submittedAt.toDate().toISOString() : null,
          interviewDate: data.interviewDate ? data.interviewDate.toDate().toISOString() : null,
          meetLink: data.meetLink || '',
          calendarEventId: data.calendarEventId || '',
          duration: data.duration || null
        };
        
        applications.push(application);
      });
    }

    console.log(`âœ… Found ${applications.length} applications for ${searchEmail}`);

    return NextResponse.json({
      applications,
      count: applications.length
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('âŒ Get candidate applications error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Internal server error while fetching applications' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
// src/app/api/jobs/route.ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getJobs, createJob, updateJob, deleteJob } from '@/services/jobs';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const activeOnly = searchParams.get('activeOnly') === 'true';
    const { jobs, categories } = await getJobs(activeOnly);
    return NextResponse.json({ jobs, categories });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching jobs:", error);
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to fetch jobs';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: errorMessage }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const jobData = await request.json();
    const { jobId } = await createJob(jobData);
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      jobId,
      message: 'Job created successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error creating job:", error);
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to create job';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: errorMessage }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function PUT(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { jobId, ...jobData } = await request.json();
    await updateJob(jobId, jobData);
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: 'Job updated successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating job:", error);
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to update job';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: errorMessage }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const jobId = searchParams.get('jobId');
    if (jobId) {
      await deleteJob(jobId);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      message: 'Job deleted successfully'
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error deleting job:", error);
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to delete job';
    return NextResponse.json({ error: errorMessage }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { 
      candidateId, 
      candidateEmail, 
      candidateName, 
      interviewDate, 
      interviewTime, 
      duration = 30,
      jobTitle, 
      adminEmail,
      notes 
    } = await request.json();

    const data = await scheduleInterview(
      candidateId,
      candidateEmail,
      candidateName,
      interviewDate,
      interviewTime,

      jobTitle,
      adminEmail,
      notes,
      request.nextUrl.origin
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true,

      data
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error scheduling interview:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { 
        error: 'Failed to schedule interview', 
        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error' 
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { scoreCandidate } from '@/services/scoring';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    console.log('ðŸŽ¯ Starting Gemini 2.5 Pro scoring request...');
    
    const { candidateId, jobId, jobTitle, jobDescription } = await request.json();

    if (!candidateId || !jobTitle || !jobDescription) {
      return NextResponse.json({ 
        error: 'Missing required fields: candidateId, jobTitle, or jobDescription' 
      }, { status: 400 });
    }

    console.log(`ðŸ“Š Scoring candidate ${candidateId} for position: ${jobTitle}`);

    const scoringResult = await scoreCandidate(candidateId, jobId, jobTitle, jobDescription);

    console.log(`âœ… Scoring completed with score: ${scoringResult.score}`);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
        success: true, 
        score: scoringResult.score, 
        analysis: scoringResult.analysis,
        keyStrengths: scoringResult.keyStrengths,
        areasForImprovement: scoringResult.areasForImprovement,
        recommendation: scoringResult.recommendation,
        jobTitle: jobTitle,
        scoringVersion: 'gemini-2.5-pro'
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error("âŒ Error in Gemini 2.5 Pro scoring:", error);
    const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
    
    if (errorMessage.includes("AI Scoring is not configured")) {
        return NextResponse.json({ 
          error: 'AI Scoring is not configured on the server.' 
        }, { status: 503 });
    }
    
    return NextResponse.json({ 
      error: 'Failed to score candidate with Gemini 2.5 Pro', 
      details: errorMessage 
    }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { sendApplicationConfirmationEmail } from '@/services/email';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const {
            candidateEmail,
            candidateName,
            jobTitle,
            jobCategory,
            specialty,
            applicationId
        } = await request.json();

        // Validate required fields
        if (!candidateEmail || !candidateName || !jobTitle || !applicationId) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { 
                    success: false, 
                    error: 'Missing required fields: candidateEmail, candidateName, jobTitle, and applicationId are required' 
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // Generate submission date
        const submissionDate = new Date();

        // Send confirmation email
        await sendApplicationConfirmationEmail({
            candidateEmail,
            candidateName,
            jobTitle,
            jobCategory,
            specialty,
            applicationId,
            submissionDate
        });

        return NextResponse.json({ 
            success: true, 
            message: 'Confirmation email sent successfully' 
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('âŒ Error in send-confirmation-email API:', error);
        
        return NextResponse.json(
            { 
                success: false, 
                error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to send confirmation email' 
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';
import admin from 'firebase-admin';
import { calculateAge } from '@/lib/dateUtils';

export async function PUT(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { candidateId, updatedData } = await request.json();

    if (!candidateId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Candidate ID is required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!updatedData || Object.keys(updatedData).length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Updated data is required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    console.log(`ðŸ”„ Updating candidate ${candidateId} with data:`, updatedData);

    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    const candidateRef = db.collection('candidates').doc(candidateId);

    // Check if candidate exists
    const candidateSnap = await candidateRef.get();
    if (!candidateSnap.exists) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Candidate not found' },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    // Prepare update data with timestamp
    const updatePayload: any = {
      ...updatedData,
      lastUpdated: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
    };

    // Calculate age if dateOfBirth is being updated
    if (updatedData.dateOfBirth) {
      const age = calculateAge(updatedData.dateOfBirth);
      if (age !== null) {
        updatePayload.age = age;
      }
    }

    // Update the candidate
    await candidateRef.update(updatePayload);

    console.log(`âœ… Successfully updated candidate ${candidateId}`);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'Candidate updated successfully',
      candidateId,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    });

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('âŒ Update candidate error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { 
        error: 'Failed to update candidate',
        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { updateCandidateStatus } from '@/services/candidate';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const { 
            candidateId, 
            newStatus, 
            interviewTimestampISO,
            calendarEventId,
            meetLink,
            duration
        } = await request.json();

        if (!candidateId || !newStatus) {
            return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing candidateId or newStatus' }, { status: 400 });
        }

        // ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: Get email status from service
        const result = await updateCandidateStatus(
            candidateId, 
            newStatus, 
            interviewTimestampISO,
            calendarEventId,
            meetLink,
            duration
        );

        return NextResponse.json({ 
            success: true, 
            newStatus: result.status,
            emailSent: result.emailSent // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Include email status
        });

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error in /api/update-status:", error);
        const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error';
        return NextResponse.json({ 
            error: 'Failed to update candidate status', 
            details: errorMessage 
        }, { status: 500 });
    }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import {
  basicPhoneValidation,
  validatePhoneWithRegex,
  validatePhoneWithAbstractAPI,
  checkRateLimit,
  updateRateLimit,
} from '@/lib/phoneValidationService';

// ðŸ”¥ MAIN API HANDLER
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { countryCode, phoneNumber } = await request.json();

    if (!countryCode || !phoneNumber) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Country code and phone number are required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const clientIP = request.headers.get('x-forwarded-for') || 
                     request.headers.get('x-real-ip') || 
                     '127.0.0.1';

    const cleanPhone = phoneNumber.replace(/\D/g, '');
    const fullPhone = `${countryCode}${cleanPhone}`;

    console.log(`ðŸ“ž [PRODUCTION] Starting validation for: ${fullPhone}`);

    // ðŸ”¥ STEP 1: Basic format validation
    console.log('ðŸ“ž [STEP 1] Basic format validation...');
    const basicResult = basicPhoneValidation(fullPhone);
    
    if (!basicResult.isValid) {
      console.log('âŒ [STEP 1] Basic validation failed');
      return NextResponse.json({
        ...basicResult,
        message: 'Phone number format is invalid. Please check your input.'
      });
    }
    console.log('âœ… [STEP 1] Basic validation passed');

    // ðŸ”¥ STEP 2: Regex pattern validation
    console.log('ðŸ“ž [STEP 2] Regex pattern validation...');
    const regexResult = validatePhoneWithRegex(fullPhone);
    
    if (!regexResult.isValid) {
      console.log('âŒ [STEP 2] Regex validation failed');
      updateRateLimit(clientIP.split(',')[0], fullPhone, false);
      return NextResponse.json({
        ...regexResult,
        message: `Phone number format is invalid for ${regexResult.country?.name || 'this country'}. Please check your input.`,
        suggestion: 'Please verify your phone number format and country code.'
      });
    }
    console.log('âœ… [STEP 2] Regex validation passed');

    // ðŸ”¥ STEP 3: Abstract API validation
    console.log('ðŸ“ž [STEP 3] Checking API rate limits...');
    const rateLimit = checkRateLimit(clientIP.split(',')[0], fullPhone);
    
    if (!rateLimit.allowed) {
      console.log(`ðŸš« [STEP 3] ${rateLimit.reason}`);
      
      if (rateLimit.isFailedNumber) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          ...regexResult,
          warning: 'This phone number was previously validated. Using cached result.',
          message: 'âš ï¸ Phone number format appears valid (using pattern matching)'
        });
      }
      
      return NextResponse.json({
        ...regexResult,
        warning: 'API limit reached. Using pattern matching validation.',
        message: 'âš ï¸ Phone number format appears valid (using pattern matching)'
      });
    }

    // Try Abstract API
    try {
      if (process.env.ABSTRACT_PHONE_API_KEY) {
        console.log('ðŸ“ž [STEP 3] Calling Abstract API...');
        const apiResult = await validatePhoneWithAbstractAPI(fullPhone);
        updateRateLimit(clientIP.split(',')[0], fullPhone, true);
        
        console.log(`âœ… [STEP 3] API validation completed: ${apiResult.isValid ? 'VALID' : 'INVALID'}`);
        
        if (!apiResult.isValid) {
          updateRateLimit(clientIP.split(',')[0], fullPhone, false);
        }
        
        return NextResponse.json({
          ...apiResult,
          message: apiResult.isValid 
            ? `âœ… Phone number verified for ${apiResult.country?.name || 'your country'}${apiResult.carrier ? ` (${apiResult.carrier})` : ''}`
            : `âŒ Phone number verification failed: ${apiResult.error || 'Invalid number'}`,
          attemptsLeft: rateLimit.attemptsLeft - 1
        });
      } else {
        throw new Error('No API key configured');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('ðŸ“ž [STEP 3] Abstract API failed, using regex result:', error);
      
      return NextResponse.json({
        ...regexResult,
        warning: 'Phone verification service temporarily unavailable. Using pattern matching.',
        message: 'âš ï¸ Phone number format appears valid (verification service unavailable)'
      });
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Phone validation error:', error);
    
    try {
      const { countryCode, phoneNumber } = await request.json();
      const cleanPhone = phoneNumber.replace(/\D/g, '');
      const fullPhone = `${countryCode}${cleanPhone}`;
      const basicResult = basicPhoneValidation(fullPhone);
      
      return NextResponse.json({
        ...basicResult,
        warning: 'Validation service temporarily unavailable. Using basic format check.',
        message: basicResult.isValid 
          ? 'âš ï¸ Phone format appears valid (service unavailable)'
          : 'âŒ Invalid phone number format',
        error: 'Service temporarily unavailable'
      });
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({
        isValid: false,
        error: 'Service temporarily unavailable',
        fallbackUsed: 'failed',
        validationLevel: 'low'
      }, { status: 500 });
    }
  }
}
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { verifyEmail } from '@/services/emailVerification';

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { email } = await request.json();

    if (!email) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: 'Email is required' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const verificationResult = await verifyEmail(email);

    return NextResponse.json(verificationResult);

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Email verification error:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Internal server error during email verification' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import React from 'react';

import ApplicationStatus from '../components/candidate-dashboard/ApplicationStatus';
import InterviewDetails from '../components/candidate-dashboard/InterviewDetails';

import DashboardFooter from '../components/common/Footer';
import WelcomeSection from '../components/candidate-dashboard/WelcomeSection';
import HelpSection from '../components/candidate-dashboard/HelpSection';

export default function CandidateDashboard() {
  const {
    candidateEmail,
    applications,
    isLoading,
    error,
    handleNewApplication,
    formatDate,
  } = useCandidateDashboard();

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-[#003A70] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4"></div>
          <p className="text-slate-600">Loading your applications...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-900 font-sans">
      <DashboardHeader />
      
      <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-start py-12 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-6xl space-y-8">
          <WelcomeSection
            candidateEmail={candidateEmail}
            handleNewApplication={handleNewApplication}
          />
          
          {error && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600">{error}</p>
            </div>
          )}

          <ApplicationStatus applications={applications} formatDate={formatDate} />
          
          <InterviewDetails applications={applications} formatDate={formatDate} />

          <HelpSection />
        </div>
      </main>

      <DashboardFooter />
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';
import { Calendar, dateFnsLocalizer, Event as CalendarEvent } from 'react-big-calendar';
import { format } from 'date-fns/format';
import { parse } from 'date-fns/parse';
import { startOfWeek } from 'date-fns/startOfWeek';
import { getDay } from 'date-fns/getDay';
import { enUS } from 'date-fns/locale/en-US';
import 'react-big-calendar/lib/css/react-big-calendar.css';
import type { InterviewEvent } from '@/types';

const locales = { 'en-US': enUS };
const localizer = dateFnsLocalizer({ format, parse, startOfWeek, getDay, locales });

interface CalendarSectionProps {
    allInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[];
    filteredInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[];
    calendarFilter: 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed';
    setCalendarFilter: (filter: 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed') => void;
    openProfileModal: (candidate: any) => void;
}

const eventStyleGetter = (event: InterviewEvent) => {
    const style: React.CSSProperties = {
        borderRadius: '4px',
        opacity: 0.8,
        color: 'white',
        border: '0px',
        display: 'block',
        fontSize: '12px',
        fontWeight: 'bold',
    };

    if (event.isCompleted) {
        style.backgroundColor = '#10B981'; // Green
        style.borderLeft = '4px solid #047857'; // Darker green border
        style.opacity = 0.9;
    } else {
        style.backgroundColor = '#3B82F6'; // Blue
        style.borderLeft = '4px solid #1D4ED8'; // Darker blue border
    }

    return { style };
};

const CalendarSection: React.FC<CalendarSectionProps> = ({
    allInterviewEvents,
    filteredInterviewEvents,
    calendarFilter,
    setCalendarFilter,
    openProfileModal,
}) => {
    return (
        <section className="mt-8">
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mb-6 gap-4">
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Interview Schedule</h3>
                    
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 mr-2">Filter:</span>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('all')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'all' 
                                    ? 'bg-gray-600 text-white border-gray-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-600'
                            }`}
                        >
                            All ({allInterviewEvents.length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('upcoming')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'upcoming' 
                                    ? 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 border-blue-300 dark:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/50'
                            }`}
                        >
                            Upcoming ({allInterviewEvents.filter(e => !e.isCompleted).length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCalendarFilter('completed')}
                            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                                calendarFilter === 'completed' 
                                    ? 'bg-green-600 text-white border-green-600' 
                                    : 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-green-600 dark:text-green-300 border-green-300 dark:border-green-600 hover:bg-green-50 dark:hover:bg-green-900/50'
                            }`}
                        >
                            Completed ({allInterviewEvents.filter(e => e.isCompleted).length})
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center space-x-6 mb-4 p-3 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700/50 rounded-lg">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-blue-500 rounded border-l-4 border-blue-700"></div>
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Upcoming Interviews</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-green-500 rounded border-l-4 border-green-700"></div>
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">Completed Interviews</span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ height: '500px' }} className="dark-calendar-theme">
                    <Calendar
                        localizer={localizer}
                        events={filteredInterviewEvents}
                        startAccessor="start"
                        endAccessor="end"
                        style={{ height: '100%' }}
                        views={['month', 'week', 'day']}
                        defaultView="month"
                        popup
                        eventPropGetter={eventStyleGetter}
                        onSelectEvent={(event) => openProfileModal(event.resource)}
                        tooltipAccessor={(event: CalendarEvent) => {
                            const interviewEvent = event as InterviewEvent;
                            const candidate = interviewEvent.resource;
                            const status = interviewEvent.isCompleted ? 'Completed' : 'Scheduled';
                            const time = format(interviewEvent.start as Date, 'PPpp');
                            return `${status}: ${candidate.fullName} - ${time}`;
                        }}
                    />
                </div>

                {filteredInterviewEvents.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                        <svg className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mb-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 7V3a1 1 0 011-1h6a1 1 0 011 1v4h2a1 1 0 011 1v10a1 1 0 01-1 1H5a1 1 0 01-1-1V8a1 1 0 011-1h3z" />
                        </svg>
                        <p className="text-lg font-medium">No interviews {calendarFilter === 'all' ? '' : calendarFilter}</p>
                        <p className="text-sm">
                            {calendarFilter === 'all' && 'Schedule some interviews to see them here.'}
                            {calendarFilter === 'upcoming' && 'No upcoming interviews scheduled.'}
                            {calendarFilter === 'completed' && 'No completed interviews yet.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </section>
    );
};

export default CalendarSection;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import Tabs from '../ui/Tabs';
import CandidateTable from '../CandidateTable';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import ExportButtons from '../candidate-table/ExportButtons';
import { useCandidateTable } from '@/hooks/useCandidateTable';


interface CandidateManagementProps {
    activeTab: string;
    setActiveTab: (tab: string) => void;
    filteredCandidates: Candidate[];
    statusUpdateLoading: Record<string, boolean>;
    openProfileModal: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    openScoringModal: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    handleStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string) => void;
    filters: {
        status: string;
        name: string;
        city: string;
        country: string;
        nationality: string;
        jobTitle: string;
        category: string;
        specialty: string;
        ageMin: string;
        ageMax: string;
    };
}

const CandidateManagement: React.FC<CandidateManagementProps> = ({
    activeTab,
    setActiveTab,
    filteredCandidates,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    openProfileModal,
    openScoringModal,
    handleStatusChange,
    filters,
}) => {
    const { isExporting, handleExport } = useCandidateTable(filteredCandidates, filters);

    return (
        <div className="mt-8 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Candidate List</h2>
                <ExportButtons isExporting={isExporting} handleExport={handleExport} />
            </div>
            <Tabs
                activeTab={activeTab}
                onTabChange={setActiveTab}
                tabs={[
                    { label: 'Main', value: 'main' },
                    { label: 'Approved', value: 'approved' },
                    { label: 'Rejected', value: 'rejected' },
                ]}
            >
                <CandidateTable
                    candidates={filteredCandidates}
                    statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    onViewCandidate={openProfileModal}
                    onScoreCandidate={openScoringModal}
                    onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                    filters={filters}
                />
            </Tabs>
        </div>
    );
};

export default CandidateManagement;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import JobManagement from '../JobManagement';
import ScoringModal from '../ScoringModal';
import CandidateProfileModal from '../CandidateProfileModal';
import InterviewSchedulingModal from '../InterviewSchedulingModal';
import ConfirmationModal from '../ui/ConfirmationModal';
import Toast from '../ui/Toast';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface DashboardModalsProps {
    isJobManagementOpen: boolean;
    closeJobManagement: () => void;
    isScoringModalOpen: boolean;
    closeScoringModal: () => void;
    selectedCandidate: Candidate | null;
    handleScoreSubmit: (jobId: string, jobTitle: string, jobDescription: string) => Promise<void>;
    isScoring: boolean;
    scoringError: string | null;
    isProfileModalOpen: boolean;
    closeProfileModal: () => void;
    handleStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string, interviewTimestampISO?: string) => Promise<void>;
    handleCancelInterview: (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => Promise<void>;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
    setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
    handleEditCandidate: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>;
    isInterviewSchedulingOpen: boolean;
    handleScheduleInterview: (
        candidateId: string, 
        date: string, 
        time: string, 
        duration: number,
        adminEmail: string,
        notes?: string
    ) => Promise<void>;
    isScheduling: boolean;
    confirmationModal: {
        isOpen: boolean;
        title: string;
        message: string;
        onConfirm: () => void;
    };
    setConfirmationModal: (modal: any) => void;
    toast: {
        message: string;
        type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';
        isVisible: boolean;
    };
    setToast: (toast: any) => void;
}

const DashboardModals: React.FC<DashboardModalsProps> = ({
    isJobManagementOpen,
    closeJobManagement,
    isScoringModalOpen,
    closeScoringModal,
    selectedCandidate,
    handleScoreSubmit,
    isScoring,
    scoringError,
    isProfileModalOpen,
    closeProfileModal,
    handleStatusChange,
    handleCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
    handleEditCandidate,
    isInterviewSchedulingOpen,
    handleScheduleInterview,
    isScheduling,
    confirmationModal,
    setConfirmationModal,
    toast,
    setToast,
}) => {
    return (
        <>
            <JobManagement
                isOpen={isJobManagementOpen}
                onClose={closeJobManagement}
            />

            <ScoringModal
                isOpen={isScoringModalOpen}
                onClose={closeScoringModal}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onSubmit={handleScoreSubmit}
                isLoading={isScoring}
                error={scoringError}
            />

            <CandidateProfileModal
                isOpen={isProfileModalOpen}
                onClose={closeProfileModal}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
                onCancelInterview={handleCancelInterview}
                statusUpdateLoading={selectedCandidate ? statusUpdateLoading : false}
                onScheduleInterview={() => setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen(true)}
                onEditCandidate={handleEditCandidate}
            />

            <InterviewSchedulingModal
                isOpen={isInterviewSchedulingOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen(false)}
                candidate={selectedCandidate}
                onSchedule={handleScheduleInterview}
                isLoading={isScheduling}
            />

            <ConfirmationModal
                isOpen={confirmationModal.isOpen}
                title={confirmationModal.title}
                message={confirmationModal.message}
                onConfirm={confirmationModal.onConfirm}
                onCancel={() => setConfirmationModal((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isOpen: false }))}
            />

            <Toast
                message={toast.message}
                type={toast.type}
                isVisible={toast.isVisible}
                onClose={() => setToast((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isVisible: false }))}
            />
        </>
    );
};

export default DashboardModals;

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';
import StatusBadge from '../ui/StatusBadge';

interface ApplicationCardProps {
  candidate: Candidate;
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const ApplicationCard: React.FC<ApplicationCardProps> = ({
  candidate: app,
  formatDate,
}) => {
  return (
    <div key={app.id} className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-xl p-6 hover:shadow-md dark:hover:border-gray-600 transition-shadow">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-6">
        <div className="flex-grow space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">
              {app.jobTitleText || 'Job Application'}
            </h3>
            <StatusBadge status={app.status} />
          </div>
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            <p><strong>Category:</strong> {app.jobCategory || 'N/A'}</p>
            {app.specialty && <p><strong>Specialty:</strong> {app.specialty}</p>}
            <p><strong>Submitted:</strong> {formatDate(app.submittedAt)}</p>
            {app.score !== null && app.score !== undefined && (
              <p><strong>Score:</strong> <span className="font-medium">{app.score}/100</span></p>
            )}
            {app.interviewDate && (
              <p><strong>Interview:</strong> {formatDate(app.interviewDate)}</p>
            )}
            {app.meetLink && (
              <p className="md:col-span-2">
                <strong>Meeting Link:</strong>{' '}
                <a
                  href={app.meetLink}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-800 dark:hover:text-blue-300 underline"
                >
                  Join Interview
                </a>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2 flex-shrink-0">
          {app.cvUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.cvUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-300 bg-[#003A70]/10 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#003A70]/20 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 10v6m0 0l-3-3m3 3l3-3m2 8H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" /></svg>
              <span>Download CV</span>
            </a>
          )}
          
          {app.passportUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.passportUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-700 dark:text-green-300 bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-green-200 dark:hover:bg-green-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M10 6H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v9a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V8a2 2 0 00-2-2h-5m-4 0V4a2 2 0 114 0v2m-4 0a2 2 0 104 0m-5 8a2 2 0 100-4 2 2 0 000 4zm0 0c1.306 0 2.417.835 2.83 2M9 14a3.001 3.001 0 00-2.83 2M15 11h3m-3 4h2" /></svg>
              <span>View Passport</span>
            </a>
          )}

          {app.photoUrl && (
            <a
              href={app.photoUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900/50 rounded-lg hover:bg-purple-200 dark:hover:bg-purple-900/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z" /></svg>
              <span>View Photo</span>
            </a>
          )}

import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';
import ApplicationCard from './ApplicationCard';

interface ApplicationStatusProps {
  applications: Candidate[];
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const ApplicationStatus: React.FC<ApplicationStatusProps> = ({ applications, formatDate }) => (
  <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700">
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">Your Applications</h2>
        <span className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          {applications.length} {applications.length === 1 ? 'application' : 'applications'}
        </span>
      </div>

      {applications.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 space-y-4">
          <svg className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z" />
          </svg>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">No Applications Yet</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            You haven't submitted any applications yet. Click the button above to get started!
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {applications.map((app) => (
            <ApplicationCard key={app.id} candidate={app} formatDate={formatDate} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  </div>
);

// src/app/components/candidate-dashboard/InterviewDetails.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface InterviewDetailsProps {
  applications: Candidate[];
  formatDate: (timestamp: any) => string;
}

const InterviewDetails: React.FC<InterviewDetailsProps> = ({ applications, formatDate }) => {
  const scheduledInterviews = applications.filter(app => app.status === 'interview_scheduled');

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-slate-200 dark:shadow-none border border-slate-100 dark:border-gray-700">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6">Upcoming Interviews</h2>
      {scheduledInterviews.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 space-y-4">
          <svg className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 7V3a1 1 0 011-1h6a1 1 0 011 1v4m-3 8v4m0 0l-3-3m3 3l3-3m-3-4a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" />
          </svg>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">No Upcoming Interviews</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            You have no interviews scheduled at this time.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {scheduledInterviews.map(app => (
            <div key={app.id} className="border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg p-6">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">{app.jobTitleText}</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400"><strong>Date:</strong> {formatDate(app.interviewDate)}</p>
              <a href={app.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline">Join Interview</a>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default InterviewDetails;

export interface CandidateFormUIProps {
    firstName: string;
    setFirstName: (name: string) => void;
    lastName: string;
    setLastName: (name: string) => void;
    email: string;
    gender: string;
    setGender: (gender: string) => void;
    phoneValidation: {
        isValid: boolean | null;
        isValidating: boolean;
        message: string;
        warning?: string;
    };
    countryCode: string;
    setCountryCode: (code: string) => void;
    phoneNumber: string;
    setPhoneNumber: (phone: string) => void;
    dateOfBirth: string;
    setDateOfBirth: (date: string) => void;
    city: string;
    setCity: (city: string) => void;
    country: string;
    setCountry: (country: string) => void;
    nationality: string;
    setNationality: (nationality: string) => void;
    doubleNationality: string;
    setDoubleNationality: (nationality: string) => void;
    jobTitle: string;
    specialty: string;
    setSpecialty: (specialty: string) => void;
    specialtySearch: string;
    setSpecialtySearch: (search: string) => void;
    websites: string[];
    setWebsites: (websites: string[]) => void;
    additionalInfo: { type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text'; content: string; file?: File }[];
    setAdditionalInfo: (info: { type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text'; content: string; file?: File }[]) => void;
    cvFile: File | null;
    passportFile: File | null;
    photoFile: File | null;
    isLoading: boolean;
    error: string | null;
    success: string | null;
    showSuccessToast: boolean;
    setShowSuccessToast: (show: boolean) => void;
    isExistingCandidate: boolean;
    addAdditionalInfo: (type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text') => void;
    removeAdditionalInfo: (index: number) => void;
    updateAdditionalInfo: (index: number, content: string, file?: File) => void;
    handleCategorySelection: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleJobSelection: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleFileChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
    handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
    selectedCategory: string;
    filteredJobs: Job[];
    selectedJob: Job | null;
    availableSpecialties: string[];
    calculateAge: (dob: string) => number | null;
}
"use client";

import React from 'react';

interface AdditionalInfoItemProps {
  info: { type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text'; content: string; file?: File };
  index: number;
  updateAdditionalInfo: (index: number, content: string, file?: File) => void;
  removeAdditionalInfo: (index: number) => void;
  isLoading: boolean;
  canRemove: boolean; // New prop to control remove button visibility
}

const AdditionalInfoItem: React.FC<AdditionalInfoItemProps> = ({
  info,
  index,
  updateAdditionalInfo,
  removeAdditionalInfo,
  isLoading,
  canRemove,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-2">
      <select
        value={info.type}
        onChange={(e) => {
          updateAdditionalInfo(index, '', undefined); // Clear content and file on type change
          // The parent component will handle updating the type in its state
        }}
        className="px-3 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white min-w-[100px]"
        disabled={isLoading}
      >
        <option value="link">Link</option>
        <option value="file">File</option>
        <option value="text">Text</option>
      </select>
      
      <div className="flex-1 w-full">
        {info.type === 'link' && (
          <input type="url" value={info.content} onChange={(e) => updateAdditionalInfo(index, e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="https://linkedin.com/in/yourprofile" disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
        {info.type === 'file' && (
          <input type="file" onChange={(e) => { const file = e.target.files?.[0]; if (file) { updateAdditionalInfo(index, file.name, file); } }}
            className="w-full text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 file:mr-4 file:py-2.5 file:px-4 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:text-sm file:font-semibold file:bg-slate-100 dark:file:bg-gray-600 file:text-[#003A70] dark:file:text-blue-300 hover:file:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:file:bg-gray-500"
            disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
        {info.type === 'text' && (
          <textarea value={info.content} onChange={(e) => updateAdditionalInfo(index, e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="Enter additional notes..." rows={2} disabled={isLoading} />
        )}
      </div>
      
      {canRemove && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => removeAdditionalInfo(index)}
          className="p-3 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-200 dark:hover:bg-red-900/80 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 self-center sm:self-auto"
          disabled={isLoading}>
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16" /></svg>
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default AdditionalInfoItem;
// src/app/components/candidate-form/AdditionalInfoSection.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { AdditionalInfoSectionProps } from '@/types';
import AdditionalInfoItem from './AdditionalInfoItem';

const AdditionalInfoSection: React.FC<AdditionalInfoSectionProps> = ({
  additionalInfo,
  addAdditionalInfo,
  removeAdditionalInfo,
  updateAdditionalInfo,
  isLoading,
}) => (
  <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-lg">
    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">Additional Information</h2>
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {additionalInfo.map((info, index) => (
        <AdditionalInfoItem
          key={index}
          info={info}
          index={index}
          updateAdditionalInfo={updateAdditionalInfo}
          removeAdditionalInfo={removeAdditionalInfo}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          canRemove={additionalInfo.length > 1}
        />
      ))}
      
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 pt-2">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => addAdditionalInfo('link')} disabled={isLoading}
          className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-sky-100 dark:bg-sky-900/50 text-sky-800 dark:text-sky-300 rounded-lg hover:bg-sky-200 dark:hover:bg-sky-900/80 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-sky-500 transition-colors">
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13.828 10.172a4 4 0 00-5.656 0l-4 4a4 4 0 105.656 5.656l1.102-1.101m-.758-4.899a4 4 0 005.656 0l4-4a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-1.1 1.1" /></svg>
          <span>Add Link</span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => addAdditionalInfo('file')} disabled={isLoading}
          className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/50 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300 rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-200 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/80 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-colors">
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15.172 7l-6.586 6.586a2 2 0 102.828 2.828l6.414-6.586a4 4 0 00-5.656-5.656l-6.415 6.585a6 6 0 108.486 8.486L20.5 13" /></svg>
          <span>Add File</span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={() => addAdditionalInfo('text')} disabled={isLoading}
          className="flex items-center space-x-2 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-violet-100 dark:bg-violet-900/50 text-violet-800 dark:text-violet-300 rounded-lg hover:bg-violet-200 dark:hover:bg-violet-900/80 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-violet-500 transition-colors">
          <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M4 6h16M4 12h16M4 18h7" /></svg>
          <span>Add Text</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
);

export default AdditionalInfoSection;

                    <p>Calculated Age: {calculateAge(dateOfBirth)} years</p>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};
