// src/app/components/candidate-form/JobApplicationSection.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { JOB_CATEGORIES } from '@/lib/job-categories';
import { JobApplicationSectionProps } from '@/types';
import SpecialtyDropdown from './SpecialtyDropdown';

const JobApplicationSection: React.FC<JobApplicationSectionProps> = ({
  selectedCategory,
  handleCategorySelection,
  jobTitle,
  handleJobSelection,
  filteredJobs,
  selectedJob,
  specialty,
  setSpecialty,
  specialtySearch,
  setSpecialtySearch,
  availableSpecialties,
  isLoading,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-lg">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">Job Application</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* Job Category */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="jobCategory" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Job Category <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            name="jobCategory"
            id="jobCategory"
            required
            value={selectedCategory}
            onChange={handleCategorySelection}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            disabled={isLoading}
          >
            <option value="">Select a category first</option>
            {JOB_CATEGORIES.map((category) => (
              <option key={category} value={category}>
                {category}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        {/* Job Title */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="jobTitle" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Job Position <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <select
            name="jobTitle"
            id="jobTitle"
            required
            value={jobTitle}
            onChange={handleJobSelection}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            disabled={isLoading || !selectedCategory}
          >
            <option value="">
              {selectedCategory ? 'Select a job position' : 'Please select a category first'}
            </option>
            {filteredJobs.map((job) => (
              <option key={job.id} value={job.id}>
                {job.title}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {selectedJob && (
            <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-gray-700/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg">
              <h4 className="font-semibold text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 mb-1">Job Description:</h4>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">{selectedJob.description}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1">Category: {selectedJob.category}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Specialty */}
        <SpecialtyDropdown
          specialty={specialty}
          setSpecialty={setSpecialty}
          specialtySearch={specialtySearch}
          setSpecialtySearch={setSpecialtySearch}
          availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          selectedJob={selectedJob}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default JobApplicationSection;
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { countryCodes, countries } from '@/lib/formData';
import { PersonalInfoSectionProps } from '@/types';
import SearchableDropdown from '../ui/SearchableDropdown';

interface PersonalInfoSectionPropsExtended extends PersonalInfoSectionProps {
  phoneValidation?: {
    isValid: boolean | null;
    isValidating: boolean;
    message: string;
    warning?: string;
  };
}

const PersonalInfoSection: React.FC<PersonalInfoSectionPropsExtended> = ({
  firstName,
  setFirstName,
  lastName,
  setLastName,
  email,
  gender,
  setGender,
  dateOfBirth,
  setDateOfBirth,
  countryCode,
  setCountryCode,
  phoneNumber,
  setPhoneNumber,
  city,
  setCity,
  country,
  setCountry,
  nationality,
  setNationality,
  doubleNationality,
  setDoubleNationality,
  isLoading,
  isExistingCandidate,
  calculateAge,
  phoneValidation
}) => {
  const countryCodeRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showCountryCodeList, setShowCountryCodeList] = useState(false);
  const [countryCodeSearch, setCountryCodeSearch] = useState('');
  
  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Name validation states
  const [firstNameError, setFirstNameError] = useState('');
  const [lastNameError, setLastNameError] = useState('');

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Name validation function
  const validateName = (name: string, fieldName: string): string => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      return `${fieldName} is required`;
    }
    
    // Check for numbers
    if (/\d/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain numbers`;
    }
    
    // Check for special characters (allow only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes)
    if (!/^[a-zA-ZÃ€-Ã¿Ä€-Å¾Ð-Ñ\s'-]+$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} can only contain letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes`;
    }
    
    // Check for dots
    if (/\./.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain dots`;
    }
    
    // Check minimum length
    if (name.trim().length < 2) {
      return `${fieldName} must be at least 2 characters long`;
    }
    
    // Check maximum length
    if (name.trim().length > 50) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot exceed 50 characters`;
    }
    
    // Check for excessive spaces
    if (/\s{2,}/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain multiple consecutive spaces`;
    }
    
    // Check if starts or ends with space/hyphen/apostrophe
    if (/^[\s'-]|[\s'-]$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot start or end with spaces, hyphens, or apostrophes`;
    }
    
    return '';
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Handle first name change with validation
  const handleFirstNameChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    
    // Allow typing but validate after
    setFirstName(value);
    
    // Real-time validation
    const error = validateName(value, 'First name');
    setFirstNameError(error);
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Handle last name change with validation
  const handleLastNameChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const value = e.target.value;
    
    // Allow typing but validate after
    setLastName(value);
    
    // Real-time validation
    const error = validateName(value, 'Last name');
    setLastNameError(error);
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Handle input key press (prevent certain characters)
  const handleNameKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const char = e.key;
    
    // Prevent numbers and common special characters from being typed
    if (/[0-9!@#$%^&*()_+=\[\]{};:"\\|,.<>?/~`]/.test(char)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Handle paste event to prevent pasting invalid content
  const handleNamePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>, fieldName: string) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    const pastedText = e.clipboardData.getData('text');
    const cleanedText = pastedText.replace(/[^a-zA-ZÃ€-Ã¿Ä€-Å¾Ð-Ñ\s'-]/g, '');
    
    if (fieldName === 'firstName') {
      setFirstName(cleanedText);
      const error = validateName(cleanedText, 'First name');
      setFirstNameError(error);
    } else {
      setLastName(cleanedText);
      const error = validateName(cleanedText, 'Last name');
      setLastNameError(error);
    }
  };

  const filteredCountryCodes = countryCodes.filter(item => 
    item.country.toLowerCase().includes(countryCodeSearch.toLowerCase()) ||
    item.code.includes(countryCodeSearch) ||
    item.flag.includes(countryCodeSearch)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (countryCodeRef.current && !countryCodeRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowCountryCodeList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-5 sm:p-6 rounded-lg">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 mb-6 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 pb-3">Personal Information</h2>
      
      {/* Age requirement notice */}
      <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
          <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
          </svg>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300 mb-1">Age Requirement</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300/80">
              You must be at least <strong>18 years old</strong> to submit an application. 
              Please ensure your date of birth is accurate as it will be verified.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: First Name with validation */}
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="firstName" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            First Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="firstName"
            id="firstName"
            required
            value={firstName}
            onChange={handleFirstNameChange}
            onKeyPress={handleNameKeyPress}
            onPaste={(e) => handleNamePaste(e, 'firstName')}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
              isExistingCandidate 
                ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600' 
                : firstNameError
                ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                : firstName && !firstNameError
                ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30'
                : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
            }`}
            placeholder="Enter your first name"
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            autoComplete="given-name"
            maxLength={50}
          />
          
          {/* ðŸ”¥ NEW: First name validation feedback */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && firstNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{firstNameError}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && firstName && !firstNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Valid first name</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && !firstName && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes allowed
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: Last Name with validation */}
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="lastName" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Last Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="lastName"
            id="lastName"
            required
            value={lastName}
            onChange={handleLastNameChange}
            onKeyPress={handleNameKeyPress}
            onPaste={(e) => handleNamePaste(e, 'lastName')}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
              isExistingCandidate 
                ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600' 
                : lastNameError
                ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                : lastName && !lastNameError
                ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30'
                : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
            }`}
            placeholder="Enter your last name"
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            autoComplete="family-name"
            maxLength={50}
          />
          
          {/* ðŸ”¥ NEW: Last name validation feedback */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && lastNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{lastNameError}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && lastName && !lastNameError && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-green-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
              </svg>
              <p className="text-sm text-green-600 dark:text-green-400">Valid last name</p>
            </div>
          )}
          
          {!isExistingCandidate && !lastName && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Only letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes allowed
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Email Address <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="email"
            name="email"
            id="email"
            required
            value={email}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed"
            disabled={true}
          />
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">This email was verified in the previous step</p>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="gender" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Gender <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <select
            name="gender"
            id="gender"
            required
            value={gender}
            onChange={(e) => setGender(e.target.value)}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white ${
              isExistingCandidate ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' : ''
            }`}
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
          >
            <option value="">Select Gender</option>
            <option value="male">Male</option>
            <option value="female">Female</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="dateOfBirth" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Date of Birth <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
            {isExistingCandidate && (
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 ml-2">(Cannot be modified)</span>
            )}
          </label>
          <input
            type="date"
            name="dateOfBirth"
            id="dateOfBirth"
            required
            value={dateOfBirth}
            onChange={(e) => setDateOfBirth(e.target.value)}
            className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white ${
              isExistingCandidate ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' : ''
            }`}
            disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
            max={new Date(new Date().setFullYear(new Date().getFullYear() - 18)).toISOString().split('T')[0]}
          />
          {/* Show calculated age with validation */}
          {dateOfBirth && (
            <div className="mt-1">
              {(() => {
                const age = calculateAge ? calculateAge(dateOfBirth) : 0;
                const isValidAge = age !== null && age >= 18;
                
                return (
                  <p className={`text-xs mt-1 ${isValidAge ? 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400' : 'text-red-600 font-medium'}`}>
                    {age !== null && age > 0 ? (
                      isValidAge ? (
                        <>Age: {age} years old</>
                      ) : (
                        <>âš ï¸ Age: {age} years old - You must be at least 18 years old to apply</>
                      )
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-red-600">Invalid date format</span>
                    )}
                  </p>
                );
              })()}
            </div>
          )}
          {isExistingCandidate && dateOfBirth && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
              Age: {calculateAge ? calculateAge(dateOfBirth) : 0} years old
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Phone Number Section - Completely different for existing vs new candidates */}
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
          <label htmlFor="phoneNumber" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Phone Number <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          
          {/* ðŸ”¥ CONDITIONAL: Only show validation info for NEW candidates */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && (
            <div className="bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-900/20 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500/30 rounded-lg p-3 mb-3">
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                </svg>
                <div className="text-xs text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">
                  <p className="font-medium mb-1">ðŸ“ž Phone Verification System</p>
                  <p>Your phone number will be validated using our secure 3-tier verification system to ensure accuracy and deliverability.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex space-x-2">
            {/* Country Code Dropdown */}
            <div className="relative" ref={countryCodeRef}>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (!isLoading && !isExistingCandidate) {
                    setShowCountryCodeList(!showCountryCodeList);
                    setCountryCodeSearch('');
                  }
                }}
                className={`w-32 px-3 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-l-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] text-sm flex items-center justify-between ${
                  isLoading || isExistingCandidate 
                    ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed opacity-75' 
                    : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-gray-700 cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600'
                }`}
                disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
              >
                <span className="dark:text-white">
                  {countryCodes.find(item => item.code === countryCode)?.flag} {countryCode}
                </span>
                {!isExistingCandidate && (
                  <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
                  </svg>
                )}
              </button>
              
              {showCountryCodeList && !isLoading && !isExistingCandidate && (
                <div className="absolute z-20 w-80 mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg">
                  <div className="p-2 border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-600">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={countryCodeSearch}
                      onChange={(e) => setCountryCodeSearch(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-600 dark:text-white"
                      placeholder="Search country..."
                      autoFocus
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
                    {filteredCountryCodes.map((item, index) => (
                      <button
                        key={index}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setCountryCode(item.code);
                          setShowCountryCodeList(false);
                          setCountryCodeSearch('');
                        }}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none flex items-center space-x-3"
                      >
                        <span className="text-lg">{item.flag}</span>
                        <span className="font-medium dark:text-white">{item.code}</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 truncate">{item.country}</span>
                      </button>
                    ))}
                    {filteredCountryCodes.length === 0 && (
                      <div className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        No countries found
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            
            {/* Phone Number Input */}
            <div className="flex-1">
              <input
                type="tel"
                name="phoneNumber"
                id="phoneNumber"
                required
                value={phoneNumber}
                onChange={(e) => setPhoneNumber(e.target.value)}
                className={`w-full px-4 py-3 border rounded-r-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white transition-all duration-300 ${
                  isExistingCandidate 
                    ? 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 cursor-not-allowed' 
                    : phoneValidation?.isValid === true 
                    ? 'border-green-400 dark:border-green-500 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 ring-1 ring-green-200 dark:ring-green-500/30' 
                    : phoneValidation?.isValid === false 
                    ? 'border-red-400 dark:border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-900/20 ring-1 ring-red-200 dark:ring-red-500/30'
                    : phoneValidation?.isValidating
                    ? 'border-blue-400 dark:border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 ring-1 ring-blue-200 dark:ring-blue-500/30'
                    : 'border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600'
                }`}
                placeholder="Enter your phone number"
                disabled={isLoading || isExistingCandidate}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          {/* ðŸ”¥ CONDITIONAL: Phone Validation Feedback - ONLY for new candidates */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && (phoneValidation?.isValidating || phoneValidation?.message) && (
            <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-lg border transition-all duration-300" style={{
              backgroundColor: phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                ? 'rgb(240 253 244)' 
                : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                ? 'rgb(254 242 242)'
                : 'rgb(239 246 255)',
              borderColor: phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                ? 'rgb(34 197 94)' 
                : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                ? 'rgb(239 68 68)'
                : 'rgb(59 130 246)'
            }}>
              <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                {phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                  <div className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    <div className="w-5 h-5 border-2 border-blue-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
                  </div>
                )}
                
                {!phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                  <div className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                    {phoneValidation.isValid === true ? (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : phoneValidation.isValid === false ? (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-red-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M10 14l2-2m0 0l2-2m-2 2l-2-2m2 2l2 2m7-2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    ) : (
                      <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                      </svg>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
                
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${
                    phoneValidation.isValid === true 
                      ? 'text-green-700' 
                      : phoneValidation.isValid === false 
                      ? 'text-red-700'
                      : 'text-blue-700'
                  }`}>
                    {phoneValidation.message}
                  </p>
                  
                  {phoneValidation.isValidating && (
                    <div className="mt-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs text-blue-600">
                        <span>Verifying through secure validation system...</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="mt-1 w-full bg-blue-200 rounded-full h-1">
                        <div className="bg-blue-600 h-1 rounded-full animate-pulse" style={{ width: '60%' }}></div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  
                  {phoneValidation.warning && (
                    <div className="mt-2 flex items-start space-x-2">
                      <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-2.5L13.732 4c-.77-.833-1.964-.833-2.732 0L3.732 16.5c-.77.833.192 2.5 1.732 2.5z" />
                      </svg>
                      <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 font-medium">
                        {phoneValidation.warning}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* ðŸ”¥ CONDITIONAL: Help text based on user type */}
          {!isExistingCandidate && !phoneValidation?.message && (
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-2">
              Enter your phone number without the country code (e.g., for +358 123 456 789, enter "123 456 789")
            </p>
          )}

          {isExistingCandidate && (
            <div className="mt-2 p-2 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/20 rounded-lg border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-500/30">
              <p className="text-xs text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 font-medium">
                ðŸ”’ Phone number from your previous application (already validated).
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="city" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            City <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            name="city"
            id="city"
            required
            value={city}
            onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
            placeholder="Enter your city"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="country" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Country <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={country}
            onChange={setCountry}
            placeholder="Search and select your country"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="nationality" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Nationality <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={nationality}
            onChange={setNationality}
            placeholder="Search and select your nationality"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="doubleNationality" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
            Second Nationality <span className="text-slate-400">(Optional)</span>
          </label>
          <SearchableDropdown
            options={countries}
            value={doubleNationality}
            onChange={setDoubleNationality}
            placeholder="Search and select second nationality"
            disabled={isLoading}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default PersonalInfoSection;
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Job } from '@/types';

interface SpecialtyDropdownProps {
  specialty: string;
  setSpecialty: (specialty: string) => void;
  specialtySearch: string;
  setSpecialtySearch: (search: string) => void;
  availableSpecialties: string[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  selectedJob: Job | null;
}

const SpecialtyDropdown: React.FC<SpecialtyDropdownProps> = ({
  specialty,
  setSpecialty,
  specialtySearch,
  setSpecialtySearch,
  availableSpecialties,
  isLoading,
  selectedJob,
}) => {
  const specialtyRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showSpecialtyList, setShowSpecialtyList] = useState(false);

  const filteredSpecialties = availableSpecialties.filter(spec => 
    spec.toLowerCase().includes(specialtySearch.toLowerCase())
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (specialtyRef.current && !specialtyRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowSpecialtyList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative" ref={specialtyRef}>
      <label htmlFor="specialtySearch" className="block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-2">
        Specialty <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
      </label>
      <input
        type="text"
        name="specialtySearch"
        id="specialtySearch"
        required
        value={specialtySearch}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const value = e.target.value;
          setSpecialtySearch(value);
          if (availableSpecialties.includes(value)) {
            setSpecialty(value);
          } else {
            setSpecialty('');
          }
          setShowSpecialtyList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowSpecialtyList(true)}
        className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-white"
        placeholder={selectedJob ? "Search and select your specialty" : "Please select a job position first"}
        disabled={isLoading || !selectedJob}
        autoComplete="off"
      />
      {showSpecialtyList && filteredSpecialties.length > 0 && selectedJob && (
        <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredSpecialties.map((spec, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSpecialty(spec);
                setSpecialtySearch(spec);
                setShowSpecialtyList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {spec}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {selectedJob && availableSpecialties.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/20 rounded-lg">
          <p className="text-sm text-green-800 dark:text-green-300 font-medium">Available Specialties:</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1">
            {availableSpecialties.map((spec, index) => (
              <span key={index} className="px-2 py-1 text-xs bg-green-200 dark:bg-green-900/50 text-green-800 dark:text-green-300 rounded-full">
                {spec}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {selectedJob && availableSpecialties.length === 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-yellow-50 dark:bg-yellow-900/20 rounded-lg">
          <p className="text-sm text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300">No specialties defined for this job position. Please contact admin.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SpecialtyDropdown;

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface CandidateTableActionsProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onViewCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onScoreCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onEditCandidate?: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
}

interface ActionButtonProps {
    onClick: () => void;
    icon: React.ReactElement;
    srText: string;
    color: string;
}

const ActionButton: React.FC<ActionButtonProps> = ({ onClick, icon, srText, color }) => (
    <button
        onClick={onClick}
        className={`p-2 rounded-full text-${color}-500 hover:bg-${color}-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 hover:text-${color}-700 dark:text-${color}-400 dark:hover:text-${color}-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-${color}-500 transition-colors`}
    >
        <span className="sr-only">{srText}</span>
        {icon}
    </button>
);

const CandidateTableActions: React.FC<CandidateTableActionsProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onViewCandidate,
    onScoreCandidate,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    return (
        <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-right text-sm font-medium">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-1">
                <ActionButton
                    onClick={() => onViewCandidate(candidate)}
                    icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z" /><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M2.458 12C3.732 7.943 7.523 5 12 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.542 7-1.274 4.057-5.064 7-9.542 7-4.477 0-8.268-2.943-9.542-7z" /></svg>}
                    srText="View"
                    color="blue"
                />
                {onEditCandidate && (
                    <ActionButton
                        onClick={() => onEditCandidate(candidate)}
                        icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z" /></svg>}
                        srText="Edit"
                        color="green"
                    />
                )}
                <ActionButton
                    onClick={() => onScoreCandidate(candidate)}
                    icon={<svg className="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M11.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.519 4.674a1 1 0 00.95.69h4.915c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-3.976 2.888a1 1 0 00-.363 1.118l1.518 4.674c.3.922-.755 1.688-1.539 1.118l-3.975-2.888a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-3.976 2.888c-.783.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.518-4.674a1 1 0 00-.363-1.118l-3.976-2.888c-.784-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h4.914a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.519-4.674z" /></svg>}
                    srText="Score"
                    color="purple"
                />
            </div>
        </td>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableActions;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { SortDirection, SortField } from '@/types';

interface CandidateTableHeaderProps {
    sortField: SortField;
    sortDirection: SortDirection;
    handleSort: (field: SortField) => void;
}

const SortableHeader: React.FC<{
    field: SortField;
    label: string;
    sortField: SortField;
    sortDirection: SortDirection;
    handleSort: (field: SortField) => void;
}> = ({ field, label, sortField, sortDirection, handleSort }) => {
    const isSorted = sortField === field;
    
    const getSortIcon = () => {
        if (!isSorted) {
            return (
                <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-300 dark:text-gray-500 group-hover:text-gray-400 dark:group-hover:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M8 9l4-4 4 4m0 6l-4 4-4-4" />
                </svg>
            );
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? (
            <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 15l7-7 7 7" />
            </svg>
        ) : (
            <svg className="w-4 h-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7" />
            </svg>
        );
    };

    return (
        <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
            <button
                onClick={() => handleSort(field)}
                className="group flex items-center space-x-2"
            >
                <span>{label}</span>
                {getSortIcon()}
            </button>
        </th>
    );
};

const CandidateTableHeader: React.FC<CandidateTableHeaderProps> = ({
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
    handleSort,
}) => {
    const headers: { field: SortField; label: string }[] = [
        { field: 'firstName', label: 'Name' },
        { field: 'email', label: 'Email' },
        { field: 'age', label: 'Age' },
        { field: 'city', label: 'City' },
        { field: 'country', label: 'Country' },
        { field: 'submittedAt', label: 'Submitted' },
        { field: 'status', label: 'Status' },
        { field: 'score', label: 'AI Score' },
    ];

    return (
        <thead className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-700 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-600">
            <tr>
                {headers.map((header) => (
                    <SortableHeader
                        key={header.field}
                        field={header.field}
                        label={header.label}
                        sortField={sortField}
                        sortDirection={sortDirection}
                        handleSort={handleSort}
                    />
                ))}
                <th scope="col" className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Actions
                </th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableHeader;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import StatusBadge from '../ui/StatusBadge';
import CandidateTableActions from './CandidateTableActions';

interface CandidateTableRowProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    splitName: (fullName: string) => { firstName: string; lastName: string };
    calculateAge: (dateOfBirth: string) => number | null;
    onViewCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onScoreCandidate: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
    onEditCandidate?: (candidate: Candidate) => void;
}

const CandidateTableRow: React.FC<CandidateTableRowProps> = ({
    candidate,
    splitName,
    calculateAge,
    onViewCandidate,
    onScoreCandidate,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    const { firstName, lastName } = splitName(candidate.fullName || '');
    const age = candidate.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(candidate.dateOfBirth) : null;

    return (
        <tr key={candidate.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-700/50 transition-colors duration-150">
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-medium text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                <div className="flex items-center">
                    <div className="flex-shrink-0 h-10 w-10">
                        {candidate.photoUrl ? (
                            <img className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full object-cover" src={candidate.photoUrl} alt="" />
                        ) : (
                            <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center">
                                <span className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{firstName.charAt(0)}{lastName.charAt(0)}</span>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="ml-4">
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white">{firstName} {lastName}</div>
                        <div className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 md:hidden">{candidate.email}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.email}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {age || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.city || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="hidden md:table-cell px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.country || 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                {candidate.submittedAt
                    ? new Date(candidate.submittedAt.toDate()).toLocaleDateString()
                    : 'N/A'
                }
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                <StatusBadge status={candidate.status} />
            </td>
            <td className="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">
                {candidate.score !== null ? candidate.score : 'N/A'}
            </td>
            <CandidateTableActions
                candidate={candidate}
                onViewCandidate={onViewCandidate}
                onScoreCandidate={onScoreCandidate}
                onEditCandidate={onEditCandidate}
            />
        </tr>
    );
};

export default CandidateTableRow;

interface ExportButtonsProps {
    isExporting: boolean;
    handleExport: (format: 'csv' | 'xlsx' | 'json') => void;
}

const ExportButtons: React.FC<ExportButtonsProps> = ({ isExporting, handleExport }) => {
    return (
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('csv')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export CSV'}
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('xlsx')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-green-600 rounded-md hover:bg-green-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export XLSX'}
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={() => handleExport('json')}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white bg-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:bg-gray-400"
            >
                {isExporting ? 'Exporting...' : 'Export JSON'}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface QuickFilterButtonsProps {
  label: string;
  options: string[];
  filterValue: string;
  onFilterChange: (value: string) => void;
  candidates: Candidate[];
  filterKey: keyof Candidate;
  colorClass: string;
  sliceLimit?: number;
}

const QuickFilterButtons: React.FC<QuickFilterButtonsProps> = ({
  label,
  options,
  filterValue,
  onFilterChange,
  candidates,
  filterKey,
  colorClass,
  sliceLimit,
}) => {
  const displayOptions = sliceLimit ? options.slice(0, sliceLimit) : options;

  // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Define static color combinations to ensure Tailwind includes them
  const getColorClasses = (color: string, isSelected: boolean) => {
    const colorMap = {
      blue: {
        selected: 'bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 border-blue-300 dark:border-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/20'
      },
      green: {
        selected: 'bg-green-600 text-white border-green-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-green-600 dark:text-green-300 border-green-300 dark:border-green-600 hover:bg-green-50 dark:hover:bg-green-900/20'
      },
      purple: {
        selected: 'bg-purple-600 text-white border-purple-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-300 border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-50 dark:hover:bg-purple-900/20'
      },
      orange: {
        selected: 'bg-orange-600 text-white border-orange-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-orange-600 dark:text-orange-300 border-orange-300 dark:border-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-50 dark:hover:bg-orange-900/20'
      },
      red: {
        selected: 'bg-red-600 text-white border-red-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-red-600 dark:text-red-300 border-red-300 dark:border-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/20'
      },
      indigo: {
        selected: 'bg-indigo-600 text-white border-indigo-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-300 border-indigo-300 dark:border-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-50 dark:hover:bg-indigo-900/20'
      },
      pink: {
        selected: 'bg-pink-600 text-white border-pink-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-pink-600 dark:text-pink-300 border-pink-300 dark:border-pink-600 hover:bg-pink-50 dark:hover:bg-pink-900/20'
      },
      teal: {
        selected: 'bg-teal-600 text-white border-teal-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-300 border-teal-300 dark:border-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-50 dark:hover:bg-teal-900/20'
      },
      cyan: {
        selected: 'bg-cyan-600 text-white border-cyan-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-cyan-600 dark:text-cyan-300 border-cyan-300 dark:border-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-50 dark:hover:bg-cyan-900/20'
      },
      slate: {
        selected: 'bg-slate-600 text-white border-slate-600',
        unselected: 'bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border-slate-300 dark:border-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-900/20'
      }
    };

    // Fallback to blue if color not found
    const colorConfig = colorMap[color as keyof typeof colorMap] || colorMap.blue;
    return isSelected ? colorConfig.selected : colorConfig.unselected;
  };

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 mr-3">{label}:</span>
      <div className="inline-flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-1">
        {displayOptions.map(option => {
          const count = candidates.filter(c => c[filterKey] === option).length;
          const isSelected = filterValue === option;
          
          return (
            <button
              key={option}
              onClick={() => {
                if (isSelected) {
                  onFilterChange(''); // Deselect if already selected
                } else {
                  onFilterChange(option);
                }
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-full border transition-all duration-200 font-medium ${getColorClasses(colorClass, isSelected)}`}
              title={`Filter by ${option} (${count} ${count === 1 ? 'candidate' : 'candidates'})`}
            >
              {option} ({count})
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default QuickFilterButtons;
"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface SearchableFilterDropdownProps {
  label: string;
  placeholder: string;
  filterValue: string; // The currently applied filter value from parent state
  onFilterChange: (value: string) => void; // Handler to update parent filter state
  searchValue: string; // Local search input value
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void; // Handler to update local search state
  options: string[]; // All unique options available
  candidates: Candidate[]; // All candidates for counting
  filterKey: keyof Candidate; // Key to filter candidates by (e.g., 'city', 'country')
  disabled?: boolean;
}

const SearchableFilterDropdown: React.FC<SearchableFilterDropdownProps> = ({
  label,
  placeholder,
  filterValue,
  onFilterChange,
  searchValue,
  onSearchChange,
  options,
  candidates,
  filterKey,
  disabled = false,
}) => {
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);

  const filteredOptions = options.filter(option => 
    option.toLowerCase().includes(searchValue.toLowerCase())
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
      <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">{label}</label>
      <input
        type="text"
        placeholder={placeholder}
        value={searchValue}
        onChange={(e) => {
          onSearchChange(e.target.value);
          onFilterChange(''); // Clear parent filter when typing in search
          setShowList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowList(true)}
        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
        autoComplete="off"
        disabled={disabled}
      />
      {showList && filteredOptions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute z-20 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredOptions.map((option, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onFilterChange(option);
                onSearchChange(option); // Update local search to match selected option
                setShowList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-gray-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none flex justify-between items-center text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
            >
              <span>{option}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                ({candidates.filter(c => c[filterKey] === option).length})
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SearchableFilterDropdown;

interface EditCandidateFormProps {
    formData: Partial<Candidate>;
    errors: Record<string, string>;
    jobCategories: any[];
    loadingJobs: boolean;
    availableSpecialties: string[];
    filteredJobs: any[];
    handleInputChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleCategoryChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleJobChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
    handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
    isLoading: boolean;
}

const EditCandidateForm: React.FC<EditCandidateFormProps> = ({
    formData,
    errors,
    jobCategories,
    loadingJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    filteredJobs,
    handleInputChange,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleSubmit,
    isLoading,
}) => {
    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <PersonalInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                errors={errors}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
            />
            <LocationInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                errors={errors}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
            />
            <JobInfoForm
                formData={formData}
                jobCategories={jobCategories}
                filteredJobs={filteredJobs}
                availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
                handleCategoryChange={handleCategoryChange}
                handleJobChange={handleJobChange}
                handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
                isLoading={isLoading}
                loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
            />
        </form>
    );
};

export default EditCandidateForm;

interface EditModalFooterProps {
    onClose: () => void;
    handleSubmit: (e: React.FormEvent) => Promise<void>;
    isLoading: boolean;
    loadingJobs: boolean;
}

const EditModalFooter: React.FC<EditModalFooterProps> = ({ onClose, handleSubmit, isLoading, loadingJobs }) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-6 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-end space-x-3 flex-shrink-0">
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors"
                disabled={isLoading}
            >
                Cancel
            </button>
            <button
                onClick={handleSubmit}
                disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs}
                className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
                {isLoading ? (
                    <>
                        <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mr-2"></div>
                        Saving...
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <>
                        <FiSave className="mr-2" />
                        Save Changes
                    </>
                )}
            </button>
        </div>
    );
};

interface JobInfoFormProps {

const JobInfoForm: React.FC<JobInfoFormProps> = ({
    formData,
    jobCategories,
    filteredJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleInputChange,

    loadingJobs,
}) => {
    return (
        <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg">

            </h3>
            {loadingJobs && (
                <div className="text-center py-4">

                        <span className="text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">Loading jobs...</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="jobCategory" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Job Category
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="jobCategory"
                        name="jobCategory"
                        value={formData.jobCategory}
                        onChange={handleCategoryChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs}
                    >
                        <option value="">Select Category</option>
                        {jobCategories.map((category) => (
                            <option key={category} value={category}>
                                {category}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="jobTitle" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Job Position
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="jobTitle"
                        name="jobTitle"
                        value={formData.jobTitle}
                        onChange={handleJobChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || loadingJobs || !formData.jobCategory}
                    >
                        <option value="">
                            {!formData.jobCategory
                                ? 'Select category first'
                                : 'Select job position'
                            }
                        </option>
                        {filteredJobs.map((job) => (
                            <option key={job.id} value={job.id}>
                                {job.title}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                    {formData.jobCategory && filteredJobs.length === 0 && !loadingJobs && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-orange-600 mt-1">
                            No active jobs available in this category
                        </p>
                    )}
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                    <label htmlFor="specialty" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Specialty
                    </label>
                    <select
                        id="specialty"
                        name="specialty"
                        value={formData.specialty}
                        onChange={handleInputChange}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
                        disabled={isLoading || !formData.jobTitle}
                    >
                        <option value="">
                            {!formData.jobTitle
                                ? 'Select job position first'
                                : 'Select specialty'
                            }
                        </option>
                        {availableSpecialties.map((specialty) => (
                            <option key={specialty} value={specialty}>
                                {specialty}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>
                    {formData.jobTitle && availableSpecialties.length === 0 && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-orange-600 mt-1">
                            No specialties available for this job
                        </p>
                    )}
                </div>
                {formData.jobTitleText && (
                    <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <label htmlFor="jobTitleText" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                            Current Job Title
                        </label>
                        <input
                            id="jobTitleText"
                            name="jobTitleText"
                            type="text"
                            value={formData.jobTitleText}
                            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-md bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400"
                            disabled
                            readOnly
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1">
                            This will be updated automatically when you select a job position
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default JobInfoForm;

                        onChange={(value) => handleInputChange({ target: { name: 'nationality', value } } as React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>)}
                        placeholder="Search and select nationality"
                        disabled={isLoading}
                    />
                    {errors.nationality && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.nationality}</p>}
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label htmlFor="doubleNationality" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">
                        Second Nationality <span className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">(Optional)</span>

        </div>

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiEdit, FiCheckCircle, FiXCircle, FiCalendar } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard } from './InfoCard';

interface ActionsCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onStatusChange: (candidateId: string, newStatus: string) => void;
    onScheduleInterview: () => void;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const ActionsCard: React.FC<ActionsCardProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onStatusChange,
    onScheduleInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
}) => {
    const handleReject = () => onStatusChange(candidate.id, 'rejected');
    const handleApprove = () => onStatusChange(candidate.id, 'approved');

    const renderActionButtons = () => {
        if (candidate.status === 'approved' || candidate.status === 'rejected') {
            return (
                <div className="text-center py-4 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 italic">
                    This candidate has been <strong>{candidate.status}</strong>.
                </div>
            );
        }

        return (
            <div className="space-y-3">
                {candidate.status === 'submitted' && (
                     <button onClick={onScheduleInterview} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiCalendar className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Processing...' : 'Schedule Interview'}
                    </button>
                )}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <button onClick={handleApprove} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiCheckCircle className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve'}
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={handleReject} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="w-full flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors">
                        <FiXCircle className="mr-2" /> {statusUpdateLoading ? 'Rejecting...' : 'Reject'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    };

    return (
        <InfoCard title="Actions" icon={<FiEdit size={20} />}>
            {renderActionButtons()}
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default ActionsCard;

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">

                    <FiFileText className="mr-2" /> View CV/Resume
                </a>
                <a href={candidate.passportUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors">
                    <FiClipboard className="mr-2" /> View ID/Passport
                </a>
                {candidate.photoUrl && (
                    <a href={candidate.photoUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 transition-colors">
                        <FiImage className="mr-2" /> View Photo
                    </a>
                )}
            </div>
            {candidate.websites && candidate.websites.length > 0 && (
                <div className="pt-3 space-y-2">
                    {candidate.websites.map((site, index) => (
                        <a key={index} href={site.startsWith('http') ? site : `https://${site}`} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline break-all">
                            <FiLink size={14} className="mr-2" /> {site}
                        </a>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
        </InfoCard>
    );

import React from 'react';

export const InfoCard: React.FC<{ title: string; icon: React.ReactNode; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ title, icon, children }) => (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 shadow-sm">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex items-center">
            <div className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mr-3">{icon}</div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">{title}</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="p-4 space-y-3 text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">{children}</div>
    </div>
);

export const InfoRow: React.FC<{ label: string; value?: string | null; icon?: React.ReactNode }> = ({ label, value, icon }) => {
    if (!value && value !== null) return null;
    return (
        <div className="flex items-start">
            {icon && <div className="text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mr-2">{icon}</div>}
            <span className="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200 w-32 flex-shrink-0">{label}:</span>
            <span className="flex-1 break-words">{value ?? 'N/A'}</span>
        </div>
    );
};
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { FiCalendar, FiClock, FiExternalLink, FiEdit, FiTrash2 } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard, InfoRow } from './InfoCard';

interface InterviewCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onScheduleInterview: () => void;
    onCancelInterview: (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => void;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const InterviewCard: React.FC<InterviewCardProps> = ({
    candidate,
    onScheduleInterview,
    onCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
}) => {
    if (candidate?.status !== 'interview_scheduled' || !candidate?.interviewDate) {
        return null;
    }

    const interviewDate = candidate.interviewDate.toDate();
    const duration = candidate.duration || 30;
    const endDate = new Date(interviewDate.getTime() + duration * 60 * 1000);

    return (
        <InfoCard title="Interview Details" icon={<FiCalendar size={20} />}>
            <InfoRow label="Date" value={format(interviewDate, 'PPP')} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Time" value={`${format(interviewDate, 'p')} - ${format(endDate, 'p')}`} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            <InfoRow label="Duration" value={`${duration} minutes`} icon={<FiClock size={14} />} />
            {candidate.meetLink && (
                <div className="pt-2">
                    <a href={candidate.meetLink} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors w-full justify-center">
                        <FiExternalLink className="mr-2" /> Join Google Meet
                    </a>
                </div>
            )}
            <div className="pt-3 flex space-x-2">
                <button onClick={onScheduleInterview} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-300 bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-200 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 disabled:opacity-50">
                    <FiEdit className="mr-2" /> Reschedule
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => onCancelInterview(candidate.id, 'Cancelled by admin')} disabled={statusUpdateLoading} className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-700 dark:text-red-300 bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-red-200 dark:hover:bg-red-900/80 disabled:opacity-50">
                    <FiTrash2 className="mr-2" /> Cancel
                </button>
            </div>
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default InterviewCard;

import React from 'react';

import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

};

import React from 'react';
import { FiX, FiUser, FiEdit } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

interface ProfileHeaderProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
    onClose: () => void;
    onEdit: () => void;
    isUpdating: boolean;
    statusUpdateLoading: boolean;
}

const ProfileHeader: React.FC<ProfileHeaderProps> = ({ candidate, onClose, onEdit, isUpdating, statusUpdateLoading }) => {
    return (
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center flex-shrink-0">
            <div className="flex items-center">
                {candidate.photoUrl ? (
                    <img src={candidate.photoUrl} alt={candidate.fullName} className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full object-cover mr-4 border-2 border-white dark:border-gray-700 shadow" />
                ) : (
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center mr-4" data-testid="icon"><FiUser size={24} className="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400" /></div>
                )}
                <div>
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{candidate.fullName}</h2>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{candidate.jobTitleText}</p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <button
                    onClick={onEdit}
                    className="flex items-center px-3 py-2 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-300 bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50 rounded-md hover:bg-blue-200 dark:hover:bg-blue-900/80 transition-colors"
                    disabled={statusUpdateLoading || isUpdating}
                >
                    <FiEdit className="mr-2" size={16} />
                    Edit
                </button>
                <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 transition-colors rounded-full p-2">
                    <FiX size={24} />
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ProfileHeader;
