"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { FiAward, FiTrendingUp, FiAlertTriangle, FiCheckCircle, FiXCircle } from 'react-icons/fi';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import { InfoCard } from './InfoCard';

interface ScoringCardProps {
    candidate: Candidate;
}

const ScoringCard: React.FC<ScoringCardProps> = ({ candidate }) => {
    if (candidate.score === null || candidate.score === undefined) {
        return null;
    }

    // Get recommendation color and icon
    const getRecommendationDisplay = (recommendation?: string) => {
        switch (recommendation) {
            case 'highly_recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-green-600 bg-green-100', 
                    icon: <FiCheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Highly Recommended'
                };
            case 'recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-blue-600 bg-blue-100', 
                    icon: <FiTrendingUp className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Recommended'
                };
            case 'conditional':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-yellow-600 bg-yellow-100', 
                    icon: <FiAlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Conditional'
                };
            case 'not_recommended':
                return { 
                    color: 'text-red-600 bg-red-100', 
                    icon: <FiXCircle className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Not Recommended'
                };
            default:
                return { 
                    color: 'text-gray-600 bg-gray-100', 
                    icon: <FiAward className="w-4 h-4" />,
                    text: 'Evaluated'
                };
        }
    };

    const recommendationDisplay = getRecommendationDisplay(candidate.aiRecommendation);

    // Get score color based on value
    const getScoreColor = (score: number) => {
        if (score >= 80) return 'text-green-600';
        if (score >= 60) return 'text-blue-600';
        if (score >= 40) return 'text-yellow-600';
        return 'text-red-600';
    };

    const scoreColor = getScoreColor(candidate.score);

    return (
        <InfoCard title="ðŸ¤– AI Analysis (Gemini 2.5 Pro)" icon={<FiAward size={20} />}>
            <div className="space-y-4">
                {/* Score and Recommendation Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                        <div className="relative w-16 h-16">
                            <svg className="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 36 36">
                                <path 
                                    className="text-gray-200" 
                                    strokeWidth="3" 
                                    fill="none" 
                                    d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" 
                                />
                                <path 
                                    className={scoreColor}
                                    strokeWidth="3" 
                                    fill="none" 
                                    strokeDasharray={`${candidate.score}, 100`} 
                                    d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" 
                                />
                            </svg>
                            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
                                <span className={`text-xl font-bold ${scoreColor}`}>{candidate.score}</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600">AI Score</p>
                            {candidate.aiRecommendation && (
                                <div className={`inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${recommendationDisplay.color} dark:bg-opacity-20`}>
                                    {recommendationDisplay.icon}
                                    <span>{recommendationDisplay.text}</span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Analysis */}
                {candidate.scoreAnalysis && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2">ðŸ“‹ Analysis</h4>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">{candidate.scoreAnalysis}</p>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Key Strengths */}
                {candidate.keyStrengths && candidate.keyStrengths.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-green-600 mr-1">âœ…</span> Key Strengths
                        </h4>
                        <ul className="space-y-1">
                            {candidate.keyStrengths.map((strength, index) => (
                                <li key={index} className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 flex items-start">
                                    <span className="text-green-500 mr-2 mt-1">â€¢</span>
                                    <span>{strength}</span>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Areas for Improvement */}
                {candidate.areasForImprovement && candidate.areasForImprovement.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-2 flex items-center">
                            <span className="text-yellow-600 mr-1">âš ï¸</span> Development Areas
                        </h4>
                        <ul className="space-y-1">
                            {candidate.areasForImprovement.map((area, index) => (
                                <li key={index} className="text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 flex items-start">
                                    <span className="text-yellow-500 mr-2 mt-1">â€¢</span>
                                    <span>{area}</span>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Metadata */}
                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                        <span>
                            {candidate.lastScoredAt && 
                                `Analyzed: ${candidate.lastScoredAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString()}`
                            }
                        </span>
                        {candidate.scoringVersion && (
                            <span className="bg-purple-100 dark:bg-purple-900/50 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                                {candidate.scoringVersion}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </InfoCard>
    );
};

export default ScoringCard;

// src/app/components/ConfirmationModal.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ConfirmationModalProps } from '@/types';

const ConfirmationModal: React.FC<ConfirmationModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  title,
  message,
  confirmText = 'Confirm',
  cancelText = 'Cancel',
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  type = 'danger'
}) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const getButtonStyles = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500';
      case 'warning':
        return 'bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-700 focus:ring-yellow-500';
      case 'info':
        return 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus:ring-blue-500';
      default:
        return 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 focus:ring-red-500';
    }
  };

  const getIconColor = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return 'text-red-600';
      case 'warning':
        return 'text-yellow-600';
      case 'info':
        return 'text-blue-600';
      default:
        return 'text-red-600';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center z-50">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-md w-full mx-4">
        <div className="flex items-center mb-4">
          <div className={`flex-shrink-0 w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${type === 'danger' ? 'bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-900/50' : type === 'warning' ? 'bg-yellow-100 dark:bg-yellow-900/50' : 'bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/50'}`}>
            <span className={`text-xl ${getIconColor()}`}>
              {type === 'danger' ? 'âš ' : type === 'warning' ? 'âš ' : 'â„¹'}
            </span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="ml-3 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200">{title}</h3>
        </div>
        
        <div className="mb-6">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">{message}</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3">
          <button
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
          >
            {cancelText}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white rounded-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 ${getButtonStyles()}`}
          >
            {confirmText}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ConfirmationModal;

// src/app/components/ui/SearchableDropdown.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { SearchableDropdownProps } from '@/types';

const SearchableDropdown: React.FC<SearchableDropdownProps> = ({
  options,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  disabled = false,
  id,
}) => {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(value);
  const [showList, setShowList] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSearch(value);
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setShowList(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, []);

  const filteredOptions = options.filter(option =>
    option.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={ref}>
      <input
        id={id}
        type="text"
        value={search}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setSearch(e.target.value);
          onChange('');
          setShowList(true);
        }}
        onFocus={() => setShowList(true)}
        className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#003A70]/50 focus:border-[#003A70] bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
        placeholder={placeholder}
        disabled={disabled}
        autoComplete="off"
      />
      {showList && filteredOptions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute z-10 w-full mt-1 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 border border-slate-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg shadow-lg max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredOptions.map((option, index) => (
            <button
              key={index}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onChange(option);
                setSearch(option);
                setShowList(false);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 text-left hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:bg-slate-100 dark:focus:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
            >
              {option}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SearchableDropdown;

// src/app/components/ui/StatusBadge.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { StatusBadgeProps } from '@/types';

const StatusBadge: React.FC<StatusBadgeProps> = ({ status }) => {
  const getStatusStyle = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'submitted':
        return 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-900/50 dark:text-blue-300 dark:border-blue-500/30';
      case 'interview_scheduled':
        return 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-200 dark:bg-yellow-900/50 dark:text-yellow-300 dark:border-yellow-500/30';
      case 'approved':
        return 'bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200 dark:bg-green-900/50 dark:text-green-300 dark:border-green-500/30';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-200 dark:bg-red-900/50 dark:text-red-300 dark:border-red-500/30';
      default:
        return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 border-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600';
    }
  };

  const getStatusText = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'submitted':
        return 'Application Submitted';
      case 'interview_scheduled':
        return 'Interview Scheduled';
      case 'approved':
        return 'Approved';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'Rejected';
      default:
        return status;
    }
  };

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-medium border ${getStatusStyle(status)}`}>
      {getStatusText(status)}
    </span>
  );
};

export default StatusBadge;
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { SuccessToastProps } from '@/types';

const SuccessToast: React.FC<SuccessToastProps & { duration?: number }> = ({ 
  message, 
  isVisible, 
  onClose, 
  duration = 5000 
}) => {
  const [isAnimating, setIsAnimating] = useState(false);
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState(Math.floor(duration / 1000));
  const timerRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible) {
      setIsAnimating(true);
      setCountdown(Math.floor(duration / 1000));
      
      // Countdown timer
      const countdownInterval = setInterval(() => {
        setCountdown(prev => {
          if (prev <= 1) {
            clearInterval(countdownInterval);
            return 0;
          }
          return prev - 1;
        });
      }, 1000);
      
      // Auto close timer
      timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setIsAnimating(false);
        setTimeout(onClose, 300); // Wait for fade out animation
      }, duration);
      
      return () => {
        if(timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
        clearInterval(countdownInterval);
      };
    }
  }, [isVisible, onClose, duration]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const handleClose = () => {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    }
    setIsAnimating(false);
    setTimeout(onClose, 300);
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 pointer-events-none">
      {/* Backdrop with subtle blur */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/10 backdrop-blur-sm"></div>
      
      {/* Toast Container */}
      <div className={`
        relative bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700 
        max-w-md w-full mx-4 transform transition-all duration-500 ease-out
        ${isAnimating 
          ? 'scale-100 opacity-100 translate-y-0' 
          : 'scale-95 opacity-0 translate-y-4'
        }
      `}>
        {/* Success Header with gradient */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#003A70] to-[#004080] text-white p-6 rounded-t-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            {/* Animated Success Icon */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white/20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                <svg 
                  className="w-8 h-8 text-white animate-bounce" 
                  fill="none" 
                  stroke="currentColor" 
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                >
                  <path 
                    strokeLinecap="round" 
                    strokeLinejoin="round" 
                    strokeWidth={2.5} 
                    d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" 
                  />
                </svg>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Application Submitted!</h3>
              <p className="text-white/90 text-sm mt-1">Redirecting to your dashboard...</p>
            </div>
            {/* Countdown circle */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full border-2 border-white/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                <span className="text-white font-bold text-sm">{countdown}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Message Content */}
        <div className="p-6">
          <p className="text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 text-base leading-relaxed mb-4">
            {message}
          </p>
          
          {/* Success indicators */}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-green-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              Application received and being processed
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              You'll be redirected to your dashboard shortly
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
              <div className="w-2 h-2 bg-purple-500 rounded-full mr-3 animate-pulse"></div>
              Our team will review your application
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Bottom section with action */}
        <div className="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-gray-700/50 px-6 py-4 rounded-b-2xl border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-gray-700">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Application ID: #APP{Date.now().toString().slice(-6)}
            </p>
            <button
              onClick={handleClose}
              className="text-[#003A70] dark:text-blue-400 hover:text-[#002B54] dark:hover:text-blue-300 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors"
            >
              Close ({countdown}s)
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Decorative elements */}
        <div className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 w-6 h-6 bg-yellow-400 rounded-full animate-ping opacity-75"></div>
        <div className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 w-4 h-4 bg-yellow-500 rounded-full"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SuccessToast;
// src/app/components/Tabs.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { TabProps, TabsProps } from '@/types';

const Tab: React.FC<TabProps> = ({ label, active, onClick }) => {
  return (
    <button
      className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium rounded-t-lg focus:outline-none ${
        active
          ? 'bg-white text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600'
          : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800'
      }`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      {label}
    </button>
  );
};

const Tabs: React.FC<TabsProps> = ({ activeTab, onTabChange, tabs, children }) => {
  return (
    <div className="w-full">
      <div className="border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
        <nav className="-mb-px flex space-x-8" aria-label="Tabs">
          {tabs.map((tab) => (
            <Tab
              key={tab.value}
              label={tab.label}
              active={activeTab === tab.value}
              onClick={() => onTabChange(tab.value)}
            />
          ))}
        </nav>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Tabs;
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
import { Sun, Moon } from "lucide-react";

export function ThemeSwitcher() {
  const { setTheme, theme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => setTheme(theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light")}
      className="relative p-2 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors"
    >
      <Sun className="h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-0 scale-100 transition-all dark:-rotate-90 dark:scale-0" />
      <Moon className="absolute top-2 left-2 h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-90 scale-0 transition-all dark:rotate-0 dark:scale-100" />
      <span className="sr-only">Toggle theme</span>
    </button>
  );
}
// src/app/components/Toast.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ToastProps } from '@/types';

const Toast: React.FC<ToastProps> = ({ 
  message, 
  type, 
  isVisible, 
  onClose, 
  duration = 5000 
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isVisible && duration > 0) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        onClose();
      }, duration);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [isVisible, duration, onClose]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const getToastStyles = () => {
    const baseStyles = "fixed top-4 right-4 p-4 rounded-lg shadow-lg z-50 max-w-sm w-full transform transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out";
    
    switch (type) {
      case 'success':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-green-500 text-white`;
      case 'error':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-red-500 text-white`;
      case 'warning':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-yellow-500 text-white`;
      case 'info':
        return `${baseStyles} bg-blue-500 text-white`;
      default:
        return `${baseStyles} bg-gray-500 text-white`;
    }
  };

  const getIcon = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'success':
        return 'âœ“';
      case 'error':
        return 'âœ•';
      case 'warning':
        return 'âš ';
      case 'info':
        return 'â„¹';
      default:
        return '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={getToastStyles()} data-testid="toast">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center">
          <span className="text-lg mr-2">{getIcon()}</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">{message}</span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="ml-4 text-white hover:text-gray-200 focus:outline-none"
        >
          âœ•
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Toast;

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { Candidate, CandidateFiltersProps } from '@/types';
import SearchableFilterDropdown from './common/SearchableFilterDropdown';
import QuickFilterButtons from '@/app/components/common/QuickFilterButtons';

const CandidateFilters: React.FC<CandidateFiltersProps> = ({
  candidates,
  filterStatus,
  filterName,
  filterCity,
  filterCountry,
  filterNationality,
  filterJobTitle,
  filterCategory,
  filterSpecialty,
  filterAgeMin,
  filterAgeMax,
  onFilterStatusChange,
  onFilterNameChange,
  onFilterCityChange,
  onFilterCountryChange,
  onFilterNationalityChange,
  onFilterJobTitleChange,
  onFilterCategoryChange,
  onFilterSpecialtyChange,
  onFilterAgeMinChange,
  onFilterAgeMaxChange,
  onClearAllFilters
}) => {

  // Search states for interactive dropdowns
  const [citySearch, setCitySearch] = useState(filterCity);
  const [countrySearch, setCountrySearch] = useState(filterCountry);
  const [jobTitleSearch, setJobTitleSearch] = useState(filterJobTitle);
  const [categorySearch, setCategorySearch] = useState(filterCategory);
  const [nationalitySearch, setNationalitySearch] = useState(filterNationality);
  const [specialtySearch, setSpecialtySearch] = useState(filterSpecialty);

  // Update search states when filters change externally
  useEffect(() => {
    setCitySearch(filterCity);
  }, [filterCity]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCountrySearch(filterCountry);
  }, [filterCountry]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setJobTitleSearch(filterJobTitle);
  }, [filterJobTitle]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCategorySearch(filterCategory);
  }, [filterCategory]);
 
  useEffect(() => {
    setNationalitySearch(filterNationality);
  }, [filterNationality]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSpecialtySearch(filterSpecialty);
  }, [filterSpecialty]);

  // Extract unique values from candidates
  const uniqueCities = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.city).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueCountries = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.country).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueJobTitles = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.jobTitleText).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueCategories = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.jobCategory).filter((c): c is string => !!c))).sort();
  const uniqueNationalities = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.nationality).filter((n): n is string => !!n))).sort();
  const uniqueSpecialties = Array.from(new Set(candidates.map(c => c.specialty).filter((s): s is string => !!s))).sort();

  const handleClearAllFilters = () => {
    setCitySearch('');
    setCountrySearch('');
    setJobTitleSearch('');
    setCategorySearch('');
    setNationalitySearch('');
    setSpecialtySearch('');
    onClearAllFilters();
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700 mb-6">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200 mb-4">Filters:</h3>
      
      {/* Main Filter Inputs */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4 mb-6">
        {/* Name Filter */}
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Filter by Name</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Filter by name..."
            value={filterName}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterNameChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        {/* City Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by City"
          placeholder="Search and select city..."
          filterValue={filterCity}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCityChange}
          searchValue={citySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCitySearch}
          options={uniqueCities}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="city"
        />
        
        {/* Country Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Country"
          placeholder="Search and select country..."
          filterValue={filterCountry}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCountryChange}
          searchValue={countrySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCountrySearch}
          options={uniqueCountries}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="country"
        />
        
        {/* Nationality Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Nationality"
          placeholder="Search and select nationality..."
          filterValue={filterNationality}
          onFilterChange={onFilterNationalityChange}
          searchValue={nationalitySearch}
          onSearchChange={setNationalitySearch}
          options={uniqueNationalities}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="nationality"
        />
        
        {/* Category Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Category"
          placeholder="Search and select category..."
          filterValue={filterCategory}
          onFilterChange={onFilterCategoryChange}
          searchValue={categorySearch}
          onSearchChange={setCategorySearch}
          options={uniqueCategories}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="jobCategory"
        />

        {/* Specialty Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Specialty"
          placeholder="Search and select specialty..."
          filterValue={filterSpecialty}
          onFilterChange={onFilterSpecialtyChange}
          searchValue={specialtySearch}
          onSearchChange={setSpecialtySearch}
          options={uniqueSpecialties}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="specialty"
        />

        {/* Job Title Filter with Dropdown */}
        <SearchableFilterDropdown
          label="Filter by Job Title"
          placeholder="Search and select job title..."
          filterValue={filterJobTitle}
          onFilterChange={onFilterJobTitleChange}
          searchValue={jobTitleSearch}
          onSearchChange={setJobTitleSearch}
          options={uniqueJobTitles}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="jobTitleText"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Age and Status Filters */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-6">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Min Age</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            placeholder="Min age..."
            value={filterAgeMin}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterAgeMinChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Max Age</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            placeholder="Max age..."
            value={filterAgeMax}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterAgeMaxChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          />
        </div>
        
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="status-filter" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mb-1">Filter by Status</label>
          <select
            id="status-filter"
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => onFilterStatusChange(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 bg-white dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200"
          >
            <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
            <option value="submitted">Submitted</option>
            <option value="interview_scheduled">Interview Scheduled</option>
            <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>
            <option value="approved">Approved</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Clear All Filters Button */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <button
          onClick={handleClearAllFilters}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-600 dark:bg-gray-700 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700 dark:hover:bg-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-gray-500 transition-colors"
        >
          Clear All Filters
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Quick Filters */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <QuickFilterButtons
          label="Status"
          options={['all', 'submitted', 'interview_scheduled', 'rejected', 'approved']}
          filterValue={filterStatus}
          onFilterChange={onFilterStatusChange}
          candidates={candidates}
          filterKey="status"
          colorClass="gray"
        />

        {candidates.length > 0 && uniqueSpecialties.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Specialties"
            options={uniqueSpecialties}
            filterValue={filterSpecialty}
            onFilterChange={onFilterSpecialtyChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="specialty"
            colorClass="emerald"
            sliceLimit={8}
          />
        )}
        
        {candidates.length > 0 && uniqueNationalities.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Nationalities"
            options={uniqueNationalities}
            filterValue={filterNationality}
            onFilterChange={onFilterNationalityChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="nationality"
            colorClass="pink"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Cities"
            options={uniqueCities}
            filterValue={filterCity}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCityChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="city"
            colorClass="indigo"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Popular Countries"
            options={uniqueCountries}
            filterValue={filterCountry}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCountryChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="country"
            colorClass="purple"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Job Categories"
            options={uniqueCategories}
            filterValue={filterCategory}
            onFilterChange={onFilterCategoryChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="jobCategory"
            colorClass="orange"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}

        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <QuickFilterButtons
            label="Job Positions"
            options={uniqueJobTitles}
            filterValue={filterJobTitle}
            onFilterChange={onFilterJobTitleChange}
            candidates={candidates}
            filterKey="jobTitleText"
            colorClass="teal"
            sliceLimit={5}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CandidateFilters;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useProfileModal } from '@/hooks/useProfileModal';
import type { CandidateProfileModalProps } from '@/types';
import EditCandidateModal from './EditCandidateModal';
import ProfileHeader from './profile-modal/ProfileHeader';
import ProfileModalContent from './profile-modal/ProfileModalContent';

const CandidateProfileModal: React.FC<CandidateProfileModalProps> = ({
    isOpen,
    onClose,
    candidate,
    onStatusChange,
    onCancelInterview,
    statusUpdateLoading,
    onScheduleInterview,
    onEditCandidate,
}) => {
    const {
        isEditModalOpen,
        setIsEditModalOpen,
        isUpdating,
        handleEditCandidate,
        calculateAge,
    } = useProfileModal(candidate, onEditCandidate);

    if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

    const age = candidate.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(candidate.dateOfBirth) : null;

    return (
        <>
            <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 flex justify-center items-center z-50 p-4">
                <div className="bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-5xl w-full max-h-[95vh] flex flex-col">
                    <ProfileHeader
                        candidate={candidate}
                        onClose={onClose}
                        onEdit={() => setIsEditModalOpen(true)}
                        isUpdating={isUpdating}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                    />
                    <ProfileModalContent
                        candidate={candidate}
                        age={age}
                        onStatusChange={onStatusChange}
                        onCancelInterview={onCancelInterview}
                        statusUpdateLoading={statusUpdateLoading}
                        onScheduleInterview={onScheduleInterview}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
            <EditCandidateModal
                isOpen={isEditModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsEditModalOpen(false)}
                candidate={candidate}
                onSave={handleEditCandidate}
                isLoading={isUpdating}
            />
        </>
    );
};

export default CandidateProfileModal;
"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useCandidateTable } from '@/hooks/useCandidateTable';
import type { CandidateTableProps } from '@/types';
import CandidateTableHeader from './candidate-table/CandidateTableHeader';
import CandidateTableRow from './candidate-table/CandidateTableRow';

const CandidateTable: React.FC<CandidateTableProps> = ({
  candidates,
  statusUpdateLoading,
  onViewCandidate,
  onScoreCandidate,
  onStatusChange,
  onEditCandidate,
  filters
}) => {
  const {
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
    handleSort,
    sortedCandidates,
    splitName,
    calculateAge,
  } = useCandidateTable(candidates, filters);

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="min-w-full divide-y divide-gray-200 dark:divide-gray-700">
        <CandidateTableHeader
          sortField={sortField}
          sortDirection={sortDirection}
          handleSort={handleSort}
        />
        <tbody className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 divide-y divide-gray-200 dark:divide-gray-700">
          {sortedCandidates.length > 0 ? (
            sortedCandidates.map((candidate) => (
              <CandidateTableRow
                key={candidate.id}
                candidate={candidate}
                splitName={splitName}
                calculateAge={calculateAge}
                onViewCandidate={onViewCandidate}
                onScoreCandidate={onScoreCandidate}
                onEditCandidate={onEditCandidate}
              />
            ))
          ) : (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={8} className="text-center py-12">
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                  <svg className="w-16 h-16 text-gray-300 dark:text-gray-600" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
                    <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={1.5} d="M12 8v4l3 3m6-3a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
                  </svg>
                  <h3 className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">No candidates found</h3>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
                    No candidates match your current filters. Try adjusting your search.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          )}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CandidateTable;

// src/app/components/DashboardCharts.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  PieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
  Tooltip as RechartsTooltip,
  Legend,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid
} from 'recharts';
import { DashboardChartsProps } from '@/types';

const STATUS_COLORS = ['#3B82F6', '#10B981', '#EF4444']; // Blue, Green, Red
const SCORE_COLORS = ['#EF4444', '#F59E0B', '#FCD34D', '#A7F3D0', '#10B981']; // Red to Green gradient

const DashboardCharts: React.FC<DashboardChartsProps> = ({
  statusChartData,
  scoreDistributionData
}) => {
  const { theme } = useTheme();
  const isDarkMode = theme === 'dark';

  const tickColor = isDarkMode ? '#9CA3AF' : '#64748b'; // gray-400 dark, gray-500 light
  const tooltipBackgroundColor = isDarkMode ? '#1F2937' : '#f8fafc'; // gray-800 dark, slate-50 light
  const tooltipBorderColor = isDarkMode ? '#374151' : '#e2e8f0'; // gray-700 dark, slate-200 light
  const legendColor = isDarkMode ? '#D1D5DB' : '#374151'; // gray-300 dark, gray-700 light
  return (
    <section className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
      {/* Status Distribution Pie Chart */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">Candidate Status Distribution</h3>
          <div className="bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-900/50 p-2 rounded-lg">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M3 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clipRule="evenodd" />
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
        {statusChartData.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="relative">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={350}>
              <PieChart>
                <Pie 
                  data={statusChartData} 
                  cx="50%" 
                  cy="50%" 
                  labelLine={false} 
                  outerRadius={120} 
                  innerRadius={60}
                  fill="#8884d8" 
                  dataKey="value" 
                  label={{ fill: legendColor }}
                >
                  {statusChartData.map((entry, index) => (
                    <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={STATUS_COLORS[index % STATUS_COLORS.length]} />
                  ))}
                </Pie>
                <RechartsTooltip 
                  contentStyle={{ 
                    backgroundColor: tooltipBackgroundColor, 
                    border: `1px solid ${tooltipBorderColor}`, 
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                  }} 
                />
                <Legend 
                  wrapperStyle={{ paddingTop: '20px', color: legendColor }}
                  iconType="circle"
                />
              </PieChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <svg className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 opacity-50" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path fillRule="evenodd" d="M3 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1zm0 4a1 1 0 011-1h12a1 1 0 110 2H4a1 1 0 01-1-1z" clipRule="evenodd" />
            </svg>
            <p className="text-lg font-medium">No candidate data available</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Status distribution will appear here once candidates are added</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Score Distribution Bar Chart */}
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800 dark:text-gray-200">AI Score Distribution</h3>
          <div className="bg-green-50 dark:bg-green-900/50 p-2 rounded-lg">
            <svg className="w-5 h-5 text-green-600 dark:text-green-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path d="M9.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.07 3.292a1 1 0 00.95.69h3.462c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-2.8 2.034a1 1 0 00-.364 1.118l1.07 3.292c.3.921-.755 1.688-1.54 1.118l-2.8-2.034a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-2.8 2.034c-.784.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.07-3.292a1 1 0 00-.364-1.118L2.98 8.72c-.783-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h3.461a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.07-3.292z" />
            </svg>
          </div>
        </div>
        {scoreDistributionData.some(d => d.count > 0) ? (
          <div className="relative">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={350}>
              <BarChart data={scoreDistributionData} margin={{ top: 20, right: 30, left: 20, bottom: 5 }}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={isDarkMode ? '#374151' : '#f1f5f9'} />
                <XAxis 
                  dataKey="name" 
                  stroke={tickColor}
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis 
                  stroke={tickColor}
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={false}
                  allowDecimals={false}
                />
                <RechartsTooltip 
                  contentStyle={{ 
                    backgroundColor: tooltipBackgroundColor, 
                    border: `1px solid ${tooltipBorderColor}`, 
                    borderRadius: '8px',
                  }} 
                  labelStyle={{ color: legendColor }}
                />
                <Bar 
                  dataKey="count" 
                  fill="#10B981"
                  radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                />
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <svg className="w-16 h-16 mb-4 opacity-50" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
              <path d="M9.049 2.927c.3-.921 1.603-.921 1.902 0l1.07 3.292a1 1 0 00.95.69h3.462c.969 0 1.371 1.24.588 1.81l-2.8 2.034a1 1 0 00-.364 1.118l1.07 3.292c.3.921-.755 1.688-1.54 1.118l-2.8-2.034a1 1 0 00-1.175 0l-2.8 2.034c-.784.57-1.838-.197-1.539-1.118l1.07-3.292a1 1 0 00-.364-1.118L2.98 8.72c-.783-.57-.38-1.81.588-1.81h3.461a1 1 0 00.951-.69l1.07-3.292z" />
            </svg>
            <p className="text-lg font-medium">No scoring data available</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Score distribution will appear here once candidates are scored</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

"use client";

import React from 'react';
import { useEditCandidateForm } from '@/hooks/useEditCandidateForm';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';
import EditModalHeader from './edit-candidate-modal/EditModalHeader';
import EditModalFooter from './edit-candidate-modal/EditModalFooter';
import EditCandidateForm from './edit-candidate-modal/EditCandidateForm';

interface EditCandidateModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  candidate: Candidate | null;
  onSave: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

const EditCandidateModal: React.FC<EditCandidateModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  candidate,
  onSave,
  isLoading
}) => {
  const {
    formData,
    errors,
    jobCategories,
    loadingJobs,
    availableSpecialties,
    filteredJobs,
    handleInputChange,
    handleCategoryChange,
    handleJobChange,
    handleSubmit,
  } = useEditCandidateForm(candidate, isOpen, onSave, onClose);

  if (!isOpen || !candidate) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-60 flex justify-center items-center z-50 p-4">
      <div className="bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[95vh] flex flex-col">
        <EditModalHeader onClose={onClose} isLoading={isLoading} />
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-grow">
          <EditCandidateForm
            formData={formData}
            errors={errors}
            jobCategories={jobCategories}
            loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
            availableSpecialties={availableSpecialties}
            filteredJobs={filteredJobs}
            handleInputChange={handleInputChange}
            handleCategoryChange={handleCategoryChange}
            handleJobChange={handleJobChange}
            handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
            isLoading={isLoading}
          />
        </div>
        <EditModalFooter
          onClose={onClose}
          handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
          isLoading={isLoading}
          loadingJobs={loadingJobs}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
