"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { ThemeProvider as NextThemesProvider } from "next-themes";

type ThemeProviderProps = React.ComponentProps<typeof NextThemesProvider>;

export function ThemeProvider({ children, ...props }: ThemeProviderProps) {
  return <NextThemesProvider {...props}>{children}</NextThemesProvider>;
}
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useContext, createContext, ReactNode } from 'react';
// import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // Removed as it's unused here
import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { 
  onAuthStateChanged, 
  signOut as firebaseSignOut, 
  signInWithEmailAndPassword, 
  User 
} from "firebase/auth";
import { AuthContextType } from '@/types';

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // const router = useRouter(); // Removed as it's unused here

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
      setUser(user);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  const signIn = async (email: string, password: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      // Redirect is handled by the component using the hook based on user state
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Firebase Sign In Error:", err);
      // Type assertion for error object
      if (err instanceof Error) {
          setError(err.message || "Failed to sign in");
      } else {
          setError("Failed to sign in: An unknown error occurred");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const signOut = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await firebaseSignOut(auth);
      setUser(null);
      // Redirect is handled where signOut is called, e.g., in the dashboard component
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Firebase Sign Out Error:", err);
      // Type assertion for error object
      if (err instanceof Error) {
          setError(err.message || "Failed to sign out");
      } else {
          setError("Failed to sign out: An unknown error occurred");
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const value = {
    user,
    loading,
    error,
    signIn,
    signOut,
  };

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  }
  return context;
};

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

export const useCandidateDashboard = () => {
    const [candidateEmail, setCandidateEmail] = useState('');
    const [applications, setApplications] = useState<Candidate[]>([]);
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const router = useRouter();

    useEffect(() => {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const email = urlParams.get('email');
        if (email) {
            setCandidateEmail(email);
            loadApplications(email);
        } else {
            router.push('/');
        }
    }, [router]);

    const loadApplications = async (email: string) => {
        setIsLoading(true);
        setError(null);
        
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/get-candidate-applications', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
            });

            const result = await response.json();
            
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to load applications');
            }

            setApplications(result.applications || []);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'An unexpected error occurred');
        } finally {
            setIsLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleNewApplication = () => {
        router.push(`/candidate-form?email=${encodeURIComponent(candidateEmail)}`);
    };

    const formatDate = (timestamp: any) => {
        if (!timestamp) return 'N/A';
        
        let date: Date;
        if (timestamp && typeof timestamp.toDate === 'function') {
            date = timestamp.toDate();
        } else if (timestamp instanceof Date) {
            date = timestamp;
        } else if (typeof timestamp === 'string') {
            date = new Date(timestamp);
        } else {
            return 'N/A';
        }
        
        if (isNaN(date.getTime())) {
            return 'N/A';
        }
        
        return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
            year: 'numeric',
            month: 'long',
            day: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        });
    };

    return {
        candidateEmail,
        applications,
        isLoading,
        error,
        handleNewApplication,
        formatDate,
        router
    };
};
// src/hooks/useCandidateForm.ts
import { useState, useEffect, FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { collection, addDoc, serverTimestamp, query, where, getDocs, Timestamp } from "firebase/firestore";
import { ref, uploadBytes, getDownloadURL } from "firebase/storage";
import { db, storage } from '@/lib/firebase';

export const useCandidateForm = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  const [candidateEmail, setCandidateEmail] = useState('');
  const [isExistingCandidate, setIsExistingCandidate] = useState(false);
  
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState('');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState('');
  const [gender, setGender] = useState('');
  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState('+358');
  const [phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber] = useState('');
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState('');
  const [city, setCity] = useState('');
  const [country, setCountry] = useState('');
  const [nationality, setNationality] = useState('');
  const [doubleNationality, setDoubleNationality] = useState('');
  const [jobTitle, setJobTitle] = useState('');
  const [specialty, setSpecialty] = useState('');
  const [specialtySearch, setSpecialtySearch] = useState('');
  const [websites, setWebsites] = useState<string[]>(['']);
  const [additionalInfo, setAdditionalInfo] = useState<{type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text', content: string, file?: File}[]>([{type: 'link', content: ''}]);
  const [cvFile, setCvFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [passportFile, setPassportFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [photoFile, setPhotoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('');
  const [selectedJob, setSelectedJob] = useState<any>(null);
  const [availableSpecialties, setAvailableSpecialties] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showSuccessToast, setShowSuccessToast] = useState(false);

  const filteredJobs = jobs.filter(job => job.category === selectedCategory);
  const [phoneValidation, setPhoneValidation] = useState<{
    isValid: boolean | null;
    isValidating: boolean;
    message: string;
    warning?: string;
  }>({
    isValid: null,
    isValidating: false,
    message: ''
  });

  const validatePhoneNumber = async (countryCode: string, phoneNumber: string): Promise<boolean> => {
    // ðŸ”¥ CRITICAL: Skip validation entirely for existing candidates
    if (isExistingCandidate) {
      console.log('ðŸ“ž Skipping phone validation for existing candidate');
      setPhoneValidation({
        isValid: true, // Assume valid since it was previously accepted
        isValidating: false,
        message: 'âœ… Phone number from previous application (validated)',
        warning: 'Using phone number from your previous application'
      });
      return true;
    }

    if (!phoneNumber.trim()) {
      setPhoneValidation({
        isValid: false,
        isValidating: false,
        message: 'Phone number is required'
      });
      return false;
    }

    setPhoneValidation({
      isValid: null,
      isValidating: true,
      message: 'Validating phone number...'
    });

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/validate-phone', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          countryCode,
          phoneNumber: phoneNumber.trim()
        }),
      });

      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.error || 'Validation failed');
      }

      const validationMessage = result.message || (result.isValid 
        ? 'âœ… Phone number is valid'
        : `âŒ ${result.error || 'Invalid phone number format'}`);

      setPhoneValidation({
        isValid: result.isValid,
        isValidating: false,
        message: validationMessage,
        warning: result.warning || result.suggestion
      });

      // Log validation details for debugging
      if (result.isValid && result.country) {
        console.log(`ðŸ“ž Phone validated for ${result.country.name} (${result.type || 'unknown type'})`);
        if (result.carrier) {
          console.log(`ðŸ“ž Carrier: ${result.carrier}`);
        }
      }

      return result.isValid;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('âŒ Phone validation error:', error);
      
      // Fallback validation - just check basic format
      const basicValidation = /^\d{6,15}$/.test(phoneNumber.replace(/\D/g, ''));
      
      setPhoneValidation({
        isValid: basicValidation,
        isValidating: false,
        message: basicValidation 
          ? 'âš ï¸ Phone format appears valid (validation service unavailable)'
          : 'âŒ Phone number format appears invalid',
        warning: 'Validation service temporarily unavailable'
      });

      return basicValidation;
    }
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Single useEffect with proper debouncing
  useEffect(() => {
    console.log(`ðŸ“ž Phone validation useEffect triggered: phone="${phoneNumber}", country="${countryCode}", existing=${isExistingCandidate}`);
    
    // Skip validation entirely for existing candidates
    if (isExistingCandidate) {
      if (phoneNumber.trim()) {
        setPhoneValidation({
          isValid: true,
          isValidating: false,
          message: 'âœ… Phone number from previous application (validated)',
          warning: 'Using phone number from your previous application'
        });
      } else {
        setPhoneValidation({
          isValid: null,
          isValidating: false,
          message: ''
        });
      }
      return;
    }

    // Clear validation state if phone is empty
    if (!phoneNumber.trim()) {
      setPhoneValidation({
        isValid: null,
        isValidating: false,
        message: ''
      });
      return;
    }

    // Only validate for new candidates with valid input
    if (phoneNumber.trim() && countryCode && phoneNumber.trim().length >= 6) {
      console.log(`ðŸ“ž Setting up debounced validation for: ${countryCode}${phoneNumber}`);
      
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        console.log(`ðŸ“ž Executing debounced validation for: ${countryCode}${phoneNumber}`);
        validatePhoneNumber(countryCode, phoneNumber);
      }, 2000); // ðŸ”¥ INCREASED: 2 seconds debounce to prevent excessive calls

      return () => {
        console.log(`ðŸ“ž Clearing timeout for: ${countryCode}${phoneNumber}`);
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      };
    }
  }, [phoneNumber, countryCode, isExistingCandidate]); // Only these dependencies

  const sendConfirmationEmail = async (candidateData: {
    email: string;
    fullName: string;
    jobTitle: string;
    jobCategory?: string;
    specialty?: string;
    applicationId: string;
  }) => {
    try {
      console.log('ðŸ“§ Attempting to send confirmation email...', candidateData);
      
      const response = await fetch('/api/send-confirmation-email', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          candidateEmail: candidateData.email,
          candidateName: candidateData.fullName,
          jobTitle: candidateData.jobTitle,
          jobCategory: candidateData.jobCategory,
          specialty: candidateData.specialty,
          applicationId: candidateData.applicationId
        }),
      });

      const result = await response.json();
      
      if (!result.success) {
        console.error('âŒ Failed to send confirmation email:', result.error);
        // Don't throw error - we don't want to fail the entire submission if email fails
      } else {
        console.log('âœ… Confirmation email sent successfully');
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('âŒ Error sending confirmation email:', error);
      // Don't throw error - we don't want to fail the entire submission if email fails
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const email = urlParams.get('email');
    if (email) {
      setCandidateEmail(email);
      loadExistingCandidateData(email);
    } else {
      router.push('/');
    }
  }, [router]);

  const loadExistingCandidateData = async (email: string) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/get-candidate-applications', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
      });

      const result = await response.json();
    
      if (response.ok && result.applications && result.applications.length > 0) {
        const latestApplication = result.applications[0];
        
        setIsExistingCandidate(true);
        
        if (latestApplication.firstName && latestApplication.lastName) {
          setFirstName(latestApplication.firstName);
          setLastName(latestApplication.lastName);
        } else if (latestApplication.fullName) {
          const nameParts = latestApplication.fullName.trim().split(' ');
          setFirstName(nameParts[0] || '');
          setLastName(nameParts.slice(1).join(' ') || '');
        }
        
        setGender(latestApplication.gender || '');
        setDateOfBirth(latestApplication.dateOfBirth || '');
        
        if (latestApplication.countryCode) {
          setCountryCode(latestApplication.countryCode);
        }
        
        if (latestApplication.phoneNumber) {
          let cleanPhone = latestApplication.phoneNumber;
          if (latestApplication.countryCode && cleanPhone.includes(latestApplication.countryCode)) {
            cleanPhone = cleanPhone.replace(latestApplication.countryCode, '').trim();
          }
          cleanPhone = cleanPhone.replace(/^[\s\-+]+/, '');
          setPhoneNumber(cleanPhone);
        }
        
        setCity(latestApplication.city || '');
        setCountry(latestApplication.country || '');
        setNationality(latestApplication.nationality || '');
        setDoubleNationality(latestApplication.doubleNationality || '');
        
        if (latestApplication.websites && Array.isArray(latestApplication.websites) && latestApplication.websites.length > 0) {
          const validWebsites = latestApplication.websites.filter((w: string) => w && w.trim() !== '');
          setWebsites(validWebsites.length > 0 ? [...validWebsites, ''] : ['']);
        } else {
          setWebsites(['']);
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error loading existing candidate data:', error);
    }
  };

  const addAdditionalInfo = (type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text') => {
    setAdditionalInfo([...additionalInfo, {type, content: ''}]);
  };

  const removeAdditionalInfo = (index: number) => {
    if (additionalInfo.length > 1) {
      setAdditionalInfo(additionalInfo.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
    }
  };

  const updateAdditionalInfo = (index: number, content: string, file?: File) => {
    const newInfo = [...additionalInfo];
    newInfo[index] = {...newInfo[index], content, file};
    setAdditionalInfo(newInfo);
  };

  const addWebsite = () => {
    setWebsites([...websites, '']);
  };

  const removeWebsite = (index: number) => {
    if (websites.length > 1) {
      setWebsites(websites.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
    }
  };

  const updateWebsite = (index: number, value: string) => {
    const newWebsites = [...websites];
    newWebsites[index] = value;
    setWebsites(newWebsites);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedCategory) {
      const filtered = jobs.filter(job => job.category === selectedCategory);
      setSpecialty('');
      setAvailableSpecialties([]);
    }
    setJobTitle('');
    setSelectedJob(null);
  }, [selectedCategory, jobs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadJobs = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/jobs?activeOnly=true');
        const data = await response.json();
        if (response.ok) {
          setJobs(data.jobs || []);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error loading jobs:', error);
      }
    };
    loadJobs();
  }, []);

useEffect(() => {
  if (selectedJob && selectedJob.specialties && Array.isArray(selectedJob.specialties)) {
    setAvailableSpecialties(selectedJob.specialties);
    // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Don't automatically clear specialty if it's already valid
    if (!selectedJob.specialties.includes(specialty)) {
      setSpecialty('');
      setSpecialtySearch('');
    }
  } else {
    setAvailableSpecialties([]);
    setSpecialty('');
    setSpecialtySearch('');
  }
}, [selectedJob]);

  const handleCategorySelection = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    setSelectedCategory(e.target.value);
  };

  const handleJobSelection = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
    const selectedJobId = e.target.value;
    setJobTitle(selectedJobId);
    const job = filteredJobs.find(j => j.id === selectedJobId);
    setSelectedJob(job || null);
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const { name, files } = e.target;
    if (files && files.length > 0) {
      if (name === 'cv') {
        setCvFile(files[0]);
      } else if (name === 'passport') {
        setPassportFile(files[0]);
      } else if (name === 'photo') {
        setPhotoFile(files[0]);
      }
    }
  };

  const checkDuplicateApplication = async (email: string, jobId: string, jobCategory: string) => {
    try {
      const candidatesRef = collection(db, "candidates");
      
      const sixMonthsAgo = new Date();
      sixMonthsAgo.setMonth(sixMonthsAgo.getMonth() - 6);
      
      const duplicateQuery = query(
        candidatesRef,
        where("email", "==", email.toLowerCase().trim()),
        where("jobTitle", "==", jobId),
        where("submittedAt", ">=", Timestamp.fromDate(sixMonthsAgo))
      );
      
      const duplicateSnapshot = await getDocs(duplicateQuery);
      
      if (!duplicateSnapshot.empty) {
        const lastApplication = duplicateSnapshot.docs[0].data();
        const lastSubmissionDate = lastApplication.submittedAt?.toDate();
        const formattedDate = lastSubmissionDate?.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
          year: 'numeric',
          month: 'long',
          day: 'numeric'
        });
        
        return {
          isDuplicate: true,
          message: `You have already applied for this position on ${formattedDate}. You can apply again after 6 months.`,
        };
      }
      
      const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date();
      thirtyDaysAgo.setDate(thirtyDaysAgo.getDate() - 30);
      
      const categoryQuery = query(
        candidatesRef,
        where("email", "==", email.toLowerCase().trim()),
        where("jobCategory", "==", jobCategory),
        where("submittedAt", ">=", Timestamp.fromDate(thirtyDaysAgo))
      );
      
      const categorySnapshot = await getDocs(categoryQuery);
      
      if (categorySnapshot.size >= 3) {
        return {
          isDuplicate: true,
          message: `You have reached the maximum of 3 applications for the "${jobCategory}" category this month.`,
        };
      }
      
      return { isDuplicate: false, message: '' };
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error checking duplicate applications:', error);
      return { isDuplicate: false, message: '' };
    }
  };

  const calculateAge = (dateOfBirth: string): number => {
    if (!dateOfBirth) return 0;
    const today = new Date();
    const birthDate = new Date(dateOfBirth);
    if (isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) return 0;
    let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
    const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();
    if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
      age--;
    }
    return age;
  };

  
  const validateAge = (dateOfBirth: string): { isValid: boolean; age: number; message?: string } => {
    if (!dateOfBirth) {
      return { isValid: false, age: 0, message: 'Date of birth is required' };
    }
    const age = calculateAge(dateOfBirth);
    if (age < 18) {
      return { 
        isValid: false, 
        age, 
        message: `You must be at least 18. Your age is ${age}.` 
      };
    }
    return { isValid: true, age, message: '' };
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  setError(null);
  setSuccess(null);

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Name validation before other checks
  const validateName = (name: string, fieldName: string): string => {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      return `${fieldName} is required`;
    }
    
    if (/\d/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain numbers`;
    }
    
    if (!/^[a-zA-ZÃ€-Ã¿Ä€-Å¾Ð-Ñ\s'-]+$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} can only contain letters, spaces, hyphens, and apostrophes`;
    }
    
    if (/\./.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain dots`;
    }
    
    if (name.trim().length < 2) {
      return `${fieldName} must be at least 2 characters long`;
    }
    
    if (name.trim().length > 50) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot exceed 50 characters`;
    }
    
    if (/\s{2,}/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot contain multiple consecutive spaces`;
    }
    
    if (/^[\s'-]|[\s'-]$/.test(name)) {
      return `${fieldName} cannot start or end with spaces, hyphens, or apostrophes`;
    }
    
    return '';
  };

  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Validate names first
  const firstNameError = validateName(firstName, 'First name');
  if (firstNameError) {
    setError(firstNameError);
    return;
  }

  const lastNameError = validateName(lastName, 'Last name');
  if (lastNameError) {
    setError(lastNameError);
    return;
  }

  // Age validation
  const ageValidation = validateAge(dateOfBirth);
  if (!ageValidation.isValid) {
    setError(ageValidation.message || 'Age validation failed.');
    return;
  }

  // Phone validation only for new candidates
  if (!isExistingCandidate) {
    console.log('ðŸ“ž Starting phone validation before submission for new candidate...');
    const isPhoneValid = await validatePhoneNumber(countryCode, phoneNumber);
    
    if (!isPhoneValid) {
      setError(`Phone number validation failed: ${phoneValidation.message}`);
      return;
    }
  } else {
    console.log('ðŸ“ž Skipping phone validation for existing candidate');
  }

  // Enhanced validation with specific missing field messages
  const missingFields = [];
  
  if (!firstName) missingFields.push('First Name');
  if (!lastName) missingFields.push('Last Name');
  if (!candidateEmail) missingFields.push('Email');
  if (!gender) missingFields.push('Gender');
  if (!countryCode) missingFields.push('Country Code');
  if (!phoneNumber) missingFields.push('Phone Number');
  if (!dateOfBirth) missingFields.push('Date of Birth');
  if (!city) missingFields.push('City');
  if (!country) missingFields.push('Country');
  if (!nationality) missingFields.push('Nationality');
  if (!selectedCategory) missingFields.push('Job Category');
  if (!jobTitle) missingFields.push('Job Position');
  if (!specialty) missingFields.push('Specialty');
  if (!cvFile) missingFields.push('CV/Resume');
  if (!passportFile) missingFields.push('Passport/ID');

  // Debug log
  console.log('ðŸ” Form validation debug:', {
    firstName,
    lastName,
    firstNameValid: !firstNameError,
    lastNameValid: !lastNameError,
    candidateEmail,
    gender,
    countryCode,
    phoneNumber,
    phoneValidation,
    dateOfBirth,
    city,
    country,
    nationality,
    selectedCategory,
    jobTitle,
    specialty,
    cvFile: cvFile ? cvFile.name : 'Missing',
    passportFile: passportFile ? passportFile.name : 'Missing',
    availableSpecialties,
    specialtyValid: availableSpecialties.includes(specialty),
    isExistingCandidate
  });

  if (missingFields.length > 0) {
    setError(`Missing required fields: ${missingFields.join(', ')}`);
    return;
  }

  // Specialty validation
  if (!availableSpecialties.includes(specialty)) {
    setError(`Please select a valid specialty from the available options. Selected: "${specialty}". Available: ${availableSpecialties.join(', ')}`);
    return;
  }

  // File validation
  if (!cvFile || !passportFile) {
    setError('CV/Resume and Passport/ID files are required.');
    return;
  }

  setIsLoading(true);

  try {
    // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Clean and trim names before saving
    const cleanFirstName = firstName.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
    const cleanLastName = lastName.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

    // Check for duplicate applications
    const duplicateCheck = await checkDuplicateApplication(candidateEmail, jobTitle, selectedCategory);
    if (duplicateCheck.isDuplicate) {
      setError(duplicateCheck.message);
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    // File uploads
    const cvFileName = `cv_${Date.now()}_${cvFile.name}`;
    const passportFileName = `passport_${Date.now()}_${passportFile.name}`;
    
    const cvStorageRef = ref(storage, `candidate_files/${cvFileName}`);
    const passportStorageRef = ref(storage, `candidate_files/${passportFileName}`);

    const [cvUploadTask, passportUploadTask] = await Promise.all([
      uploadBytes(cvStorageRef, cvFile),
      uploadBytes(passportStorageRef, passportFile)
    ]);

    const [cvUrl, passportUrl] = await Promise.all([
      getDownloadURL(cvUploadTask.ref),
      getDownloadURL(passportUploadTask.ref)
    ]);
    
    let photoUrl = null;
    if (photoFile) {
      const photoFileName = `photo_${Date.now()}_${photoFile.name}`;
      const photoStorageRef = ref(storage, `candidate_files/${photoFileName}`);
      const photoUploadTask = await uploadBytes(photoStorageRef, photoFile);
      photoUrl = await getDownloadURL(photoUploadTask.ref);
    }

    const normalizedEmail = candidateEmail.toLowerCase().trim();

    // Save application to database with cleaned names
    const docRef = await addDoc(collection(db, "candidates"), {
      firstName: cleanFirstName, // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Use cleaned name
      lastName: cleanLastName,   // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Use cleaned name
      fullName: `${cleanFirstName} ${cleanLastName}`, // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Use cleaned names
      email: normalizedEmail,
      gender,
      countryCode,
      phoneNumber: `${countryCode} ${phoneNumber}`,
      dateOfBirth,
      age: ageValidation.age,
      city,
      country,
      nationality,
      doubleNationality: doubleNationality || null,
      jobCategory: selectedCategory,
      specialty,
      jobTitle,
      jobTitleText: selectedJob?.title || '',
      websites: websites.filter(w => w.trim() !== ''),
      cvUrl,
      passportUrl,
      photoUrl,
      submittedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      status: 'submitted',
      score: null,
      applicationSource: 'web_form',
      lastUpdated: serverTimestamp(),
      phoneValidationStatus: {
        isValid: phoneValidation.isValid,
        validatedAt: new Date(),
        fallbackUsed: phoneValidation.warning?.includes('pattern') ? 'regex' : 
                     phoneValidation.warning?.includes('basic') ? 'basic' : 'api'
      }
    });

    // Send confirmation email after successful submission
    if (docRef && docRef.id) {
      console.log('âœ… Application saved successfully, sending confirmation email...');
      
      await sendConfirmationEmail({
        email: normalizedEmail,
        fullName: `${cleanFirstName} ${cleanLastName}`, // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Use cleaned names
        jobTitle: selectedJob?.title || '',
        jobCategory: selectedCategory,
        specialty: specialty,
        applicationId: docRef.id
      });
    }

    setSuccess("Application submitted successfully! Your information has been validated and a confirmation email has been sent to your email address.");
    setShowSuccessToast(true);

    setTimeout(() => {
      router.push(`/candidate-dashboard?email=${encodeURIComponent(normalizedEmail)}`);
    }, 3000);

  } catch (err) {
    console.error('âŒ Submission error:', err);
    setError(err instanceof Error ? `Submission failed: ${err.message}` : 'An unknown error occurred');
  } finally {
    setIsLoading(false);
  }
};
  // ðŸ”¥ NEW: Return phone validation state
  return {
    firstName, setFirstName,
    lastName, setLastName,
    email: candidateEmail,
    gender, setGender,
    countryCode, setCountryCode,
    phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber,
    phoneValidation, // ðŸ”¥ NEW
    dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth,
    city, setCity,
    country, setCountry,
    nationality, setNationality,
    doubleNationality, setDoubleNationality,
    jobTitle, setJobTitle,
    specialty, setSpecialty,
    specialtySearch, setSpecialtySearch,
    websites, setWebsites,
    additionalInfo, setAdditionalInfo,
    cvFile, setCvFile,
    passportFile, setPassportFile,
    photoFile, setPhotoFile,
    jobs,
    selectedCategory,
    filteredJobs,
    selectedJob,
    availableSpecialties,
    isLoading,
    error,
    success,
    showSuccessToast, setShowSuccessToast,
    isExistingCandidate,
    addAdditionalInfo,
    removeAdditionalInfo,
    updateAdditionalInfo,
    addWebsite,
    removeWebsite,
    updateWebsite,
    handleCategorySelection,
    handleJobSelection,
    handleFileChange,
    handleSubmit,
    calculateAge,
    validatePhoneNumber, // ðŸ”¥ NEW
  };
};
// src/hooks/useCandidates.ts
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { collection, query, onSnapshot, QueryDocumentSnapshot, DocumentData } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

export const useCandidates = (user: any) => {
  const [candidates, setCandidates] = useState<Candidate[]>([]);
  const [loadingCandidates, setLoadingCandidates] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      setLoadingCandidates(true);
      const q = query(collection(db, "candidates"));
      const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(q, (querySnapshot) => {
        const candidatesData: Candidate[] = [];
        querySnapshot.forEach((doc: QueryDocumentSnapshot<DocumentData>) => {
          const data = doc.data();
          candidatesData.push({
            id: doc.id,
            email: data.email || '',
            scoreAnalysis: data.scoreAnalysis || '',
            interviewDate: data.interviewDate || undefined,
            calendarEventId: data.calendarEventId || '',
            meetLink: data.meetLink || '',
            duration: data.duration || 30,
            ...data
          } as Candidate);
        });
        candidatesData.sort((a, b) => (b.submittedAt?.toMillis() ?? 0) - (a.submittedAt?.toMillis() ?? 0));
        setCandidates(candidatesData);
        setLoadingCandidates(false);
      }, (error) => {
        console.error("Error fetching candidates: ", error);
        setLoadingCandidates(false);
      });
      return () => unsubscribe();
    } else {
      setCandidates([]);
      setLoadingCandidates(false);
    }
  }, [user]);

  return { candidates, loadingCandidates };
};
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx';
import type { Candidate, SortDirection, SortField } from '@/types';

export const useCandidateTable = (candidates: Candidate[], filters: any) => {
    const [sortField, setSortField] = useState<SortField>('submittedAt');
    const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>('desc');
    const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);

    const handleSort = (field: SortField) => {
        if (sortField === field) {
            setSortDirection(sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc');
        } else {
            setSortField(field);
            setSortDirection('asc');
        }
    };

    const handleExport = async (format: 'csv' | 'xlsx' | 'json') => {
        setIsExporting(true);
        try {
            const params = new URLSearchParams({
                format,
                ...filters
            });

            const response = await fetch(`/api/export-candidates?${params.toString()}`);
            
            if (!response.ok) {
                const errorData = await response.json();
                throw new Error(errorData.details || 'Failed to export candidates');
            }

            if (format === 'xlsx') {
                const { data, filename, count } = await response.json();
                
                const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data);
                const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new();
                XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, 'Candidates');
                
                const metadataSheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet([{
                    'Export Date': new Date().toLocaleString(),
                    'Total Records': count,
                    'Applied Filters': Object.entries(filters)
                        .filter(([_, value]) => value && value !== 'all' && value !== '')
                        .map(([key, value]) => `${key}: ${value}`)
                        .join(', ') || 'None',
                }]);
                XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, metadataSheet, 'Export Info');
                
                XLSX.writeFile(workbook, filename);
            } else {
                const blob = await response.blob();
                const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const a = document.createElement('a');
                a.href = url;
                
                const contentDisposition = response.headers.get('content-disposition');
                let filename = `candidates_${format}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}`;
                if (contentDisposition) {
                    const filenameMatch = contentDisposition.match(/filename="?(.+)"?/);
                    if (filenameMatch && filenameMatch.length > 1) {
                        filename = filenameMatch[1];
                    }
                }
                
                a.download = filename;
                document.body.appendChild(a);
                a.click();
                a.remove();
                window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Export failed:', error);
            alert(`Export failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`);
        } finally {
            setIsExporting(false);
        }
    };

    const splitName = (fullName: string) => {
        const parts = fullName.trim().split(' ');
        const firstName = parts[0] || '';
        const lastName = parts.slice(1).join(' ') || '';
        return { firstName, lastName };
    };

    const calculateAge = (dateOfBirth: string) => {
        if (!dateOfBirth) return null;
        try {
            const birthDate = new Date(dateOfBirth);
            const today = new Date();
            if (isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) return null;
            let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
            const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();
            if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
                age--;
            }
            return age;
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    };

    const sortedCandidates = useMemo(() => {
        return [...candidates].sort((a, b) => {
            let aValue: any;
            let bValue: any;

            switch (sortField) {
                case 'firstName':
                    aValue = splitName(a.fullName).firstName.toLowerCase();
                    bValue = splitName(b.fullName).firstName.toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'lastName':
                    aValue = splitName(a.fullName).lastName.toLowerCase();
                    bValue = splitName(b.fullName).lastName.toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'email':
                    aValue = a.email.toLowerCase();
                    bValue = b.email.toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'age':
                    aValue = a.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(a.dateOfBirth) : 0;
                    bValue = b.dateOfBirth ? calculateAge(b.dateOfBirth) : 0;
                    break;
                case 'city':
                    aValue = (a.city || '').toLowerCase();
                    bValue = (b.city || '').toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'country':
                    aValue = (a.country || '').toLowerCase();
                    bValue = (b.country || '').toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'submittedAt':
                    aValue = a.submittedAt?.toMillis() || 0;
                    bValue = b.submittedAt?.toMillis() || 0;
                    break;
                case 'status':
                    aValue = a.status.toLowerCase();
                    bValue = b.status.toLowerCase();
                    break;
                case 'score':
                    aValue = a.score || 0;
                    bValue = b.score || 0;
                    break;
                default:
                    return 0;
            }

            if (aValue < bValue) return sortDirection === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
            if (aValue > bValue) return sortDirection === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
            return 0;
        });
    }, [candidates, sortField, sortDirection]);

    return {
        sortField,
        sortDirection,
        isExporting,
        handleSort,
        handleExport,
        sortedCandidates,
        splitName,
        calculateAge,
    };
};
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { collection, query, onSnapshot, QueryDocumentSnapshot, DocumentData } from "firebase/firestore";
import type { Candidate, InterviewEvent } from '@/types';
import { isBefore } from 'date-fns';
import { calculateStats } from '@/lib/dashboardUtils';

export const useDashboard = () => {
    const { user, loading: authLoading, signOut } = useAuth();
    const router = useRouter();
    
    // State
    const [candidates, setCandidates] = useState<Candidate[]>([]);
    const [loadingCandidates, setLoadingCandidates] = useState(true);
    
    // Filter states
    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<string>('main');
    const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<string>('all');
    const [filterName, setFilterName] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterCity, setFilterCity] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterCountry, setFilterCountry] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterNationality, setFilterNationality] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterJobTitle, setFilterJobTitle] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterAgeMin, setFilterAgeMin] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterAgeMax, setFilterAgeMax] = useState<string>('');
    const [filterSpecialty, setFilterSpecialty] = useState<string>('');

    // Calendar filter states
    const [calendarFilter, setCalendarFilter] = useState<'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed'>('all');
    
    // Modal states
    const [isScoringModalOpen, setIsScoringModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isProfileModalOpen, setIsProfileModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isJobManagementOpen, setIsJobManagementOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isInterviewSchedulingOpen, setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen] = useState(false);
    const [selectedCandidate, setSelectedCandidate] = useState<Candidate | null>(null);
    
    // Loading and error states
    const [statusUpdateLoading, setStatusUpdateLoading] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [statusUpdateError, setStatusUpdateError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isScoring, setIsScoring] = useState(false);
    const [isScheduling, setIsScheduling] = useState(false);
    const [scoringError, setScoringError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    
    // Toast and confirmation states
    const [toast, setToast] = useState<{
        message: string;
        type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';
        isVisible: boolean;
    }>({ message: '', type: 'info', isVisible: false });
    
    const [confirmationModal, setConfirmationModal] = useState<{
        isOpen: boolean;
        title: string;
        message: string;
        onConfirm: () => void;
    }>({ isOpen: false, title: '', message: '', onConfirm: () => {} });

    // Authentication check
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!authLoading && !user) {
            router.push('/admin-dashboard-secret/login');
        }
    }, [user, authLoading, router]);

    // Data fetching
    useEffect(() => {
        if (user) {
            setLoadingCandidates(true);
            const q = query(collection(db, "candidates"));
            const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(q, (querySnapshot) => {
                const candidatesData: Candidate[] = [];
                querySnapshot.forEach((doc: QueryDocumentSnapshot<DocumentData>) => {
                    const data = doc.data();
                    candidatesData.push({
                        id: doc.id,
                        email: data.email || '',
                        scoreAnalysis: data.scoreAnalysis || '',
                        interviewDate: data.interviewDate || undefined,
                        calendarEventId: data.calendarEventId || '',
                        meetLink: data.meetLink || '',
                        duration: data.duration || 30,
                        ...data
                    } as Candidate);
                });
                candidatesData.sort((a, b) => (b.submittedAt?.toMillis() ?? 0) - (a.submittedAt?.toMillis() ?? 0));
                setCandidates(candidatesData);
                setLoadingCandidates(false);
            }, (error) => {
                console.error("Error fetching candidates: ", error);
                setLoadingCandidates(false);
            });
            return () => unsubscribe();
        } else {
            setCandidates([]);
            setLoadingCandidates(false);
        }
    }, [user]);

    // Calendar events with completion status
    const allInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[] = useMemo(() => {
        const now = new Date();
        
        return candidates
            .filter(c => c.interviewDate)
            .map(c => {
                const interviewStart = c.interviewDate!.toDate();
                const duration = c.duration || 30;
                const interviewEnd = new Date(interviewStart.getTime() + (duration * 60 * 1000));
                const isCompleted = isBefore(interviewEnd, now);
                
                return {
                    title: isCompleted 
                        ? `âœ“ Completed: ${c.fullName}` 
                        : `Interview: ${c.fullName}`,
                    start: interviewStart,
                    end: interviewEnd,
                    resource: c,
                    isCompleted,
                    eventType: isCompleted ? 'completed' as const : 'scheduled' as const,
                };
            });
    }, [candidates]);

    // Filtered calendar events
    const filteredInterviewEvents = useMemo(() => {
        switch (calendarFilter) {
            case 'upcoming':
                return allInterviewEvents.filter(event => !event.isCompleted);
            case 'completed':
                return allInterviewEvents.filter(event => event.isCompleted);
            default:
                return allInterviewEvents;
        }
    }, [allInterviewEvents, calendarFilter]);

    // Stats calculation
const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const total = candidates.length;
    const submitted = candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'submitted').length;
    const scheduled = candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'interview_scheduled').length;
    const rejected = candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'rejected').length;
    const approved = candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'approved').length; // ðŸ”¥ ADD: Calculate approved

    return {
        total,
        submitted,
        scheduled,
        rejected,
        approved // ðŸ”¥ ADD: Include approved in return
    };
}, [candidates]);

    // Filtered candidates
    const filteredCandidates = useMemo(() => {
        return candidates.filter(candidate => {
            const statusMatch = filterStatus === 'all' || candidate.status === filterStatus;
            const nameMatch = filterName === '' || candidate.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(filterName.toLowerCase());
            const cityMatch = filterCity === '' || (candidate.city && candidate.city.toLowerCase().includes(filterCity.toLowerCase()));
            const countryMatch = filterCountry === '' || (candidate.country && candidate.country.toLowerCase().includes(filterCountry.toLowerCase()));
            const nationalityMatch = filterNationality === '' || (candidate.nationality && candidate.nationality.toLowerCase().includes(filterNationality.toLowerCase()));
            const jobTitleMatch = filterJobTitle === '' || (candidate.jobTitleText && candidate.jobTitleText.toLowerCase().includes(filterJobTitle.toLowerCase()));
            const categoryMatch = filterCategory === '' || (candidate.jobCategory && candidate.jobCategory.toLowerCase().includes(filterCategory.toLowerCase()));
            const specialtyMatch = filterSpecialty === '' || (candidate.specialty && candidate.specialty.toLowerCase().includes(filterSpecialty.toLowerCase()));
            
            let ageMatch = true;
            if (candidate.dateOfBirth && (filterAgeMin || filterAgeMax)) {
                const birthDate = new Date(candidate.dateOfBirth);
                const today = new Date();
                if (!isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) {
                    let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
                    const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();
                    if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
                        age--;
                    }
                    if (filterAgeMin && age < parseInt(filterAgeMin)) ageMatch = false;
                    if (filterAgeMax && age > parseInt(filterAgeMax)) ageMatch = false;
                }
            }

            let tabMatch = true;
            if (activeTab === 'main') {
                tabMatch = candidate.status === 'submitted' || candidate.status === 'interview_scheduled';
            } else if (activeTab === 'approved') {
                tabMatch = candidate.status === 'approved';
            } else if (activeTab === 'rejected') {
                tabMatch = candidate.status === 'rejected';
            }
            
            return statusMatch && nameMatch && cityMatch && countryMatch && nationalityMatch && jobTitleMatch && categoryMatch && ageMatch && tabMatch;
        });
    }, [candidates, filterStatus, filterName, filterCity, filterCountry, filterNationality, filterJobTitle, filterCategory, filterAgeMin, filterAgeMax, activeTab]);

    // Toast handler
    const showToast = (message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info') => {
        setToast({ message, type, isVisible: true });
        setTimeout(() => {
            setToast(prev => ({ ...prev, isVisible: false }));
        }, 5000);
    };

    // Modal handlers
    const openScoringModal = (candidate: Candidate) => {
        setSelectedCandidate(candidate);
        setIsScoringModalOpen(true);
    };

    const closeScoringModal = () => {
        setIsScoringModalOpen(false);
        setSelectedCandidate(null);
        setScoringError(null);
    };

    const openProfileModal = (candidate: Candidate) => {
        setSelectedCandidate(candidate);
        setIsProfileModalOpen(true);
    };

    const closeProfileModal = () => {
        setIsProfileModalOpen(false);
        setSelectedCandidate(null);
    };

    const openJobManagement = () => {
        setIsJobManagementOpen(true);
    };

    const closeJobManagement = () => {
        setIsJobManagementOpen(false);
    };

    const clearAllFilters = () => {
        setFilterStatus('all');
        setFilterName('');
        setFilterCity('');
        setFilterCountry('');
        setFilterNationality('');
        setFilterJobTitle('');
        setFilterCategory('');
        setFilterSpecialty('');
        setFilterAgeMin('');
        setFilterAgeMax('');
    };

    return {
        user,
        authLoading,
        signOut,
        candidates,
        loadingCandidates,
        activeTab,
        setActiveTab,
        filterStatus,
        setFilterStatus,
        filterName,
        setFilterName,
        filterCity,
        setFilterCity,
        filterCountry,
        setFilterCountry,
        filterNationality,
        setFilterNationality,
        filterJobTitle,
        setFilterJobTitle,
        filterCategory,
        setFilterCategory,
        filterAgeMin,
        setFilterAgeMin,
        filterAgeMax,
        setFilterAgeMax,
        filterSpecialty,
        setFilterSpecialty,
        calendarFilter,
        setCalendarFilter,
        isScoringModalOpen,
        setIsScoringModalOpen,
        isProfileModalOpen,
        setIsProfileModalOpen,
        isJobManagementOpen,
        setIsJobManagementOpen,
        isInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen,
        selectedCandidate,
        setSelectedCandidate,
        statusUpdateLoading,
        setStatusUpdateLoading,
        statusUpdateError,
        setStatusUpdateError,
        isScoring,
        setIsScoring,
        isScheduling,
        setIsScheduling,
        scoringError,
        setScoringError,
        toast,
        setToast,
        confirmationModal,
        setConfirmationModal,
        allInterviewEvents,
        filteredInterviewEvents,
        stats,
        filteredCandidates,
        showToast,
        openScoringModal,
        closeScoringModal,
        openProfileModal,
        closeProfileModal,
        openJobManagement,
        closeJobManagement,
        clearAllFilters,
    };
};

export const useDashboardState = () => {

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Candidate, Job } from '@/types';

export const useEditCandidateForm = (
    candidate: Candidate | null,
    isOpen: boolean,
    onSave: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>,
    onClose: () => void
) => {
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        firstName: '',
        lastName: '',
        email: '',
        gender: '',
        countryCode: '',
        phoneNumber: '',
        dateOfBirth: '',
        city: '',
        country: '',
        nationality: '',
        doubleNationality: '',
        jobCategory: '',
        jobTitle: '',
        specialty: '',
        jobTitleText: ''
    });

    const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<Job[]>([]);
    const [jobCategories, setJobCategories] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [loadingJobs, setLoadingJobs] = useState(false);
    const [selectedJob, setSelectedJob] = useState<Job | null>(null);
    const [availableSpecialties, setAvailableSpecialties] = useState<string[]>([]);
    const [filteredJobs, setFilteredJobs] = useState<Job[]>([]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            fetchJobs();
        }
    }, [isOpen]);

    const fetchJobs = async () => {
        setLoadingJobs(true);
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/jobs?activeOnly=true');
            const data = await response.json();
            if (response.ok) {
                setJobs(data.jobs || []);
                setJobCategories(data.categories || []);
            } else {
                console.error('Failed to fetch jobs:', data.error);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error fetching jobs:', error);
        } finally {
            setLoadingJobs(false);
        }
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (formData.jobCategory) {
            const filtered = jobs.filter(job => job.category === formData.jobCategory && job.isActive);
            setFilteredJobs(filtered);
        } else {
            setFilteredJobs([]);
        }
    }, [formData.jobCategory, jobs]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (selectedJob && selectedJob.specialties) {
            setAvailableSpecialties(selectedJob.specialties);
            if (!selectedJob.specialties.includes(formData.specialty)) {
                setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, specialty: '' }));
            }
        } else {
            setAvailableSpecialties([]);
        }
    }, [selectedJob]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (candidate) {
            const nameParts = candidate.fullName ? candidate.fullName.trim().split(' ') : [];
            const firstName = candidate.firstName || nameParts[0] || '';
            const lastName = candidate.lastName || nameParts.slice(1).join(' ') || '';
            let cleanPhone = candidate.phoneNumber || '';
            if (candidate.countryCode && cleanPhone.includes(candidate.countryCode)) {
                cleanPhone = cleanPhone.replace(candidate.countryCode, '').trim();
                cleanPhone = cleanPhone.replace(/^[\s\-+]+/, '');
            }
            setFormData({
                firstName,
                lastName,
                email: candidate.email || '',
                gender: candidate.gender || '',
                countryCode: candidate.countryCode || '+358',
                phoneNumber: cleanPhone,
                dateOfBirth: candidate.dateOfBirth || '',
                city: candidate.city || '',
                country: candidate.country || '',
                nationality: candidate.nationality || '',
                doubleNationality: candidate.doubleNationality || '',
                jobCategory: candidate.jobCategory || '',
                jobTitle: candidate.jobTitle || '',
                specialty: candidate.specialty || '',
                jobTitleText: candidate.jobTitleText || ''
            });
            if (candidate.jobTitle && jobs.length > 0) {
                const foundJob = jobs.find(job => job.id === candidate.jobTitle);
                if (foundJob) {
                    setSelectedJob(foundJob);
                }
            }
            setErrors({});
        }
    }, [candidate, jobs]);

    const handleInputChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
        if (errors[name]) {
            setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: '' }));
        }
    };

    const handleCategoryChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const category = e.target.value;
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            jobCategory: category,
            jobTitle: '',
            specialty: ''
        }));
        setSelectedJob(null);
        setAvailableSpecialties([]);
    };

    const handleJobChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
        const jobId = e.target.value;
        const job = jobs.find(j => j.id === jobId);
        if (job) {
            setSelectedJob(job);
            setFormData(prev => ({
                ...prev,
                jobTitle: job.id,
                jobTitleText: job.title,
                specialty: ''
            }));
        }
    };

    const validateForm = () => {
        const newErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
        if (!formData.firstName.trim()) newErrors.firstName = 'First name is required';
        if (!formData.lastName.trim()) newErrors.lastName = 'Last name is required';
        if (!formData.email.trim()) newErrors.email = 'Email is required';
        if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(formData.email)) {
            newErrors.email = 'Please enter a valid email address';
        }
        if (!formData.gender) newErrors.gender = 'Gender is required';
        if (!formData.phoneNumber.trim()) newErrors.phoneNumber = 'Phone number is required';
        if (!formData.dateOfBirth) newErrors.dateOfBirth = 'Date of birth is required';
        if (!formData.city.trim()) newErrors.city = 'City is required';
        if (!formData.country.trim()) newErrors.country = 'Country is required';
        if (!formData.nationality.trim()) newErrors.nationality = 'Nationality is required';
        if (formData.dateOfBirth) {
            const birthDate = new Date(formData.dateOfBirth);
            const today = new Date();
            const age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear() -
                (today.getMonth() < birthDate.getMonth() ||
                    (today.getMonth() === birthDate.getMonth() && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate()) ? 1 : 0);
            if (age < 18) {
                newErrors.dateOfBirth = 'Candidate must be at least 18 years old';
            }
        }
        setErrors(newErrors);
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!candidate || !validateForm()) return;
        try {
            const updatedData: Partial<Candidate> = {
                firstName: formData.firstName.trim(),
                lastName: formData.lastName.trim(),
                fullName: `${formData.firstName.trim()} ${formData.lastName.trim()}`,
                email: formData.email.toLowerCase().trim(),
                gender: formData.gender,
                countryCode: formData.countryCode,
                phoneNumber: `${formData.countryCode} ${formData.phoneNumber.trim()}`,
                dateOfBirth: formData.dateOfBirth,
                city: formData.city.trim(),
                country: formData.country.trim(),
                nationality: formData.nationality.trim(),
                doubleNationality: formData.doubleNationality.trim() || undefined,
                jobCategory: formData.jobCategory.trim(),
                jobTitle: formData.jobTitle.trim(),
                jobTitleText: formData.jobTitleText.trim(),
                specialty: formData.specialty.trim()
            };
            await onSave(candidate.id, updatedData);
            onClose();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to update candidate:', error);
        }
    };

    return {
        formData,
        errors,
        jobs,
        jobCategories,
        loadingJobs,
        selectedJob,
        availableSpecialties,
        filteredJobs,
        handleInputChange,
        handleCategoryChange,
        handleJobChange,
        handleSubmit,
    };
};
// src/hooks/useInterviewEvents.ts
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { isBefore } from 'date-fns';
import { Candidate, InterviewEvent } from '@/types';

export const useInterviewEvents = (candidates: Candidate[], calendarFilter: 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed') => {
  const allInterviewEvents: InterviewEvent[] = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    
    return candidates
      .filter(c => c.interviewDate)
      .map(c => {
        const interviewStart = c.interviewDate!.toDate();
        const duration = c.duration || 30;
        const interviewEnd = new Date(interviewStart.getTime() + (duration * 60 * 1000));
        const isCompleted = isBefore(interviewEnd, now);
        
        return {
          title: isCompleted 
            ? `âœ“ Completed: ${c.fullName}` 
            : `Interview: ${c.fullName}`,
          start: interviewStart,
          end: interviewEnd,
          resource: c,
          isCompleted,
          eventType: isCompleted ? 'completed' as const : 'scheduled' as const,
        };
      });
  }, [candidates]);

  const filteredInterviewEvents = useMemo(() => {
    switch (calendarFilter) {
      case 'upcoming':
        return allInterviewEvents.filter(event => !event.isCompleted);
      case 'completed':
        return allInterviewEvents.filter(event => event.isCompleted);
      default:
        return allInterviewEvents;
    }
  }, [allInterviewEvents, calendarFilter]);

  return { allInterviewEvents, filteredInterviewEvents };
};
"use client";

import { useState } from 'react';
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

export const useProfileModal = (
    candidate: Candidate | null,
    onEditCandidate?: (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => Promise<void>
) => {
    const [isEditModalOpen, setIsEditModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isUpdating, setIsUpdating] = useState(false);

    const handleEditCandidate = async (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => {
        if (!onEditCandidate) return;
        
        setIsUpdating(true);
        try {
            await onEditCandidate(candidateId, updatedData);
            setIsEditModalOpen(false);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to update candidate:', error);
        } finally {
            setIsUpdating(false);
        }
    };

    const calculateAge = (dateOfBirth: string) => {
        if (!dateOfBirth) return null;
        try {
            const birthDate = new Date(dateOfBirth);
            const today = new Date();
            if (isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) return null;
            let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
            const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();
            if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
                age--;
            }
            return age;
        } catch {
            return null;
        }
    };

    return {
        isEditModalOpen,
        setIsEditModalOpen,
        isUpdating,
        handleEditCandidate,
        calculateAge,
    };
};
import type { Candidate } from '@/types';

export const createApiHandlers = (
    showToast: (message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info') => void,
    closeScoringModal: () => void,
    closeProfileModal: () => void,
    setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void,
    setConfirmationModal: (modal: any) => void,
    setStatusUpdateLoading: (loading: any) => void,
    setIsScoring: (isScoring: boolean) => void,
    setScoringError: (error: string | null) => void,
    setIsScheduling: (isScheduling: boolean) => void,
    selectedCandidate: Candidate | null,
    candidates: Candidate[]
) => {
    const handleScoreSubmit = async (jobId: string, jobTitle: string, jobDescription: string) => {
        if (!selectedCandidate) return;
        setIsScoring(true);
        setScoringError(null);
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/score-candidate', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    candidateId: selectedCandidate.id,
                    jobId,
                    jobTitle,
                    jobDescription
                }),
            });
            const result = await response.json();
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to score candidate');
            }
            console.log('Scoring successful:', result);
            showToast('Candidate scored successfully!', 'success');
            closeScoringModal();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Scoring failed:', error);
            setScoringError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'An unknown error occurred');
        } finally {
            setIsScoring(false);
        }
    };

    const performStatusChange = async (candidateId: string, newStatus: string, interviewTimestampISO?: string) => {
        setStatusUpdateLoading((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, [candidateId]: true }));
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/update-status', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ candidateId, newStatus, interviewTimestampISO }),
            });
            const result = await response.json();
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to update status');
            }
            console.log('Status update successful:', result);

            if (newStatus === 'rejected') {
                if (result.emailSent) {
                    showToast('Candidate rejected and email notification sent successfully!', 'success');
                } else {
                    showToast('Candidate rejected. Email notification may have failed - check server logs.', 'warning');
                }
            } else {
                showToast(`Candidate status updated to ${newStatus.replace('_', ' ')} successfully!`, 'success');
            }

            if (selectedCandidate?.id === candidateId) {
                closeProfileModal();
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Status update failed:', error);
            const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'An unknown error occurred';
            showToast(`Failed to update status: ${errorMessage}`, 'error');
        } finally {
            setStatusUpdateLoading((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, [candidateId]: false }));
        }
    };

    const handleStatusChange = async (candidateId: string, newStatus: string, interviewTimestampISO?: string) => {
        if (newStatus === 'rejected') {
            const candidate = candidates.find(c => c.id === candidateId);
            setConfirmationModal({
                isOpen: true,
                title: 'Confirm Candidate Rejection',
                message: `Are you sure you want to reject ${candidate?.fullName || 'this candidate'}? This action cannot be undone.`,
                onConfirm: async () => {
                    setConfirmationModal((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isOpen: false }));
                    await performStatusChange(candidateId, newStatus, interviewTimestampISO);
                }
            });
            return;
        }

        await performStatusChange(candidateId, newStatus, interviewTimestampISO);
    };

    const handleScheduleInterview = async (
        candidateId: string,
        date: string,
        time: string,
        duration: number,
        adminEmail: string,
        notes?: string
    ) => {
        const candidate = candidates.find(c => c.id === candidateId);
        if (!candidate) return;

        setIsScheduling(true);

        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/schedule-interview-meet', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    candidateId: candidate.id,
                    candidateEmail: candidate.email,
                    candidateName: candidate.fullName,
                    interviewDate: date,
                    interviewTime: time,
                    duration,
                    jobTitle: candidate.jobTitleText,
                    adminEmail,
                    notes
                }),
            });

            const result = await response.json();

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to schedule interview');
            }

            showToast('Interview scheduled successfully with Google Meet!', 'success');
            setIsInterviewSchedulingOpen(false);

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Failed to schedule interview:', error);
            showToast(`Failed to schedule interview: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
        } finally {
            setIsScheduling(false);
        }
    };

    const handleCancelInterview = async (candidateId: string, reason?: string) => {
        const candidate = candidates.find(c => c.id === candidateId);
        setConfirmationModal({
            isOpen: true,
            title: 'Cancel Scheduled Interview',
            message: `Are you sure you want to cancel the interview for ${candidate?.fullName || 'this candidate'}? This will delete the calendar event and notify the candidate.`,
            onConfirm: async () => {
                setConfirmationModal((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, isOpen: false }));
                setStatusUpdateLoading((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, [candidateId]: true }));

                try {
                    const response = await fetch('/api/cancel-interview', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                        body: JSON.stringify({
                            candidateId,
                            reason: reason || 'Cancelled by admin'
                        }),
                    });

                    const result = await response.json();
                    if (!response.ok) {
                        throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to cancel interview');
                    }

                    showToast('Interview cancelled successfully and candidate has been notified.', 'success');
                    closeProfileModal();
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Cancel interview failed:', error);
                    showToast(`Failed to cancel interview: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
                } finally {
                    setStatusUpdateLoading((prev: any) => ({ ...prev, [candidateId]: false }));
                }
            }
        });
    };

    const handleEditCandidate = async (candidateId: string, updatedData: Partial<Candidate>) => {
        try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/update-candidate', {
                method: 'PUT',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ candidateId, updatedData }),
            });

            const result = await response.json();

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(result.error || 'Failed to update candidate');
            }

            console.log('âœ… Candidate updated successfully:', result);
            showToast('Candidate information updated successfully!', 'success');

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('âŒ Failed to update candidate:', error);
            const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error occurred';
            showToast(`Failed to update candidate: ${errorMessage}`, 'error');
            throw error;
        }
    };

    return {
        handleScoreSubmit,
        handleStatusChange,
        handleScheduleInterview,
        handleCancelInterview,
        handleEditCandidate,
    };
};
// src/lib/chartData.ts
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

export const getStatusChartData = (candidates: Candidate[]) => {
    const counts = candidates.reduce((acc, curr) => {
        acc[curr.status] = (acc[curr.status] || 0) + 1;
        return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, number>);

    return [
        { name: 'Submitted', value: counts.submitted || 0 },
        { name: 'Interview Scheduled', value: counts.interview_scheduled || 0 },
        { name: 'Rejected', value: counts.rejected || 0 },
        { name: 'Approved', value: counts.approved || 0 },
    ].filter(item => item.value > 0);
};

export const getScoreDistributionData = (candidates: Candidate[]) => {
    const scoredCandidates = candidates.filter(c => c.score !== null && c.score !== undefined);
    const bins = {
        '0-20': 0,
        '21-40': 0,
        '41-60': 0,
        '61-80': 0,
        '81-100': 0,
    };

    scoredCandidates.forEach(c => {
        const score = c.score!;
        if (score >= 0 && score <= 20) bins['0-20']++;
        else if (score <= 40) bins['21-40']++;
        else if (score <= 60) bins['41-60']++;
        else if (score <= 80) bins['61-80']++;
        else if (score <= 100) bins['81-100']++;
    });

    return Object.entries(bins).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, count: value }));
};
import { format } from 'date-fns';

// ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: Generate CSV with complete candidate information
export function generateCSV(candidates: any[]): string {
  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    return 'No candidates found';
  }

  const headers = [
    'Application ID',
    'Full Name',
    'First Name',
    'Last Name',
    'Email',
    'Gender',
    'Phone Number',
    'Country Code',
    'Date of Birth',
    'Age',
    'City',
    'Country',
    'Nationality',
    'Second Nationality',
    'Job Category',
    'Job ID',
    'Job Title',
    'Specialty',
    'Websites',
    'Status',
    'AI Score',
    'Score Analysis',
    'Submitted At',
    'Interview Date',
    'Meet Link',
    'Calendar Event ID',
    'Meet Space ID',
    'Duration (minutes)',
    'Last Scored At',
    'Last Updated',
    'Application Source',
    'CV URL',
    'Passport URL',
    'Photo URL'
  ];

  const csvRows = [
    headers.join(','),
    ...candidates.map(candidate => [
      escapeCSV(candidate.id),
      escapeCSV(candidate.fullName),
      escapeCSV(candidate.firstName),
      escapeCSV(candidate.lastName),
      escapeCSV(candidate.email),
      escapeCSV(candidate.gender),
      escapeCSV(candidate.phoneNumber),
      escapeCSV(candidate.countryCode),
      escapeCSV(candidate.dateOfBirth),
      candidate.age || '',
      escapeCSV(candidate.city),
      escapeCSV(candidate.country),
      escapeCSV(candidate.nationality),
      escapeCSV(candidate.doubleNationality),
      escapeCSV(candidate.jobCategory),
      escapeCSV(candidate.jobTitle),
      escapeCSV(candidate.jobTitleText),
      escapeCSV(candidate.specialty),
      escapeCSV(candidate.websites),
      escapeCSV(candidate.status),
      candidate.score || '',
      escapeCSV(candidate.scoreAnalysis),
      escapeCSV(candidate.submittedAt),
      escapeCSV(candidate.interviewDate),
      escapeCSV(candidate.meetLink),
      escapeCSV(candidate.calendarEventId),
      escapeCSV(candidate.meetSpaceId),
      candidate.duration || '',
      escapeCSV(candidate.lastScoredAt),
      escapeCSV(candidate.lastUpdated),
      escapeCSV(candidate.applicationSource),
      escapeCSV(candidate.cvUrl),
      escapeCSV(candidate.passportUrl),
      escapeCSV(candidate.photoUrl)
    ].join(','))
  ];

  return csvRows.join('\n');
}

function escapeCSV(value: string): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
    return '';
  }
  const stringValue = String(value);
  if (stringValue.includes(',') || stringValue.includes('"') || stringValue.includes('\n')) {
    return `"${stringValue.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  }
  return stringValue;
}

        '0-20': 0,
        '21-40': 0,

// ðŸ”¥ ADD: New calculateStats function
export const calculateStats = (candidates: Candidate[]): Stats => {
    return {
        total: candidates.length,
        submitted: candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'submitted').length,
        scheduled: candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'interview_scheduled').length,
        rejected: candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'rejected').length,
        approved: candidates.filter(c => c.status === 'approved').length,
    };
};
export const calculateAge = (dateOfBirth: string): number | null => {
    if (!dateOfBirth) return null;

    const birthDate = new Date(dateOfBirth);
    const today = new Date();

    if (isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) {
        return null; // Invalid date
    }

    let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
    const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();

    if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
        age--;
    }

    return age;
};
// src/lib/firebase-admin.ts
import admin from 'firebase-admin';
import { getApps } from 'firebase-admin/app';

// Function to initialize Firebase Admin SDK lazily
const initializeFirebaseAdmin = () => {
  if (!getApps().length) { // Check if already initialized
    try {
      const serviceAccount = {
        projectId: process.env.FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
        clientEmail: process.env.FIREBASE_CLIENT_EMAIL,
        privateKey: process.env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY?.replace(/\\n/g, '\n'),
      };

      if (!serviceAccount.projectId || !serviceAccount.clientEmail || !serviceAccount.privateKey) {
        throw new Error("Firebase Admin SDK environment variables are not fully set.");
      }

      admin.initializeApp({
        credential: admin.credential.cert(serviceAccount),
      });
      console.log("Firebase Admin SDK initialized successfully.");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Firebase Admin SDK initialization error:', error);
      // Throw the error to prevent API routes from proceeding without proper admin access
      throw new Error("Failed to initialize Firebase Admin SDK."); 
    }
  }
  return admin;
};

// Export functions to get specific admin services, ensuring initialization
const getFirestoreAdmin = () => {
  const adminInstance = initializeFirebaseAdmin();
  return adminInstance.firestore();
};

const getAuthAdmin = () => {
  const adminInstance = initializeFirebaseAdmin();
  return adminInstance.auth();
};

const getStorageAdmin = () => {
  const adminInstance = initializeFirebaseAdmin();
  return adminInstance.storage();
};

// Export the functions, not the direct instances
export { getFirestoreAdmin, getAuthAdmin, getStorageAdmin, initializeFirebaseAdmin };

import { getStorage } from 'firebase/storage';
