import { phonePatterns, getCountryName } from './phoneValidationData';

interface PhoneValidationResponse {
  isValid: boolean;
  phone?: string;
  format?: {
    international: string;
    local: string;
  };
  country?: {
    code: string;
    name: string;
    prefix: string;
  };
  location?: string;
  type?: string;
  carrier?: string;
  error?: string;
  fallbackUsed?: 'basic' | 'regex' | 'api' | 'failed';
  validationLevel?: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
}

// Rate limiting storage (production: use Redis)
const validationAttempts = new Map<string, { 
  count: number; 
  lastAttempt: number; 
  failedNumbers: Set<string> 
}>();

const API_DAILY_LIMIT = 80;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_IP = 3;

// ðŸ”¥ STEP 1: Basic validation (most lenient)
export function basicPhoneValidation(fullPhone: string): PhoneValidationResponse {
  const basicPattern = /^\+[1-9]\d{6,14}$/;
  const isValid = basicPattern.test(fullPhone);

  return {
    isValid,
    phone: fullPhone,
    fallbackUsed: 'basic',
    validationLevel: 'low',
    error: isValid ? undefined : 'Phone number must start with + and contain 7-15 digits'
  };
}

// ðŸ”¥ STEP 2: Advanced regex validation with comprehensive country patterns
export function validatePhoneWithRegex(fullPhone: string): PhoneValidationResponse {
  // Find matching country code (prioritize longer codes first)
  const sortedCodes = Object.keys(phonePatterns).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
  const countryCode = sortedCodes.find(code => fullPhone.startsWith(code));

  if (!countryCode) {
    return {
      isValid: false,
      error: 'Unsupported country code',
      fallbackUsed: 'regex',
      validationLevel: 'medium'
    };
  }

  const pattern = phonePatterns[countryCode];
  const isValid = pattern.test(fullPhone);

  return {
    isValid,
    phone: fullPhone,
    format: {
      international: fullPhone,
      local: fullPhone.replace(countryCode, '').trim()
    },
    country: {
      code: countryCode.replace('+', ''),
      name: getCountryName(countryCode),
      prefix: countryCode
    },
    fallbackUsed: 'regex',
    validationLevel: 'medium',
    error: isValid ? undefined : `Invalid phone number format for ${getCountryName(countryCode)}`
  };
}

// HTTP utility function
async function httpGetAsync(url: string): Promise<string> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 10000);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'User-Agent': 'InternationalSkills-PhoneValidator/1.0'
      },
      signal: controller.signal
    });

    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
    }

    return await response.text();
  } catch (error) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    throw error;
  }
}

// ðŸ”¥ STEP 3: Abstract API validation (only if regex passes)
export async function validatePhoneWithAbstractAPI(fullPhone: string): Promise<PhoneValidationResponse> {
  try {
    const apiKey = process.env.ABSTRACT_PHONE_API_KEY;
    if (!apiKey) {
      throw new Error('Abstract Phone API key not configured');
    }

    const phoneForAPI = fullPhone.startsWith('+') ? fullPhone.substring(1) : fullPhone;
    const url = `https://phonevalidation.abstractapi.com/v1/?api_key=${apiKey}&phone=${encodeURIComponent(phoneForAPI)}`;
    
    console.log(`ðŸ“ž [PRODUCTION] Validating phone via Abstract API: ${phoneForAPI}`);
    
    const responseText = await httpGetAsync(url);
    const data = JSON.parse(responseText);
    
    return {
      isValid: data.valid === true,
      phone: data.phone,
      format: data.format ? {
        international: data.format.international,
        local: data.format.local
      } : undefined,
      country: data.country ? {
        code: data.country.code,
        name: data.country.name,
        prefix: data.country.prefix
      } : undefined,
      location: data.location,
      type: data.type,
      carrier: data.carrier,
      fallbackUsed: 'api',
      validationLevel: 'high'
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Abstract Phone API validation failed:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

// Rate limiting with psychological protection
export function checkRateLimit(ip: string, phoneNumber: string): { 
  allowed: boolean; 
  attemptsLeft: number; 
  isFailedNumber: boolean;
  reason?: string;
} {
  const today = new Date().toDateString();
  const key = `${ip}-${today}`;
  
  const current = validationAttempts.get(key) || { 
    count: 0, 
    lastAttempt: 0, 
    failedNumbers: new Set<string>() 
  };
  
  // Check if this number has already failed validation (psychological protection)
  if (current.failedNumbers.has(phoneNumber)) {
    return { 
      allowed: false, 
      attemptsLeft: MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_IP - current.count,
      isFailedNumber: true,
      reason: 'This phone number has already failed validation today'
    };
  }
  
  if (current.count >= MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_IP) {
    return { 
      allowed: false, 
      attemptsLeft: 0,
      isFailedNumber: false,
      reason: 'Daily API limit reached for your IP address'
    };
  }
  
  return { 
    allowed: true, 
    attemptsLeft: MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_IP - current.count,
    isFailedNumber: false
  };
}

// Update rate limit and track failed numbers
export function updateRateLimit(ip: string, phoneNumber?: string, success: boolean = true) {
  const today = new Date().toDateString();
  const key = `${ip}-${today}`;
  
  const current = validationAttempts.get(key) || { 
    count: 0, 
    lastAttempt: 0, 
    failedNumbers: new Set<string>() 
  };
  
  const updated = {
    count: success ? current.count + 1 : current.count,
    lastAttempt: Date.now(),
    failedNumbers: current.failedNumbers
  };
  
  // Track failed numbers for psychological protection
  if (!success && phoneNumber) {
    updated.failedNumbers.add(phoneNumber);
  }
  
  validationAttempts.set(key, updated);
}

// ðŸ”¥ CLEANUP: Automatic cleanup for in-memory storage
setInterval(() => {
  const oneDayAgo = Date.now() - (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  for (const [key, data] of validationAttempts.entries()) {
    if (data.lastAttempt < oneDayAgo) {
      validationAttempts.delete(key);
      console.log(`ðŸ§¹ Cleaned up old rate limit data for: ${key}`);
    }
  }
}, 60 * 60 * 1000); // Clean every hour
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';
import admin from 'firebase-admin';
import { FieldValue } from 'firebase-admin/firestore';
import { sendNotificationEmail, sendRejectionEmail } from './email';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

// ðŸ”¥ NEW: Helper function to calculate age
export const calculateAge = (dateOfBirth: string): number | null => {
  if (!dateOfBirth) return null;
  
  try {
    const birthDate = new Date(dateOfBirth);
    const today = new Date();
    
    // Check if date is valid
    if (isNaN(birthDate.getTime())) return null;
    
    let age = today.getFullYear() - birthDate.getFullYear();
    const monthDiff = today.getMonth() - birthDate.getMonth();
    
    // If birth month hasn't occurred this year, or it's the birth month but the day hasn't occurred
    if (monthDiff < 0 || (monthDiff === 0 && today.getDate() < birthDate.getDate())) {
      age--;
    }
    
    return age;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

export const updateCandidateStatus = async (
    candidateId: string, 
    newStatus: string, 
    interviewTimestampISO?: string,
    calendarEventId?: string,
    meetLink?: string,
    duration?: number
): Promise<{ status: string; emailSent?: boolean }> => {
    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    const validStatuses = ['submitted', 'rejected', 'interview_scheduled', 'approved'];
    if (!validStatuses.includes(newStatus)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid status provided');
    }

    const candidateRef = db.collection("candidates").doc(candidateId);
    const candidateSnap = await candidateRef.get();

    if (!candidateSnap.exists) {
        throw new Error('Candidate not found');
    }

    const candidateData = candidateSnap.data();
    if (!candidateData) {
        throw new Error('Candidate data not found');
    }

    const updateData: { 
        status: string; 
        interviewDate?: admin.firestore.Timestamp | FieldValue;
        calendarEventId?: string | FieldValue;
        meetLink?: string | FieldValue;
        duration?: number | FieldValue;
    } = {
        status: newStatus,
    };

    let interviewDate: Date | undefined = undefined;
    if (newStatus === 'interview_scheduled') {
        if (!interviewTimestampISO) {
            throw new Error('Interview date and time are required for scheduling.');
        }
        try {
            interviewDate = new Date(interviewTimestampISO);
            if (isNaN(interviewDate.getTime())) {
                throw new Error('Invalid date format');
            }
            updateData.interviewDate = admin.firestore.Timestamp.fromDate(interviewDate);
            
            if (calendarEventId) updateData.calendarEventId = calendarEventId;
            if (meetLink) updateData.meetLink = meetLink;
            if (duration) updateData.duration = duration;
            
        } catch (dateError) {
            console.error("Invalid interview date format received:", interviewTimestampISO, dateError);
            throw new Error('Invalid interview date format provided. Please use ISO format.');
        }
    } else {
        if (candidateData.status === 'interview_scheduled') {
            updateData.interviewDate = admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete();
            updateData.calendarEventId = admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete();
            updateData.meetLink = admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete();
            updateData.duration = admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete();
        }
    }

    // ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: Update database first
    await candidateRef.update(updateData);

    let emailSent = false;

    // ðŸ”¥ UPDATED: Handle email sending with proper error handling
    if (candidateData.email) {
        try {
            if (newStatus === 'rejected') {
                console.log(`ðŸ“§ Sending rejection email to ${candidateData.email}...`);
                await sendRejectionEmail(
                    candidateData.email, 
                    candidateData.fullName || 'Candidate', 
                    candidateData.jobTitleText || ''
                );
                console.log(`âœ… Rejection email sent successfully to ${candidateData.email}`);
                emailSent = true;
            } else if (newStatus === 'interview_scheduled' && interviewDate) {
                console.log(`ðŸ“§ Sending interview notification to ${candidateData.email}...`);
                await sendNotificationEmail(
                    candidateData.email, 
                    newStatus, 
                    candidateData.fullName || 'Candidate', 
                    interviewDate
                );
                console.log(`âœ… Interview notification sent successfully to ${candidateData.email}`);
                emailSent = true;
            }
        } catch (emailError) {
            console.error(`âŒ Failed to send email to ${candidateData.email}:`, emailError);
            // Don't throw error - status update was successful, just email failed
            emailSent = false;
        }
    }

    return { status: newStatus, emailSent };
};

// Rest of the exportCandidates function remains the same...
export const exportCandidates = async (
    filterStatus: string,
    filterName: string,
    filterNationality: string,
    filterCity: string = '',
    filterCountry: string = '',
    filterJobTitle: string = '',
    filterCategory: string = '',
    filterSpecialty: string = '',
    filterAgeMin: string = '',
    filterAgeMax: string = ''
) => {
    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    let query: any = db.collection("candidates");

    if (filterStatus !== 'all') {
      query = query.where('status', '==', filterStatus);
    }

    const snapshot = await query.orderBy('submittedAt', 'desc').get();
    
    let candidates = snapshot.docs.map((doc: any) => {
      const data = doc.data();
      const age = calculateAge(data.dateOfBirth);
      
      return {
        id: doc.id,
        fullName: data.fullName || '',
        firstName: data.firstName || '',
        lastName: data.lastName || '',
        email: data.email || '',
        gender: data.gender || '',
        phoneNumber: data.phoneNumber || '',
        countryCode: data.countryCode || '',
        dateOfBirth: data.dateOfBirth || '',
        age: age || '',
        city: data.city || '',
        country: data.country || '',
        nationality: data.nationality || '',
        doubleNationality: data.doubleNationality || '',
        jobTitle: data.jobTitle || '',
        jobTitleText: data.jobTitleText || '',
        jobCategory: data.jobCategory || '',
        specialty: data.specialty || '',
        websites: Array.isArray(data.websites) ? data.websites.join(', ') : '',
        status: data.status || '',
        score: data.score || null,
        scoreAnalysis: data.scoreAnalysis || '',
        submittedAt: data.submittedAt ? format(data.submittedAt.toDate(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') : '',
        interviewDate: data.interviewDate ? format(data.interviewDate.toDate(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') : '',
        meetLink: data.meetLink || '',
        calendarEventId: data.calendarEventId || '',
        meetSpaceId: data.meetSpaceId || '',
        duration: data.duration || '',
        cvUrl: data.cvUrl || '',
        passportUrl: data.passportUrl || '',
        photoUrl: data.photoUrl || '',
        applicationSource: data.applicationSource || 'web_form',
        lastUpdated: data.lastUpdated ? format(data.lastUpdated.toDate(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') : '',
        lastScoredAt: data.lastScoredAt ? format(data.lastScoredAt.toDate(), 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') : ''
      };
    });

    // Apply all filters...
    if (filterName) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(filterName.toLowerCase()) ||
        candidate.firstName.toLowerCase().includes(filterName.toLowerCase()) ||
        candidate.lastName.toLowerCase().includes(filterName.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterNationality) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.nationality.toLowerCase().includes(filterNationality.toLowerCase()) ||
        candidate.doubleNationality.toLowerCase().includes(filterNationality.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterCity) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.city.toLowerCase().includes(filterCity.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterCountry) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.country.toLowerCase().includes(filterCountry.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterJobTitle) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.jobTitleText.toLowerCase().includes(filterJobTitle.toLowerCase()) ||
        candidate.jobTitle.toLowerCase().includes(filterJobTitle.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterCategory) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.jobCategory.toLowerCase().includes(filterCategory.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterSpecialty) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => 
        candidate.specialty.toLowerCase().includes(filterSpecialty.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    // Age filtering
    if (filterAgeMin || filterAgeMax) {
      candidates = candidates.filter((candidate: any) => {
        const candidateAge = candidate.age;
        if (!candidateAge && candidateAge !== 0) return false;
        
        const age = typeof candidateAge === 'string' ? parseInt(candidateAge) : candidateAge;
        if (isNaN(age)) return false;
        
        if (filterAgeMin && age < parseInt(filterAgeMin)) return false;
        if (filterAgeMax && age > parseInt(filterAgeMax)) return false;
        
        return true;
      });
    }

    return candidates;
};
import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

const SMTP_HOST = process.env.SMTP_HOST;
const SMTP_PORT = process.env.SMTP_PORT ? parseInt(process.env.SMTP_PORT, 10) : 587;
const SMTP_USER = process.env.SMTP_USER;
const SMTP_PASS = process.env.SMTP_PASS;
const SMTP_FROM = process.env.SMTP_FROM_EMAIL;

const smtpConfigured = SMTP_HOST && SMTP_USER && SMTP_PASS && SMTP_FROM;

if (!smtpConfigured) {
    console.warn("SMTP environment variables are not fully set. Email notifications will be disabled.");
}

const transporter = smtpConfigured ? nodemailer.createTransport({
    host: SMTP_HOST,
    port: SMTP_PORT,
    secure: SMTP_PORT === 465,
    auth: {
        user: SMTP_USER,
        pass: SMTP_PASS,
    },
    connectionTimeout: 15000,
    greetingTimeout: 10000,
    socketTimeout: 20000,
    pool: true,
    maxConnections: 3,
    maxMessages: 50,
    tls: {
        rejectUnauthorized: false,
        minVersion: 'TLSv1.2'
    },
    debug: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development',
    logger: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'
}) : null;

if (transporter && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development') {
    transporter.verify((error, success) => {
        if (error) {
            console.error('âŒ SMTP connection failed:', error);
        } else {
            console.log('âœ… SMTP server is ready to take our messages');
        }
    });
}

const getTemplate = (templateName: string, type: 'html' | 'txt') => {
    const filePath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'src', 'lib', 'emailTemplates', `${templateName}.${type}`);
    return readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
};

export const sendNotificationEmail = async (to: string, status: string, candidateName: string, interviewDate?: Date) => {
    if (!transporter) {
        console.log("SMTP not configured, skipping email notification.");
        return;
    }

    let subject = '';
    let htmlBody = '';
    let textBody = '';
    const companyName = "International Skills Labor Company";

    if (status === 'rejected') {
        subject = `Application Status Update - ${companyName}`;
        htmlBody = getTemplate('statusRejected', 'html').replace(/\${companyName}/g, companyName).replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName);
        textBody = getTemplate('statusRejected', 'txt').replace(/\${companyName}/g, companyName).replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName);
    } else if (status === 'interview_scheduled' && interviewDate) {
        subject = `Interview Invitation from ${companyName}`;
        const formattedDate = interviewDate.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'long', year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
        const formattedTime = interviewDate.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', timeZoneName: 'short' });
        htmlBody = getTemplate('statusInterviewScheduled', 'html')
            .replace(/\${companyName}/g, companyName)
            .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)
            .replace(/\${formattedDate}/g, formattedDate)
            .replace(/\${formattedTime}/g, formattedTime);
        textBody = getTemplate('statusInterviewScheduled', 'txt')
            .replace(/\${companyName}/g, companyName)
            .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)
            .replace(/\${formattedDate}/g, formattedDate)
            .replace(/\${formattedTime}/g, formattedTime);
    } else {
        console.log(`No email template for status: ${status}`);
        return;
    }

    const mailOptions = {
        from: {
            name: 'International Skills Labor Company',
            address: SMTP_FROM || 'noreply@internationalskills.fi'
        },
        to: to,
        subject: subject,
        html: htmlBody,
        text: textBody,
        replyTo: 'Apply@internationalskills.fi'
    };

    try {
        console.log(`ðŸ“§ Sending ${status} email to ${to}...`);
        const info = await transporter.sendMail(mailOptions);
        console.log('âœ… Email sent successfully:', info.messageId);
        return info;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('âŒ Error sending email:', error);
        throw error;
    }
};

export const sendApplicationConfirmationEmail = async ({
    candidateEmail,
    candidateName,
    jobTitle,
    jobCategory,
    specialty,
    applicationId,
    submissionDate
}: {
    candidateEmail: string;
    candidateName: string;
    jobTitle: string;
    jobCategory?: string;
    specialty?: string;
    applicationId: string;
    submissionDate: Date;
}) => {
    if (!transporter) {
        console.warn("SMTP not configured, skipping confirmation email.");
        return;
    }

    const formattedDate = submissionDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
        weekday: 'long',
        year: 'numeric',
        month: 'long',
        day: 'numeric',
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
        timeZone: 'Europe/Helsinki'
    });

    let htmlTemplate = getTemplate('applicationConfirmation', 'html')
        .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)
        .replace(/\${jobTitle}/g, jobTitle)
        .replace(/\${applicationId}/g, applicationId)
        .replace(/\${formattedDate}/g, formattedDate);

    let textTemplate = getTemplate('applicationConfirmation', 'txt')
        .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)
        .replace(/\${jobTitle}/g, jobTitle)
        .replace(/\${applicationId}/g, applicationId)
        .replace(/\${formattedDate}/g, formattedDate);

    let jobCategoryHtml = '';
    let jobCategoryText = '';
    if (jobCategory) {
        jobCategoryHtml = `
                        <div class="detail-row">
                            <span class="detail-label">Job Category:</span>
                            <span class="detail-value">${jobCategory}</span>
                        </div>`;
        jobCategoryText = `- Job Category: ${jobCategory}`;
    }
    htmlTemplate = htmlTemplate.replace('<!-- JOB_CATEGORY_PLACEHOLDER -->', jobCategoryHtml);
    textTemplate = textTemplate.replace('<!-- JOB_CATEGORY_PLACEHOLDER -->', jobCategoryText);

    let specialtyHtml = '';
    let specialtyText = '';
    if (specialty) {
        specialtyHtml = `
                        <div class="detail-row">
                            <span class="detail-label">Specialty:</span>
                            <span class="detail-value">${specialty}</span>
                        </div>`;
        specialtyText = `- Specialty: ${specialty}`;
    }
    htmlTemplate = htmlTemplate.replace('<!-- SPECIALTY_PLACEHOLDER -->', specialtyHtml);
    textTemplate = textTemplate.replace('<!-- SPECIALTY_PLACEHOLDER -->', specialtyText);

    const mailOptions = {
        from: {
            name: 'International Skills Labor Company',
            address: SMTP_FROM || 'noreply@internationalskills.fi'
        },
        to: candidateEmail,
        subject: `âœ… Application Confirmed - ${jobTitle} Position | Application #${applicationId}`,
        text: textTemplate,
        html: htmlTemplate,
        replyTo: 'Apply@internationalskills.fi'
    };

    try {
        console.log(`ðŸ“§ Sending confirmation email to ${candidateEmail}...`);
        const info = await transporter.sendMail(mailOptions);
        console.log('âœ… Confirmation email sent successfully:', info.messageId);
        return info;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('âŒ Error sending confirmation email:', error);
        throw error;
    }
};

  if (!candidateEmail || !candidateName) {

    .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)

    .replace(/\${candidateName}/g, candidateName)
    .replace(/\${jobTitle ? `the ${jobTitle} position` : 'opportunities'}/g, jobTitle ? `the ${jobTitle} position` : 'opportunities');

    from: {
      name: 'International Skills Labor Company',

    subject: `Application Status Update - International Skills Labor Company`,
    text: textTemplate,
    html: htmlTemplate,
    replyTo: 'Apply@internationalskills.fi'

  try {
    console.log(`ðŸ“§ Sending rejection email to ${candidateEmail}...`);

    console.log('âœ… Rejection email sent successfully:', info.messageId);
    return info;

    console.error('âŒ Error sending rejection email:', error);
    throw error;
  }
};

interface EmailVerificationResponse {
  isValid: boolean;
  deliverable?: boolean;
  disposable?: boolean;
  role_account?: boolean;
  error?: string;
}

async function verifyEmailWithAbstractAPI(email: string): Promise<EmailVerificationResponse> {
  try {
    const apiKey = process.env.ABSTRACT_API_KEY;
    if (!apiKey) {
      throw new Error('Abstract API key not configured');
    }

    const url = `https://emailvalidation.abstractapi.com/v1/?api_key=${apiKey}&email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`;
    
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Abstract API error: ${response.status} - ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    
    return {
      isValid: data.deliverability === 'DELIVERABLE' && !data.is_disposable_email?.value,
      deliverable: data.deliverability === 'DELIVERABLE',
      disposable: data.is_disposable_email?.value || false,
      role_account: data.is_role_email?.value || false,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Abstract API verification failed:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

async function verifyEmailWithHunter(email: string): Promise<EmailVerificationResponse> {
  try {
    const apiKey = process.env.HUNTER_API_KEY;
    if (!apiKey) {
      throw new Error('Hunter API key not configured');
    }

    const response = await fetch(
      `https://api.hunter.io/v2/email-verifier?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}&api_key=${apiKey}`,
      {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Hunter API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    
    return {
      isValid: data.data.result === 'deliverable',
      deliverable: data.data.result === 'deliverable',
      disposable: data.data.disposable,
      role_account: data.data.role,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Hunter verification failed:', error);
    throw error;
  }
}

// TypeScript version of the async HTTP function
async function httpGetAsync(url: string): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    fetch(url)
      .then(response => {
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(`HTTP error! status: ${response.status}`);
        }
        return response.text();
      })
      .then(data => resolve(data))
      .catch(error => reject(error));
  });
}

// Basic email format validation as fallback
function basicEmailValidation(email: string): boolean {
  const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
  return emailRegex.test(email);
}

export const verifyEmail = async (email: string): Promise<EmailVerificationResponse> => {
    // Basic format validation first
    if (!basicEmailValidation(email)) {
        return {
            isValid: false,
            error: 'Invalid email format'
        };
    }

    // Try production email verification services
    let verificationResult: EmailVerificationResponse;

    try {
        // Try Abstract API first (recommended)
        if (process.env.ABSTRACT_API_KEY) {
            verificationResult = await verifyEmailWithAbstractAPI(email);
        }
        // Fallback to Hunter.io
        else if (process.env.HUNTER_API_KEY) {
            verificationResult = await verifyEmailWithHunter(email);
        }
        // If no API keys are configured, use basic validation
        else {
            console.warn('No email verification API configured. Using basic validation only.');
            verificationResult = {
                isValid: true, // Allow through for basic validation
            };
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Email verification service failed:', error);
        
        // In production, you might want to be more restrictive
        // For now, we'll allow the email through if the service fails
        verificationResult = {
            isValid: true,
            error: 'Verification service temporarily unavailable'
        };
    }
    return verificationResult;
};

// src/services/interview.ts
import { google } from 'googleapis';
import { db } from '@/lib/firebase';
import { doc, updateDoc, getDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { getGoogleAuth } from './google';
import { sendCancellationEmail } from './email';

const generateMeetLink = () => {
  const chars = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
  const part1 = Array.from({length: 3}, () => chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)]).join('');
  const part2 = Array.from({length: 4}, () => chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)]).join('');
  const part3 = Array.from({length: 3}, () => chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)]).join('');
  
  return `https://meet.google.com/${part1}-${part2}-${part3}`;
};

export const scheduleInterview = async (
  candidateId: string,
  candidateEmail: string,
  candidateName: string,
  interviewDate: string,
  interviewTime: string,
  duration = 30,
  jobTitle: string,
  adminEmail: string,
  notes: string,
  origin: string
) => {
  if (!candidateEmail || !candidateName || !interviewDate || !interviewTime) {
    throw new Error('Missing required fields');
  }

  const interviewDateTime = new Date(`${interviewDate}T${interviewTime}:00`);

  if (isNaN(interviewDateTime.getTime())) {
    throw new Error('Invalid date or time format');
  }

  const endDateTime = new Date(interviewDateTime.getTime() + (duration * 60 * 1000));

  let meetLink = '';
  let calendarEventId = '';

  try {
    const auth = getGoogleAuth();
    const calendar = google.calendar({ version: 'v3', auth });

    const eventDetails = {
      summary: `Interview: ${jobTitle || 'Position'} - ${candidateName}`,
      description: `Interview scheduled with ${candidateName} for ${jobTitle || 'the position'} at International Skills Labor Company.\n\nCandidate Email: ${candidateEmail}\nDuration: ${duration} minutes${notes ? `\n\nNotes: ${notes}` : ''}`,
      start: {
        dateTime: interviewDateTime.toISOString(),
        timeZone: 'Europe/Helsinki'
      },
      end: {
        dateTime: endDateTime.toISOString(),
        timeZone: 'Europe/Helsinki'
      },
      attendees: [
        { 
          email: candidateEmail, 
          displayName: candidateName,
          responseStatus: 'needsAction'
        },
        { 
          email: adminEmail || 'wassim@internationalskills.fi', 
          displayName: 'Interviewer',
          responseStatus: 'accepted'
        }
      ],
      conferenceData: {
        createRequest: {
          requestId: `interview-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)}`,
          conferenceSolutionKey: {
            type: 'hangoutsMeet'
          }
        }
      },
      reminders: {
        useDefault: false,
        overrides: [
          { method: 'email', minutes: 24 * 60 },
          { method: 'email', minutes: 60 },
          { method: 'popup', minutes: 30 }
        ]
      },
      guestsCanModify: false,
      guestsCanInviteOthers: false,
      guestsCanSeeOtherGuests: true,
      sendUpdates: 'all'
    };

    const response = await calendar.events.insert({
      calendarId: 'primary',
      conferenceDataVersion: 1,
      sendUpdates: 'all',
      requestBody: eventDetails
    });

    if (response.data) {
      calendarEventId = response.data.id || '';
      meetLink = response.data.conferenceData?.entryPoints?.[0]?.uri || 
                 response.data.hangoutLink || 
                 generateMeetLink();
    }

  } catch (googleError) {
    console.error('Google Calendar API error:', googleError);
    meetLink = generateMeetLink();
  }

  try {
    const updateResponse = await fetch(`${origin}/api/update-status`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ 
        candidateId, 
        newStatus: 'interview_scheduled', 
        interviewTimestampISO: interviewDateTime.toISOString()
      }),
    });

    if (!updateResponse.ok) {
      const errorData = await updateResponse.json();
      console.error('Failed to update candidate status:', errorData);
      throw new Error(errorData.error || 'Failed to update status');
    }
  } catch (updateError) {
    console.error('Database update error:', updateError);
  }

  return {
    interviewDateTime: interviewDateTime.toISOString(),
    endDateTime: endDateTime.toISOString(),
    duration: duration,
    meetLink: meetLink,
    calendarEventId: calendarEventId,
    emailsSent: {
      candidate: candidateEmail,
      interviewer: adminEmail || 'wassim@internationalskills.fi'
    }
  };
};

export const cancelInterview = async (candidateId: string, reason: string) => {
  if (!candidateId) {
    throw new Error('Candidate ID is required');
  }

  const candidateRef = doc(db, 'candidates', candidateId);
  const candidateDoc = await getDoc(candidateRef);
  
  if (!candidateDoc.exists()) {
    throw new Error('Candidate not found');
  }

  const candidateData = candidateDoc.data();
  
  if (candidateData.calendarEventId) {
    try {
      const auth = getGoogleAuth();
      const calendar = google.calendar({ version: 'v3', auth });
      
      await calendar.events.delete({
        calendarId: 'primary',
        eventId: candidateData.calendarEventId,
        sendUpdates: 'all'
      });
      
      console.log('Google Calendar event deleted successfully:', candidateData.calendarEventId);
    } catch (googleError) {
      console.error('Failed to delete Google Calendar event:', googleError);
    }
  }

  await updateDoc(candidateRef, {
    status: 'submitted',
    interviewDate: null,
    calendarEventId: null,
    meetLink: null,
    duration: null
  });

  if (candidateData.email) {
    try {
      await sendCancellationEmail({
        candidateEmail: candidateData.email,
        candidateName: candidateData.fullName,
        jobTitle: candidateData.jobTitleText,
        reason
      });
    } catch (emailError) {
      console.error('Failed to send cancellation email:', emailError);
    }
  }
};
// src/services/jobs.ts
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';
import { JOB_CATEGORIES } from '@/lib/job-categories';
import { Job } from '@/types';
import admin from 'firebase-admin';

const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
const jobsCollection = db.collection("jobs");

export const getJobs = async (activeOnly = false): Promise<{ jobs: Job[], categories: typeof JOB_CATEGORIES }> => {
  let query = jobsCollection.orderBy("category").orderBy("title");

  if (activeOnly) {
    query = query.where("isActive", "==", true);
  }

  const jobsSnapshot = await query.get();

  const jobs = jobsSnapshot.docs.map(doc => {
    const data = doc.data();
    return {
      id: doc.id,
      ...data,
      specialties: data.specialties || []
    } as Job;
  });

  return { jobs, categories: JOB_CATEGORIES };
};

export const createJob = async (jobData: Omit<Job, 'id'>): Promise<{ jobId: string }> => {
  const { title, description, category, specialties } = jobData;

  if (!title || !description || !category || !specialties || !Array.isArray(specialties) || specialties.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Missing title, description, category, or specialties');
  }

  if (!JOB_CATEGORIES.includes(category)) {
    throw new Error('Invalid category');
  }

  const validSpecialties = specialties.filter(spec => spec && spec.trim() !== '');
  if (validSpecialties.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('At least one specialty is required');
  }

  const jobRef = await jobsCollection.add({
    ...jobData,
    specialties: validSpecialties,
    createdAt: admin.firestore.Timestamp.now(),
    isActive: true
  });

  return { jobId: jobRef.id };
};

export const updateJob = async (jobId: string, jobData: Partial<Job>): Promise<void> => {
  if (!jobId) {
    throw new Error('Missing jobId');
  }

  if (jobData.category && !JOB_CATEGORIES.includes(jobData.category)) {
    throw new Error('Invalid category');
  }

  const updateData: any = {
    ...jobData,
    updatedAt: admin.firestore.Timestamp.now()
  };

  if (jobData.specialties !== undefined) {
    if (!Array.isArray(jobData.specialties)) {
      throw new Error('Specialties must be an array');
    }
    const validSpecialties = jobData.specialties.filter(spec => spec && spec.trim() !== '');
    if (validSpecialties.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('At least one specialty is required');
    }
    updateData.specialties = validSpecialties;
  }

  await jobsCollection.doc(jobId).update(updateData);
};

export const deleteJob = async (jobId: string): Promise<void> => {
  if (!jobId) {
    throw new Error('Missing jobId');
  }

  await jobsCollection.doc(jobId).delete();
};
import { getFirestoreAdmin } from '@/lib/firebase-admin';
import admin from 'firebase-admin';
import { DocumentData } from "firebase-admin/firestore";
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { GoogleAIFileManager } from "@google/generative-ai/server";
import { Candidate } from '@/types';

const GEMINI_API_KEY = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY;

if (!GEMINI_API_KEY) {
  console.warn("GEMINI_API_KEY environment variable not set. AI scoring will be disabled.");
}

const genAI = GEMINI_API_KEY ? new GoogleGenerativeAI(GEMINI_API_KEY) : null;
const fileManager = GEMINI_API_KEY ? new GoogleAIFileManager(GEMINI_API_KEY) : null;

interface CandidateDataForScoring {
    fullName: string;
    cvUrl: string;
    email: string;
    city?: string;
    country?: string;
    phoneNumber?: string;
    websites?: string[];
    additionalInfo?: {
        type: 'link' | 'file' | 'text';
        content: string;
        fileUrl?: string;
    }[];
}

interface GeminiScoringResponse {
    score: number;
    analysis: string;
    keyStrengths: string[];
    areasForImprovement: string[];
    recommendation: 'highly_recommended' | 'recommended' | 'conditional' | 'not_recommended';
}

// ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Function to upload file to Gemini using the correct GoogleAIFileManager
async function uploadFileToGemini(fileUrl: string, mimeType: string) {
    if (!fileManager) {
        console.error("File Manager not initialized");
        return null;
    }

    try {
        console.log(`ðŸ“¤ Uploading file to Gemini: ${fileUrl}`);
        
        // Fetch the file from the URL
        const response = await fetch(fileUrl);
        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Failed to fetch file: ${response.statusText}`);
        }
        
        const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
        const buffer = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);
        
        // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Use the GoogleAIFileManager for file uploads
        const uploadResult = await fileManager.uploadFile(buffer, {
            mimeType: mimeType,
            displayName: `candidate-cv-${Date.now()}`
        });
        
        console.log(`âœ… File uploaded successfully: ${uploadResult.file.uri}`);
        
        // Wait for file to be processed if needed
        let file = uploadResult.file;
        while (file.state === 'PROCESSING') {
            console.log("â³ File processing...");
            await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
            file = await fileManager.getFile(file.name);
        }
        
        if (file.state === 'FAILED') {
            throw new Error('File processing failed');
        }
        
        console.log(`ðŸŽ‰ File ready for analysis: ${file.uri}`);
        return file;
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("âŒ Error uploading file to Gemini:", error);
        return null;
    }
}

// Function to get MIME type from URL
function getMimeTypeFromUrl(url: string): string {
    const urlWithoutQuery = url.split('?')[0];
    const extension = urlWithoutQuery.toLowerCase().split('.').pop();
    
    switch (extension) {
        case 'pdf':
            return 'application/pdf';
        case 'doc':
            return 'application/msword';
        case 'docx':
            return 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document';
        case 'jpg':
        case 'jpeg':
            return 'image/jpeg';
        case 'png':
            return 'image/png';
        case 'gif':
            return 'image/gif';
        case 'webp':
            return 'image/webp';
        case 'txt':
            return 'text/plain';
        default:
            return 'application/octet-stream';
    }
}

// Enhanced document content extraction with actual file upload
async function extractDocumentContent(fileUrl: string): Promise<{ content: string; geminiFile?: any }> {
    try {
        console.log(`ðŸ“„ Processing document: ${fileUrl}`);
        
        const mimeType = getMimeTypeFromUrl(fileUrl);
        console.log(`ðŸ“‹ Detected MIME type: ${mimeType}`);
        
        // Upload file to Gemini for direct analysis
        const geminiFile = await uploadFileToGemini(fileUrl, mimeType);
        
        if (geminiFile) {
            return {
                content: `ðŸ“„ Document uploaded to Gemini for analysis: ${fileUrl}`,
                geminiFile: geminiFile
            };
        } else {
            // Fallback: provide file information for context
            return {
                content: `ðŸ“„ Document available (processing failed): ${fileUrl} - This document contains candidate information that should be considered in evaluation.`
            };
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error processing document:", error);
        return {
            content: `ðŸ“„ Document processing failed: ${fileUrl}`
        };
    }
}

// Enhanced website analysis
async function analyzeWebsiteContent(url: string): Promise<string> {
    try {
        const cleanUrl = url.startsWith('http') ? url : `https://${url}`;
        console.log(`ðŸ”— Analyzing website: ${cleanUrl}`);
        
        // Return instruction for comprehensive analysis
        return `ðŸŒ Please analyze this website: ${cleanUrl} - Extract relevant professional information, portfolio details, work experience, skills, and any other information that would help evaluate this candidate's suitability for the position.`;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error preparing website analysis:", error);
        return `ðŸ”— Website provided: ${url} (analysis preparation failed)`;
    }
}

// ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Enhanced scoring analysis using the correct API structure
async function getGemini25ScoringAnalysis(
    jobTitle: string, 
    jobDescription: string, 
    candidateData: CandidateDataForScoring
): Promise<GeminiScoringResponse | null> {
    if (!genAI) {
        console.error("Gemini API not initialized. Skipping scoring.");
        return null;
    }

    try {
        console.log(`ðŸ¤– Initializing Gemini 2.5 Pro for candidate: ${candidateData.fullName}`);

        // Process CV file first
        const cvMimeType = getMimeTypeFromUrl(candidateData.cvUrl);
        const cvFile = await uploadFileToGemini(candidateData.cvUrl, cvMimeType);
        
        // Collect all files and content for analysis
        const additionalContent: string[] = [];
        const geminiFiles: any[] = [];
        
        // Add CV file if successfully uploaded
        if (cvFile) {
            geminiFiles.push(cvFile);
            console.log(`ðŸ“Ž CV file added for analysis: ${cvFile.uri}`);
        }

        // Process websites
        if (candidateData.websites && candidateData.websites.length > 0) {
            for (const website of candidateData.websites) {
                const websiteAnalysis = await analyzeWebsiteContent(website);
                additionalContent.push(websiteAnalysis);
            }
        }

        // Process additional information
        if (candidateData.additionalInfo) {
            for (const info of candidateData.additionalInfo) {
                if (info.type === 'link') {
                    const linkAnalysis = await analyzeWebsiteContent(info.content);
                    additionalContent.push(linkAnalysis);
                } else if (info.type === 'file' && info.fileUrl) {
                    const fileResult = await extractDocumentContent(info.fileUrl);
                    additionalContent.push(fileResult.content);
                    if (fileResult.geminiFile) {
                        geminiFiles.push(fileResult.geminiFile);
                    }
                } else if (info.type === 'text') {
                    additionalContent.push(`ðŸ“ Additional Information: ${info.content}`);
                }
            }
        }

        // Get model
        const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({
            model: "gemini-2.5-pro"
        });

        // Construct comprehensive analysis prompt
        const prompt = `
You are an expert HR recruiter and talent acquisition specialist. Analyze this candidate thoroughly for the specified position using ALL available information including uploaded files.

ðŸŽ¯ **POSITION DETAILS:**
Job Title: ${jobTitle}

Job Description:
--- START JOB DESCRIPTION ---
${jobDescription}
--- END JOB DESCRIPTION ---

ðŸ‘¤ **CANDIDATE PROFILE:**
Full Name: ${candidateData.fullName}
Email: ${candidateData.email}
Location: ${candidateData.city ? `${candidateData.city}, ${candidateData.country || ''}` : candidateData.country || 'Not specified'}
Phone: ${candidateData.phoneNumber || 'Not provided'}

ðŸ“„ **CV/RESUME:**
${cvFile ? 'The candidate\'s CV/resume has been uploaded and is available for analysis. Please thoroughly analyze the document content including work experience, education, skills, achievements, and any other relevant information.' : `CV Document URL: ${candidateData.cvUrl} - Please consider this contains the candidate's professional history.`}

${additionalContent.length > 0 ? `
ðŸ” **ADDITIONAL INFORMATION TO ANALYZE:**
${additionalContent.join('\n\n')}
` : ''}

ðŸ“Š **ANALYSIS REQUIREMENTS:**
1. Analyze the CV/resume content thoroughly for technical skills, experience, and qualifications
2. Provide a numerical score (0-100) based on overall job fit
3. Give detailed analysis covering technical skills, experience, education, and growth potential
4. Identify 3 key strengths that make them suitable for this role
5. Identify 2 areas where they might need development or support
6. Provide a clear recommendation category

Score the candidate based on:
- Job Requirements Match (40%)
- Experience Quality & Relevance (30%)
- Skills & Technical Competency (20%)
- Growth Potential & Cultural Fit (10%)

Provide your response in the following JSON format:
{
  "score": <number between 0-100>,
  "analysis": "<detailed 4-6 sentence analysis>",
  "keyStrengths": ["<strength1>", "<strength2>", "<strength3>"],
  "areasForImprovement": ["<area1>", "<area2>"],
  "recommendation": "<highly_recommended|recommended|conditional|not_recommended>"
}

Please provide a thorough assessment based on ALL available information including uploaded documents.
        `;

        console.log(`ðŸ” Sending comprehensive analysis request to Gemini 2.5 Pro...`);
        console.log(`ðŸ“Š Analysis includes: ${geminiFiles.length} uploaded files, ${additionalContent.length} additional info items`);

        // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Create content parts using the correct API structure
        const parts: any[] = [];
        
        // Add uploaded files first using the correct format
        geminiFiles.forEach(file => {
            parts.push({
                fileData: {
                    mimeType: file.mimeType,
                    fileUri: file.uri
                }
            });
        });
        
        // Add the prompt text
        parts.push({
            text: prompt
        });

        // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Use the correct API structure for content generation
        const result = await model.generateContent({
            contents: [{
                role: "user",
                parts: parts
            }],
            generationConfig: {
                responseMimeType: "application/json",
                temperature: 0.7,
                topK: 40,
                topP: 0.95,
                maxOutputTokens: 2048,
            }
        });

        // Handle potentially undefined responseText
        const response = result.response;
        const responseText = response.text();
        if (!responseText) {
            throw new Error("No response text received from Gemini API");
        }
        
        console.log("ðŸ¤– Gemini 2.5 Pro Pro Raw Response:", responseText.substring(0, 200) + "...");

        // ðŸ”¥ FIXED: Cleanup using the correct FileManager API
        for (const file of geminiFiles) {
            try {
                await fileManager!.deleteFile(file.name);
                console.log(`ðŸ—‘ï¸ Cleaned up file: ${file.name}`);
            } catch (cleanupError) {
                console.warn(`âš ï¸ Failed to cleanup file ${file.name}:`, cleanupError);
            }
        }

        try {
            // Clean and parse JSON response
            let cleanedText = responseText.trim();
            cleanedText = cleanedText.replace(/```json\n?|```/g, "");
            
            const jsonStart = cleanedText.indexOf('{');
            const jsonEnd = cleanedText.lastIndexOf('}');
            
            if (jsonStart === -1 || jsonEnd === -1) {
                throw new Error("No valid JSON object found in response");
            }
            
            cleanedText = cleanedText.substring(jsonStart, jsonEnd + 1);
            const parsedResponse: GeminiScoringResponse = JSON.parse(cleanedText);

            // Validate response structure
            if (
                typeof parsedResponse.score === 'number' &&
                parsedResponse.score >= 0 && parsedResponse.score <= 100 &&
                typeof parsedResponse.analysis === 'string' &&
                parsedResponse.analysis.length > 0 &&
                Array.isArray(parsedResponse.keyStrengths) &&
                Array.isArray(parsedResponse.areasForImprovement) &&
                ['highly_recommended', 'recommended', 'conditional', 'not_recommended'].includes(parsedResponse.recommendation)
            ) {
                console.log(`âœ… Gemini 2.5 Pro Analysis Complete - Score: ${parsedResponse.score}, Recommendation: ${parsedResponse.recommendation}`);
                return parsedResponse;
            } else {
                console.error("âŒ Parsed response doesn't match expected format:", parsedResponse);
                return null;
            }
        } catch (parseError) {
            console.error("âŒ JSON parsing failed:", parseError);
            console.log("Raw response text:", responseText);
            
            // Fallback scoring
            const scoreMatch = responseText.match(/['"]*score['"]*\s*:\s*(\d+)/i);
            if (scoreMatch && scoreMatch[1]) {
                const score = parseInt(scoreMatch[1], 10);
                if (!isNaN(score) && score >= 0 && score <= 100) {
                    console.warn("âš ï¸ Using fallback scoring due to JSON parse error");
                    return {
                        score,
                        analysis: "Analysis completed with comprehensive file and content review, but detailed response parsing failed. The score reflects thorough evaluation of all available information including uploaded documents.",
                        keyStrengths: ["Comprehensive file analysis completed", "Document content reviewed", "Professional evaluation conducted"],
                        areasForImprovement: ["Complete analysis parsing needed", "Response format optimization required"],
                        recommendation: score >= 80 ? 'recommended' : score >= 60 ? 'conditional' : 'not_recommended'
                    };
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("âŒ Error calling Gemini 2.5 Pro API:", error);
        return null;
    }
}

export const scoreCandidate = async (
    candidateId: string, 
    jobId: string, 
    jobTitle: string, 
    jobDescription: string
): Promise<GeminiScoringResponse> => {
    if (!GEMINI_API_KEY || !genAI) {
        throw new Error('AI Scoring is not configured on the server.');
    }

    console.log(`ðŸŽ¯ Starting comprehensive Gemini 2.5 Pro scoring for candidate: ${candidateId}`);

    const db = getFirestoreAdmin();
    const candidateRef = db.collection("candidates").doc(candidateId);
    const candidateSnap = await candidateRef.get();

    if (!candidateSnap.exists) {
        throw new Error('Candidate not found');
    }

    const candidateDocData = candidateSnap.data() as DocumentData;
    
    const candidateDataForScoring: CandidateDataForScoring = {
        fullName: candidateDocData.fullName || '',
        cvUrl: candidateDocData.cvUrl || '',
        email: candidateDocData.email || '',
        city: candidateDocData.city,
        country: candidateDocData.country,
        phoneNumber: candidateDocData.phoneNumber,
        websites: candidateDocData.websites || [],
        additionalInfo: candidateDocData.additionalInfo || []
    };

    console.log(`ðŸ“Š Candidate data prepared for comprehensive analysis:`, {
        name: candidateDataForScoring.fullName,
        hasCV: !!candidateDataForScoring.cvUrl,
        websiteCount: candidateDataForScoring.websites?.length || 0,
        additionalInfoCount: candidateDataForScoring.additionalInfo?.length || 0
    });

    const scoringResult = await getGemini25ScoringAnalysis(jobTitle, jobDescription, candidateDataForScoring);

    if (scoringResult === null) {
        throw new Error('Failed to get AI score and analysis from Gemini 2.5 Pro');
    }

    // Update candidate document with enhanced scoring data
    const updateData = {
        score: scoringResult.score,
        scoreAnalysis: scoringResult.analysis,
        keyStrengths: scoringResult.keyStrengths,
        areasForImprovement: scoringResult.areasForImprovement,
        aiRecommendation: scoringResult.recommendation,
        scoredForJobId: jobId || null,
        scoredForJobTitle: jobTitle,
        lastScoredAt: admin.firestore.Timestamp.now(),
        scoringVersion: 'gemini-2.5-pro-enhanced'
    };

    await candidateRef.update(updateData);

    console.log(`âœ… Candidate ${candidateId} scored successfully with comprehensive Gemini 2.5 Pro analysis`);
    return scoringResult;
};
